Motivation: 现有跨语言主题模型依赖稀疏的双语资源，生成的语义结构不连贯或弱对齐；LLM精炼方法虽提升可解释性但成本高、仅适用于文档级别且易产生幻觉，白盒方法需不可获取的token概率。

Method: 提出了LLM-XTM框架，集成LLM引导的主题精炼与自一致性不确定性量化，以黑盒方式实现跨语言主题模型的稳定与可扩展增强。

Result: 在多语言语料库上的实验表明，LLM-XTM实现了更优的主题连贯性与对齐度，同时减少了对双语词典和昂贵LLM调用的依赖。

Conclusion: LLM-XTM通过黑盒LLM精炼与自一致性量化，有效地平衡了跨语言主题建模的质量与计算成本。

Abstract: Cross-lingual topic modeling aims to discover shared semantic structures across languages, yet existing models depend on sparse bilingual resources and often yield incoherent or weakly aligned topics. Recent LLM-based refinements improve interpretability but are costly, document-level, and prone to hallucination, with prior white-box approaches requiring inaccessible token probabilities. We propose LLM-XTM, a framework that integrates LLM-guided topic refinement with self-consistency uncertainty quantification, enabling black-box, stable, and scalable enhancement of cross-lingual topic models. Experiments on multilingual corpora show that LLM-XTM achieves superior topic coherence and alignment while reducing reliance on bilingual dictionaries and expensive LLM calls.

</details>


### [70] [When to Think, When to Speak: Learning Disclosure Policies for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03314)
*Jiaqi Wei,Xuehang Guo,Pengfei Yu,Xiang Zhang,Wanli Ouyang,Siqi Sun,Qingyun Wang,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Side-by-Side (SxS) 交错推理方法，将单流自回归接口中的披露时机作为可控决策，通过将部分披露与持续私有推理交错进行，仅在推理充分支持时才释放内容。采用蕴含对齐的交错轨迹构建、SFT 和 RL 训练，在 Qwen3-30B-A3B 和 Qwen3-4B 模型上，于 AIME25 和 GPQA-Diamond 基准测试中改善了准确率-内容延迟的帕累托权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单流自回归界面中，同一token同时更新模型状态并构成不可逆的公开承诺，导致"静默税"：额外思考延迟首个任务相关内容，而提前流式输出则可能产生过早承诺，偏差后续生成。需要解耦推理与披露时机。

Method: 提出 SxS 交错推理方法：(1) 将部分披露与持续私有推理在同一上下文中交错进行；(2) 仅当推理充分支持时才释放内容；(3) 通过匹配答案前缀与支持性推理前缀构建蕴含对齐的交错轨迹；(4) 使用 SFT 学习双动作语义，再通过 RL 恢复推理性能。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B-A3B（MoE）和 Qwen3-4B（Dense）两种架构/规模上，以及在域内（AIME25）和域外（GPQA-Diamond）基准测试中，SxS 改善了准确率-内容延迟的帕累托权衡。

Conclusion: 将披露时机作为可控决策可以有效缓解单流自回归接口中的"静默税"问题，在保持推理质量的同时优化延迟-准确率权衡。

Abstract: In single-stream autoregressive interfaces, the same tokens both update the model state and constitute an irreversible public commitment. This coupling creates a \emph{silence tax}: additional deliberation postpones the first \emph{task-relevant} content, while naive early streaming risks premature commitments that bias subsequent generations. We introduce \textbf{\emph{Side-by-Side (SxS)}} Interleaved Reasoning, which makes \emph{disclosure timing} a controllable decision within standard autoregressive generation. SxS interleaves partial disclosures with continued private reasoning in the same context, but releases content only when it is \emph{supported} by the reasoning so far. To learn such pacing without incentivizing filler, we construct entailment-aligned interleaved trajectories by matching answer prefixes to supporting reasoning prefixes, then train with SFT to acquire the dual-action semantics and RL to recover reasoning performance under the new format. Across two Qwen3 architectures/scales (MoE \textbf{Qwen3-30B-A3B}, dense \textbf{Qwen3-4B}) and both in-domain (AIME25) and out-of-domain (GPQA-Diamond) benchmarks, SxS improves accuracy--\emph{content-latency} Pareto trade-offs under token-level proxies (e.g., inter-update waiting).

</details>


### [71] [From prompting to evidence-based translation: A RAG+prompt system for Japanese-Chinese translation and its pedagogical potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03387)
*Wenshi Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了一种RAG+Prompt翻译系统，通过集成语言分析（NMCC内/外区分和风险预测）、嵌入检索和增强提示，在不修改基础模型的情况下提升日汉名词修饰节构句的翻译质量。基于GPT-4o和66句测试集，知识库从0扩展至2000时，宏观平均句子级BLEU从24.28提升至29.96（+23.4%），表明该方法有效且可解释、可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在高资源语言对上表现良好，但对于包含名词修饰节构（NMCC）的日汉翻译可靠性不足，亟需一种可解释且无需修改基础模型的提升方法。

Method: 构建RAG+Prompt翻译系统，包含语言分析模块（输出NMCC内/外区分A1和风险预测A2）、嵌入检索（L2距离，top-k=5）和增强提示构建。使用GPT-4o作为基础模型，在66句测试集上评估6种知识库规模（0/100/200/500/1,000/2,000），以宏观平均句子级BLEU（1-4-gram带长度惩罚，区分大小写，中文按字符级）为唯一指标。

Result: 平均BLEU从知识库为0（RAG禁用）的24.28提升至2000的29.96，增长5.68点（+23.4%）。提升趋势随知识库规模增大而持续。

Conclusion: RAG+Prompt翻译系统能够以可解释和可审计的方式改善包含NMCC的日汉翻译质量。局限性包括仅使用单一基础模型、单一评价指标，以及依赖已发表文本和商业API；未来工作将拓展语料体裁、语言对和评价指标。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on high-resource pairs but are less reliable for Japanese-Chinese sentences containing noun-modifying clause constructions (NMCCs). This study evaluates a retrieval-augmented generation RAG+Prompt translation system that integrates linguistic analysis, embedding-based retrieval, prompt construction, and LLM generation without modifying the base model. The analysis module outputs A1 (inner vs. outer NMCC) and A2 (risk predictions: lexical choice/NMCC handling/word order/style/register); top-k = 5 similar Ja-Zh examples (L2 distance) and A1/A2 are inserted into an enhanced prompt. Using GPT-4o and a 66-sentence test set, we compare six knowledge-base sizes (0/100/200/500/1,000/2,000). Macro-averaged sentence-level BLEU (1-4-gram with brevity penalty; cased; Chinese at the character level) is the sole metric. Mean BLEU increases from 24.28 at 0 (RAG disabled) to 29.96 at 2,000 (+5.68; +23.4%). The upward trend holds across sizes, with larger knowledge bases yielding higher scores. We conclude that the RAG+Prompt translation system improves Ja-Zh translation of sentences containing NMCCs in an interpretable and auditable manner. Limitations include one base model, one metric, and reliance on published texts and commercial APIs; future work will broaden genres, language pairs, and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [72] [Geolocating News about Extreme Climate Events: A Comparative Analysis of Off-the-Shelf Tools for Toponym Identification in German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03414)
*Brielen Madureira,Mariana Madruga de Brito,Andreas Niekler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了Flair、Spacy和Stanza三种NER工具在德语新闻文章中识别极端气候事件地理位置的表现，发现不同工具的输出差异会传播到下游任务，影响对文档地理焦点的判断，进而影响关于国家在德国媒体中显著性的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本中确定极端气候事件和灾害的地理位置是气候影响与适应研究中的常见问题，现有NER工具用于识别候选地名的效果尚缺乏系统性比较。

Method: 对三种现成NER工具（Flair、Spacy和Stanza）进行对比分析，描述并量化它们在德语新闻文章输出中的差异，并基于三种方法进行外部评估以确定事件发生的国家。

Result: 不同NER工具的输出存在显著差异，这些差异会传播到下游任务，导致关于文档地理焦点的不同决策，进而影响对各国在德国媒体中显著性的结论。

Conclusion: NER工具的选择会影响极端气候事件地理定位的准确性，进而影响气候影响和适应研究的结论可靠性。

Abstract: Determining the geolocation of extreme climate events and disasters in texts is a common problem in climate impact and adaptation research. Named-entity recognition (NER) tools are typically used to identify a pool of toponyms that serve as candidate event locations. In this study, we conduct a comparative analysis of three off-the-shelf NER tools, namely Flair, Spacy and Stanza. We describe and quantify differences between their outputs for German news articles and evaluate them extrinsically based on three methods to determine the country where events took place. We show how their contrasts are propagated into downstream tasks and can yield distinct decisions about a document's geographical focus, which, in turn, can impact conclusions about countries' prominence in German media.

</details>


### [73] [Benchmarking Logistic Regression, SVM, Naive Bayes, and IndoBERT Fine-Tuning for Sentiment Analysis on Indonesian Product Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03439)
*Nabila Zakiyah Zahra,Salwa Farhanatussaidah,Nasywa Nur Afifah,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在印尼电商评论数据集上对比了传统机器学习（TF-IDF + 逻辑回归、线性SVM、多项式朴素贝叶斯）与Transformer深度学习模型（IndoBERT），发现线性SVM在准确率（97.60%）和Macro F1（0.5510）上均优于IndoBERT（88.70%，0.5088），差异主要源于数据采样不一致（基线使用全量数据，Transformer使用采样子集），最后将模型部署为Gradio Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印尼电商平台快速增长产生了大量用户评论，准确分析这些评论的情感对于规模化衡量客户满意度和识别产品问题至关重要。

Method: 采用TF-IDF特征提取结合逻辑回归、线性SVM、多项式朴素贝叶斯作为基线；微调IndoBERT模型进行上下文序列分类；对基线使用平衡类权重、对IndoBERT设计自定义加权交叉熵损失函数以处理类别不平衡问题。

Result: 线性SVC取得准确率97.60%、Macro F1 0.5510；IndoBERT取得准确率88.70%、Macro F1 0.5088。性能差距主要源于基线使用全量数据而Transformer使用采样子集。

Conclusion: 传统机器学习模型在特定实验设置下优于Transformer模型，且通过Gradio Web应用验证了情感分类管线的实际可用性。

Abstract: The exponential growth of e-commerce platforms in Indonesia has generated a massive volume of user-generated product reviews. Analyzing the sentiment of these reviews is critical for measuring customer satisfaction and identifying product issues at scale. This paper benchmarks traditional Machine Learning (ML) approaches against a Transformer-based Deep Learning model for a three-class sentiment analysis task (positive, neutral, negative) on the Tokopedia Product Reviews 2025 dataset. We implemented Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency (TF-IDF) feature extraction coupled with three algorithms: Logistic Regression, Linear Support Vector Machine (SVM), and Multinomial Naive Bayes as robust baselines. Subsequently, we fine-tuned the IndoBERT model (indobenchmark/indobert-base-p1) for contextual sequence classification. To computationally address the severe class imbalance inherent in e-commerce feedback, we applied balanced class weights for the baseline models and engineered a custom weighted cross-entropy loss function within the IndoBERT training loop, following the broader motivation of imbalanced-learning research. Our comprehensive evaluation using Accuracy, Macro F1-score, and Weighted F1-score revealed that the traditional Linear SVC model significantly outperformed the IndoBERT model in our experimental setup, achieving an Accuracy of 97.60% and a Macro F1-score of 0.5510, compared to IndoBERT's 88.70% and 0.5088. Detailed analysis indicates that this performance gap was primarily driven by discrepancies in the data sampling regimes, where baselines utilized the full corpus while the Transformer was constrained to a sampled subset. Finally, we demonstrate the practical viability of our pipeline by deploying the final sentiment classification model as an interactive Gradio web application.

</details>


### [74] [An ERP Study of Recursive Possessive Parsing in ASD Children and Its Cognitive Neuro Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03447)
*Fu Chenxi,Wang Xiaoyi,Zhuang Ziman,Yang Caimei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该ERP研究对比了12名ASD儿童和12名TD儿童处理汉语两级递归所有格结构的过程，发现ASD儿童在早期知觉加工（P200）和句法重分析（P600）上存在显著缺陷，而词汇语义加工（N400）相对保留，支持语言模块化分离假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归结构是人类语言的核心属性，但ASD儿童如何处理复杂递归的神经机制尚不明确。

Method: 采用句图匹配范式，通过ERP记录ASD和TD儿童处理两级递归所有格结构时的P200、N400和P600成分。

Result: ASD儿童P200波幅显著降低且无典型后部语法效应；两组均无稳健N400违例效应；ASD儿童P600波幅显著降低、无后部语法效应且潜伏期有延迟趋势。

Conclusion: ASD儿童的词汇语义加工相对保留，但在线句法递归计算严重受损，支持自闭症语言加工模块化分离的观点。

Abstract: Recursive structures are a core property of human language, yet little is known about how children with autism spectrum disorder (ASD) process complex recursion. This ERP study investigated the online processing of two-level recursive possessive structures in Mandarin-speaking children with ASD (n = 12) compared to typically developing (TD) peers (n = 12) using a sentence-picture matching paradigm. ERPs were analyzed for P200 (150-250 ms), N400 (300-500 ms), and P600 (500-1000 ms). Results showed that ASD children exhibited significantly reduced P200 amplitudes and failed to show the typical posterior grammaticality effect, indicating atypical early perceptual processing. No robust N400 violation effect was observed in either group, confirming the mismatch was not a semantic anomaly; however, ASD children showed a reversed anterior effect and an attenuated posterior effect. For the P600, ASD children had significantly reduced amplitudes, no posterior grammaticality effect, and a trend toward delayed latency, reflecting a core deficit in syntactic reanalysis. These findings demonstrate that while lexical-semantic processing is relatively preserved in ASD, the online syntactic computation required for recursion is severely impaired, supporting modular dissociation accounts of language in autism.

</details>


### [75] [Retrieving Floods without Floodlights: Topic Models as Binary Classifiers for Extreme Climate Events in German News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03450)
*Brielen Madureira,Mariana Madruga de Brito,Andreas Niekler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用主题模型作为二元分类器，从德国媒体中检索七类极端气候事件相关新闻，无需修改训练过程即可提升检索精度，并对比了微调文本嵌入分类器和开源大语言模型的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模新闻数据库中用于训练深度学习分类器的标注数据往往不足，而主题模型虽具备无监督和可解释性优势，却通常仅用于探索性分析。研究者希望探索主题模型作为分类器的潜力，以改进极端气候事件相关新闻的检索效果。

Method: 利用主题模型的后验分布选择相关文档，不修改训练过程；通过关键词概率辅助选择相关主题并提升样本精度；与微调文本嵌入分类器和开源LLM进行对比评估。

Result: 主题模型的关键词概率可用于选择相关主题并提升样本精度；LLM的精确率最低；结果具有灾害类型依赖性，表明不宜将气候事件视为单一类别。

Conclusion: 主题模型可有效作为二元分类器用于极端气候事件新闻检索，但需注意不同灾害类型的结果差异，建议按灾害类型分别建模而非统一处理。

Abstract: In studies of media coverage of extreme climate events, NLP methods have become indispensable for identifying relevant texts in large news databases. Still, enough annotated data to train accurate deep learning-based classifiers from scratch is often not available. Topic Models have the advantage of being both unsupervised and interpretable, but are typically used only for exploratory analysis or data characterisation. In this study, we investigate how to employ Topic Models as binary classifiers for refining the retrieval of relevant news about seven types of extreme climate events in the German media. Our method relies on the posterior distributions estimated by Topic Models to select relevant documents, without modifying their training procedure. Using an annotated sample to guide the evaluation, we show that the probabilities assigned to keywords used to query news databases can also be informative for selecting relevant topics and improve sample precision. We compare our results to a fine-tuned text embedding classifier and an open-weight LLM, discussing observed trade-offs, e.g. the LLM's lowest precision. Moreover, we show that results are hazard-dependent, which speaks against considering climate events as a single category in NLP tasks.

</details>


### [76] [Detecting Stealth Sycophancy in Mental-Health Dialogue with Dynamic Emotional Signature Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03472)
*Tianze Han,Beining Xu,Hanbo Zhang,Yongming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需大型语言模型作为最终评判者的多领域心理支持对话评估方法——动态情感签名图（DESG），通过解耦临床状态并采用非对称临床几何评分，在包含3000个对话窗口的基准测试中以DESG-Ensemble达到0.9353 macro-F1，显著优于直接LLM评判和对称文本相似度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于直接LLM评判和对称文本相似度的方法与心理治疗质量不对齐，因为治疗响应质量取决于临床方向（是否推动用户状态向调节/重构发展、保持不变或恶化），而非简单的文本相似性或表面有害性判断。

Method: 提出Dynamic Emotional Signature Graphs（DESG），一种模型无关的评估器：将对话窗口表示为解耦的临床状态，并使用非对称临床几何进行评分；构建包含EmpatheticDialogues、ESConv和CRADLE-Dialogue三个领域的3000窗口诊断性压力测试基准。

Result: 在600窗口的保留测试集上，DESG-Ensemble达到0.9353 macro-F1，超过ConcatANN 1.51个百分点、BERTScore 19.63个百分点、TRACT 33.81个百分点。

Conclusion: 临床状态流形是主要判别基础，基于图的轨迹组件提供非对称评分和可解释诊断而非性能的唯一来源；消融实验、人工审核和定性分歧案例验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: As conversational AI therapists are increasingly used in psychological support settings, reliable offline evaluation of therapeutic response quality remains an open problem. This paper studies multi-domain support-dialogue evaluation without relying on large language models as final judges. We use a direct LLM judge as a baseline that reads raw dialogue text and predicts whether the target response is harmful, productive, or neutral. We find that direct LLM judges and symmetric text-similarity metrics are poorly aligned with therapeutic quality because the target label depends on clinical direction: whether the response moves the user state toward regulation or reframing, leaves it broadly unchanged, or reinforces deterioration through higher risk affect or cognitive-distortion mass. To address this issue, we propose Dynamic Emotional Signature Graphs (DESG), a model-agnostic evaluator that represents dialogue windows with decoupled clinical states and scores them using asymmetric clinical geometry. We evaluate DESG on a constructed diagnostic stress-test benchmark of 3{,}000 dialogue windows from EmpatheticDialogues, ESConv, and CRADLE-Dialogue, covering peer support, counseling dialogue, and crisis-oriented interaction. On the 600-window held-out test aggregate, DESG-Ensemble achieves 0.9353 macro-F1, exceeding ConcatANN by 1.51 percentage points, BERTScore by 19.63 points, and TRACT by 33.81 points. Feature ablations, artifact controls, a 100-window blinded adjudicator audit, and qualitative disagreement cases indicate that the clinical state manifold is the main discriminative substrate, while graph-based trajectory components provide asymmetric scoring and interpretable diagnostics rather than serving as the sole source of performance.

</details>


### [77] [CuraView: A Multi-Agent Framework for Medical Hallucination Detection with GraphRAG-Enhanced Knowledge Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03476)
*Severin Ye,Xiao Kong,Xiaopeng He,Guangsu Yan,Dongsuk Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CuraView，一个基于多智能体框架的出院小结忠实性幻觉检测与证据溯源系统。通过构建GraphRAG知识图谱和闭环生成-检测流水线，实现句子级幻觉检测与四级证据分级（E1-E4），在Discharge-Me基准测试中，针对安全关键的E4指标F1达0.831，较基线方法提升50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出院小结需要从冗长的电子健康记录中提取关键信息，手动提取劳动密集；大语言模型虽能提升生成效率，但易产生忠实性幻觉（与源记录矛盾的陈述），直接威胁患者安全。

Method: 提出CuraView多智能体框架，包含：(1) 基于GraphRAG构建患者级知识图谱；(2) 闭环生成-检测流水线；(3) 句子级证据检索与四级证据分级（E1强支持至E4直接矛盾）；(4) 结构化可解释证据链生成。使用Qwen3-14B作为检测模型，在Discharge-Me基准的250名患者子集上微调评估。

Result: 在50名测试患者上，微调Qwen3-14B在安全关键E4指标上F1达0.831（召回率90.9%，精确率76.5%），E3+E4上F1为0.823，较基线模型相对提升50%，优于RAGTruth-style和QAGS-style基线。

Conclusion: 基于证据链的图检索验证能显著提升临床文档的事实可靠性，同时产生可复用的标注数据集用于下游模型训练与蒸馏。

Abstract: Discharge summaries require extracting critical information from lengthy electronic health records (EHRs), a process that is labor-intensive when performed manually. Large language models (LLMs) can improve generation efficiency; however, they are prone to producing faithfulness hallucinations, statements that contradict source records, posing direct risks to patient safety. To address this, we present CuraView, a multi-agent framework for sentence-level detection and evidence-grounded explanation of faithfulness hallucinations in discharge summaries. CuraView constructs a GraphRAG-based knowledge graph from patient-level EHRs and implements a closed-loop generation-detection pipeline with sentence-level evidence retrieval and classification spanning four evidence grades from strong support to direct contradiction (E1-E4), yielding structured and interpretable evidence chains.
  We evaluate CuraView on a subset of 250 patients from the Discharge-Me benchmark, with 50 patients held out for testing. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-14B detection model achieves an F1 of 0.831 on the safety-critical E4 metric (90.9% recall, 76.5% precision) and an F1 of 0.823 on E3+E4, representing a 50.0% relative improvement over the base model and outperforming RAGTruth-style and QAGS-style baselines. These results demonstrate that evidence-chain-based graph retrieval verification substantially improves the factual reliability of clinical documentation, while simultaneously producing reusable annotated datasets for downstream model training and distillation.

</details>


### [78] [Rational Communication Shapes Morphological Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03510)
*Fengyuan Yang,Yongqian Peng,Yuxi Ma,Chenheng Xu,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在理性言语行为（RSA）框架下，利用历史语料库（COHA和COCA）中4323个英语复合词和派生词（1820-2019），证明语言中实际出现的形态组合（复合与派生）系统性地优于同时期可用的替代组合，且整合语义信息性与产出成本的语用模型优于纯语义或纯成本基线，表明词汇化过程体现了表达效率与交际效率之间的理性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言通过组合已有语素而非任意发明来扩展词汇，但形态组合（复合/派生）作为历史情境下说话者在竞争性语素序列中的选择，为何某种组合被选定而非其他合理替代方案，这一问题尚未被建模解释。

Method: 在RSA框架下，利用COHA和COCA构建时索引词典，生成4323个英语复合词和派生词（1820-2019），并将其与同时期可用语素构成的实际未出现替代组合进行对比，模型整合语义信息性与产出成本，以MRR和Acc@k评估。

Result: 实际出现的形态组合系统性地优于未出现的替代组合；整合语义信息性与产出成本的语用模型（S₁）优于纯语义和纯成本基线；随着候选集扩大，语用模型的优势增长，纯语义无法充分决定形态选择。

Conclusion: 词汇化反映了表达效率与交际效率之间的理性权衡，将理性沟通的解释从话语层面拓展至词内部结构。

Abstract: Human languages expand vocabularies by combining existing morphemes rather than inventing arbitrary forms. Communicative efficiency shapes lexical systems at multiple levels (Gibson et al., 2019), yet morphological composition -- combining morphemes through compounding or affixation -- has rarely been modeled as a historically situated speaker choice among competing morpheme sequences, leaving unanswered why a language settles on one morpheme combination over other plausible alternatives. We ask whether a trade-off between listener recoverability and speaker production cost can predict attested compositions over contemporaneously available alternatives. Here we show, within the Rational Speech Act (RSA) framework (Frank & Goodman, 2012; Goodman & Frank, 2016) using a time-indexed lexicon constructed from Corpus of Historical American English (COHA) and Corpus of Contemporary American English (COCA), that across 4323 naturally occurring English compounds and derivations spanning 1820--2019, attested compositions are systematically ranked above unattested alternatives generated from contemporaneously available morphemes. Models integrating semantic informativeness with production cost outperform semantic-only and cost-only baselines on Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) and top-k accuracy (Acc@k), with the advantage of the Pragmatic Speaker model ($S_1$) over the semantic-only baseline growing as the candidate set expands, where meaning alone leaves morphological choice underdetermined. These findings suggest that lexicalization reflects a communicative trade-off between expressiveness and efficiency, extending rational accounts of communication from utterance-level choice to the internal structure of words.

</details>


### [79] [Revisiting Graph-Tokenizing Large Language Models: A Systematic Evaluation of Graph Token Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03514)
*Zhongjian Zhang,Yue Yu,Mengmei Zhang,Junping Du,Xiao Wang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文质疑图分词LLM（GTokenLLM）是否真正理解图标记，通过提出统一框架和评估管道GTEval（涵盖格式和内容层面的指令变换），对6个代表性模型进行实验，发现现有模型对图标记理解不充分：对指令变化过度敏感或迟钝、严重依赖文本推理，且指令微调无法完全解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GTokenLLM作为一种广泛认可的范式，将复杂图数据压缩为图标记并作为前缀输入LLM，但现有研究默认LLM能充分理解这些图标记。作者质疑这一假设，旨在探究GTokenLLM是否真正在自然语言嵌入空间中理解图标记。

Method: 形式化定义了GTokenLLM的统一框架；提出GTEval评估管道，通过格式层面（如指令措辞变化）和内容层面（如任务信息修改）的指令变换来测试模型对图标记的理解；对6个代表性GTokenLLM进行系统实验。

Result: (1) 现有GTokenLLM未充分理解图标记，对指令变化表现出过度敏感或迟钝，且严重依赖文本进行推理；(2) 图标记虽保留了任务相关图信息并在LLM各层受到关注，但其利用率因模型和指令变体而异；(3) 额外指令微调可提升原始和已见指令的性能，但无法完全解决图标记理解挑战。

Conclusion: 现有GTokenLLM对图标记的理解存在根本性缺陷，简单增加指令微调无法根治，需要进一步改进模型设计以增强LLM对图标记的深层语义理解能力。

Abstract: The remarkable success of large language models (LLMs) has motivated researchers to adapt them as universal predictors for various graph tasks. As a widely recognized paradigm, Graph-Tokenizing LLMs (GTokenLLMs) compress complex graph data into graph tokens and treat them as prefix tokens for querying LLMs, leading many to believe that LLMs can understand graphs more effectively and efficiently. In this paper, we challenge this belief: \textit{Do GTokenLLMs fully understand graph tokens in the natural-language embedding space?} Motivated by this question, we formalize a unified framework for GTokenLLMs and propose an evaluation pipeline, \textbf{GTEval}, to assess graph-token understanding via instruction transformations at the format and content levels. We conduct extensive experiments on 6 representative GTokenLLMs with GTEval. The primary findings are as follows: (1) Existing GTokenLLMs do not fully understand graph tokens. They exhibit over-sensitivity or over-insensitivity to instruction changes, and rely heavily on text for reasoning; (2) Although graph tokens preserve task-relevant graph information and receive attention across LLM layers, their utilization varies across models and instruction variants; (3) Additional instruction tuning can improve performance on the original and seen instructions, but it does not fully address the challenge of graph-token understanding, calling for further improvement.

</details>


### [80] [PatRe: A Full-Stage Office Action and Rebuttal Generation Benchmark for Patent Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03571)
*Qiyao Wang,Xinyi Chen,Longze Chen,Hongbo Wang,Hamid Alinejad-Rokny,Yuan Lin,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PatRe基准，将专利审查建模为包含审查意见生成和申请人答辩的多轮交互过程，涵盖480个真实案例，评估了多种LLM在专利审查全生命周期中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将专利审查视为判别式分类或静态抽取任务，未能捕捉其固有的交互式和迭代式特性（类似于学术论文的同行评审与 rebuttal 过程），且专利申请量不断增长，亟需更贴近真实审查流程的评估框架。

Method: 构建了包含480个真实案例的PatRe基准，建模专利审查全生命周期（审查意见生成与申请人答辩两个环节），支持oracle（真实信息）和retrieval-simulated（检索模拟）两种评估设置。

Result: 实验揭示了闭源与开源模型之间的性能差异，以及审查员分析与申请人答辩之间的任务不对称性，展示了LLM在专利审查法律推理和技术新颖性判断方面的潜力与当前局限。

Conclusion: PatRe为专利审查建模提供了首个多轮交互式基准，揭示了LLM在该复杂法律推理任务中的能力边界，并公开代码与数据集以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Patent examination is a complex, multi-stage process requiring both technical expertise and legal reasoning, increasingly challenged by rising application volumes. Prior benchmarks predominantly view patent examination as discriminative classification or static extraction, failing to capture its inherently interactive and iterative nature, similar to the peer review and rebuttal process in academic publishing. In this paper, we introduce PatRe, the first benchmark that models the full patent examination lifecycle, including Office Action generation and applicant rebuttal. PatRe comprises 480 real-world cases and supports both oracle and retrieval-simulated evaluation settings. Our benchmark reframes patent examination as a dynamic, multi-turn process of justification and response. Extensive experiments across various LLMs reveal critical insights into model performance, including differences between proprietary and open-source models, as well as task asymmetries between examiner analysis and applicant-side rebuttal. These findings highlight both the potential and current limitations of LLMs in modeling complex, real-world legal reasoning and technical novelty judgment in patent examination. We release our code and dataset to facilitate future research on patent examination modeling.

</details>


### [81] [BIT.UA-AAUBS at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Evaluating Open-Source and Proprietary LLMs via Prompting in Low-Resource QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03618)
*Richard A. A. Jonker,Alexander Christiansen,Alexandros Maniatis,Rúben Garrido,Rogério Braunschweiger de Freitas Lima,Roman Jurowetzki,Sérgio Matos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍BIT.UA和AAUBS团队在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中的方法，利用无需权重更新的LLM（包括商业模型和开源模型），通过提示工程策略（任务分解、思维链、上下文学习）以及多数投票和LLM-as-a-judge集成技术，在临床问答和证据溯源的低资源场景下取得Subtask 4第一名和Subtask 3第三名的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗领域低资源设置下（无训练数据、严格数据隐私约束如GDPR），探索无需权重更新的LLM在临床问答和证据溯源任务中的能力。

Method: 使用多种提示工程策略（任务分解、Chain-of-Thought、上下文学习）评估多个商业模型和本地可部署的开源模型；采用多数投票和LLM-as-a-judge集成技术提升鲁棒性。

Result: 商业模型对提示变化具有强鲁棒性；领域适应的开源模型（如MedGemma 3 27B）配合适当提示可达到高度竞争性表现；在Subtask 4（证据引文对齐）获得第1名，Subtask 3（患者友好答案生成）获得第3名。

Conclusion: 基于提示的方法在低资源医疗QA任务中高度有效，无需模型权重更新即可取得领先性能。

Abstract: This paper presents the joint participation of the BIT.UA and AAUBS groups in the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task, which focuses on clinical question answering and evidence grounding in a low-resource setting. Due to the absence of training data and the strict data privacy constraints inherent to the healthcare domain (e.g. GDPR), we investigate the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) without weight updates. We evaluate several state-of-the-art proprietary models and locally deployable open-source alternatives using various prompt engineering strategies, including task decomposition, Chain-of-Thought, and in-context learning. Furthermore, we explore majority voting and LLM-as-a-judge ensembling techniques to maximize predictive robustness. Our results demonstrate that while proprietary models exhibit strong resilience to prompt variations, domain-adapted open-source models (such as MedGemma 3 27B) achieve highly competitive performance when paired with the right prompt. Overall, our prompt-based approach proved highly effective, securing 1st place in Subtask 4 (evidence citation alignment) and 3rd place in Subtask 3 (patient-friendly answer generation). All code, results, and prompts are available on our GitHub repository: https://github.com/bioinformatics-ua/ArchEHR-QA-2026.

</details>


### [82] [Annotation Quality in Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis: A Case Study Comparing Experts, Students, Crowdworkers, and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03624)
*Niklas Donhauser,Jakob Fehle,Nils Constantin Hellwig,Markus Weinberger,Udo Kruschwitz,Christian Wolff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同标注来源（专家、学生、众包工人、大语言模型）对德语基于方面的情感分析（ABSA）的影响，通过重新标注现有数据集建立黄金标准，比较标注一致性和下游模型性能，为低资源NLP场景下的数据集构建提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABSA在英语中已被广泛研究，但德语等语言由于缺乏高质量标注数据集而研究有限，因此需要探究不同标注来源对德语ABSA开发的影响。

Method: 对现有数据集进行专家重新标注以建立黄金标准，比较学生、众包工人、LLM和专家的标注质量，使用标注者间一致性（IAA）和下游模型性能进行评估，采用BERT、T5和LLaMA等SOTA方法进行ACSA和TASD子任务分析。

Result: 不同标注来源在标注质量和下游模型性能上存在差异，提供了标注可靠性与效率之间权衡的实证结果。

Conclusion: 研究为低资源NLP场景下的数据集构建提供了实用指导，揭示了不同标注来源在德语ABSA任务中的优劣。

Abstract: Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) enables fine-grained opinion analysis by identifying sentiments toward specific aspects or targets within a text. While ABSA has been widely studied for English, research on other languages such as German remains limited, largely due to the lack of high-quality annotated datasets. This paper examines how different annotation sources influence the development of German ABSA. To this end, an existing dataset is re-annotated by experts to establish a ground truth, which serves as a reference for evaluating annotations produced by students, crowdworkers, Large Language Models (LLMs), and experts. Annotation quality is compared using Inter-Annotator Agreement (IAA) and its impact on downstream model performance for different ABSA subtasks. The evaluation focuses on Aspect Category Sentiment Analysis (ACSA) and Target Aspect Sentiment Detection (TASD). We apply State-of-the-Art (SOTA) methods for ABSA, including BERT-, T5-, and LLaMA-based approaches to assess performance differences, spanning fine-tuning and in-context learning with instruction prompts. The findings provide practical insights into trade-offs between annotation reliability and efficiency, offering guidance for dataset construction in under-resourced Natural Language Processing (NLP) scenarios.

</details>


### [83] [A Paradigm for Interpreting Metrics and Identifying Critical Errors in Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03671)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将选定度量指标融入最小编辑距离（minED）的新范式，以在保持WER/CER可解释性的同时，更好地反映人类对语音转写错误的感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WER和CER虽然广泛使用，但与人类感知相关性差，且无法利用语言和语义信息；而基于度量的嵌入虽能近似人类感知，但其分数难以解释。因此需要一种兼具两者优点的评估方法。

Method: 提出minED（最小编辑距离）范式，将选定的度量指标融入编辑距离计算中，使转写错误的评估与其人类感知对齐。

Result: minED能够产生与人类感知对齐的错误率等价物，并可从人类视角研究不同错误的严重程度。

Conclusion: 该范式成功克服了现有指标可解释性与感知一致性不可兼得的矛盾，为语音转写评估提供了新的方向。

Abstract: The most commonly used metrics for evaluating automatic speech transcriptions, namely Word Error Rate (WER) and Character Error Rate (CER), have been heavily criticized for their poor correlation to human perception and their inability to take into account linguistic and semantic information. While metric-based embeddings, seeking to approximate human perception, have been proposed, their scores remain difficult to interpret, unlike WER and CER. In this article, we overcome this problem by proposing a paradigm that consists in incorporating a chosen metric into it in order to obtain an equivalent of the error rate: a Minimum Edit Distance (minED). This approach parallels transcription errors with their human perception, also allowing an original study of the severity of these errors from a human perspective.

</details>


### [84] [A Comprehensive Analysis of Tokenization and Self-Supervised Learning in End-to-End Automatic Speech Recognition applied on French Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03696)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对法语端到端ASR系统，研究了子词分词算法和自监督学习模型对系统性能的影响，超越了传统的CER/WER指标，采用更全面的评估指标集进行定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的CER和WER指标无法充分描述自动转录文本的下游应用效果，需要更全面的评估方法来理解超参数和模型选择对ASR系统真实性能的影响。

Method: 对法语进行定性研究，从不同语言学和声学视角，采用全面的评估指标集，探究子词分词算法和自监督学习模型的影响。

Result: 尚未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果，但指出将超越传统CER/WER指标进行多维度评估。

Conclusion: 超参数和模型选择对ASR性能至关重要，仅依赖CER/WER指标是不充分的，需要更全面的评估框架。

Abstract: The performance of end-to-end automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems enables their increasing integration into numerous applications. While there are various benefits to such speech-to-text systems, the choice of hyperparameters and models plays a crucial role in their performance. Typically, these choices are determined by considering only the character (CER) and/or word error rate (WER) metrics. However, it has been shown in several studies that these metrics are largely incomplete and fail to adequately describe the downstream application of automatic transcripts. In this paper, we conduct a qualitative study on the French language that investigates the impact of subword tokenization algorithms and self-supervised learning models from different linguistic and acoustic perspectives, using a comprehensive set of evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [85] [SERE: Structural Example Retrieval for Enhancing LLMs in Event Causality Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03701)
*Zhifeng Hao,Zhongjie Chen,Junhao Lu,Shengyin Yu,Guimin Hu,Keli Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Boyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SERE框架，通过基于概念路径、句法结构和因果模式的结构化示例检索，增强大语言模型在事件因果关系识别任务中的表现，缓解因果幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在事件因果关系识别任务中存在因果推理偏差，倾向于过度预测因果关系（因果幻觉），导致性能受限。

Method: SERE框架包含三种结构化检索策略：(i) 概念路径度量——基于ConceptNet编辑距离衡量事件间概念关系；(ii) 句法度量——通过句法树编辑距离量化结构相似性；(iii) 因果模式过滤——利用LLM基于预定义因果结构筛选示例。通过集成这些策略检索相关示例，引导LLM进行少样本因果推理。

Result: 在多个ECI数据集上的大量实验验证了SERE框架的有效性。

Conclusion: SERE通过结构化示例检索能够有效缓解LLM在ECI任务中的因果推理偏差，提升因果关系识别的准确性。

Abstract: Event Causality Identification (ECI) requires models to determine whether a given pair of events in a context exhibits a causal relationship. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across various NLP tasks, their effectiveness in ECI remains limited due to biases in causal reasoning, often leading to overprediction of causal relationships (causal hallucination). To mitigate these issues and enhance LLM performance in ECI, we propose SERE, a structural example retrieval framework that leverages LLMs' few-shot learning capabilities. SERE introduces an innovative retrieval mechanism based on three structural concepts: (i) Conceptual Path Metric, which measures the conceptual relationship between events using edit distance in ConceptNet; (ii) Syntactic Metric, which quantifies structural similarity through tree edit distance on syntactic trees; and (iii) Causal Pattern Filtering, which filters examples based on predefined causal structures using LLMs. By integrating these structural retrieval strategies, SERE selects more relevant examples to guide LLMs in causal reasoning, mitigating bias and improving accuracy in ECI tasks. Extensive experiments on multiple ECI datasets validate the effectiveness of SERE. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/DMIRLAB-Group/SERE.

</details>


### [86] [SAM-NER: Semantic Archetype Mediation for Zero-Shot Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03706)
*Ruichu Cai,Juntao Gan,Miao Mai,Zhifeng Hao,Boyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAM-NER提出基于语义原型中介的三阶段零样本命名实体识别框架，通过领域不变的原型空间稳定跨域迁移，在CrossNER基准上超越先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本NER在领域和模式迁移下表现脆弱，因为未见标签定义与LLM内在语义组织之间常存在错位，直接映射实体到细粒度标签会导致系统性的语义漂移，尤其当目标模式新颖或语义重叠时。

Method: 三阶段框架：(i) 实体发现——通过协同抽取和基于共识的去噪获得高覆盖、高保真实体跨度；(ii) 抽象中介——将实体投影到从高层本体抽象中提炼的紧凑通用语义原型集；(iii) 语义校准——通过约束的、定义对齐的推理，用冻结的LLM将原型级预测解析为目标领域类型。

Result: 在CrossNER基准上，SAM-NER在跨领域设置中一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过中间领域不变的原型空间进行语义中介，能有效稳定零样本NER的跨域迁移，缓解标签定义与LLM语义组织之间的错位问题。

Abstract: Zero-shot Named Entity Recognition (ZS-NER) remains brittle under domain and schema shifts, where unseen label definitions often misalign with a large language model's (LLM's) intrinsic semantic organization. As a result, directly mapping entity mentions to fine-grained target labels can induce systematic semantic drift, especially when target schemas are novel or semantically overlapping. We propose \textbf{SAM-NER}, a three-stage framework based on \emph{Semantic Archetype Mediation} that stabilizes cross-domain transfer through an intermediate, domain-invariant archetype space. SAM-NER: (i) performs \emph{Entity Discovery} via cooperative extraction and consensus-based denoising to obtain high-coverage, high-fidelity entity spans; (ii) conducts \emph{Abstract Mediation} by projecting entities into a compact set of universal semantic archetypes distilled from high-level ontological abstractions; and (iii) applies \emph{Semantic Calibration} to resolve archetype-level predictions into target-domain types through constrained, definition-aligned inference with a frozen LLM. Experiments on the CrossNER benchmark show that SAM-NER consistently outperforms strong prior ZS-NER baselines in cross-domain settings. Our implementation will be open-sourced at https://github.com/DMIRLAB-Group/SAM-NER.

</details>


### [87] [Segmenting Human-LLM Co-authored Text via Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03723)
*Mengchu Li,Jin Zhu,Jinglai Li,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出将人类与LLM合著文本分割任务类比为时间序列中的变点检测问题，设计了加权和广义算法以实现最优分割，实验表现优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM文本检测器仅提供整段二分类，无法处理人类与LLM合著文本中需要定位具体片段的场景。

Method: 将文本分割任务类比为时间序列变点检测，据此适配LLM文本检测场景，开发加权算法和广义算法以应对异构检测分数变异性，并证明了方法的极小化极大最优性。

Result: 在广泛的基线对比中，所提方法表现出优异的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于变点检测的文本分割方法有效解决了人类与LLM合著文本中片段定位问题，理论上达到了最优性，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: The rise of large language models (LLMs) has created an urgent need to distinguish between human-written and LLM-generated text to ensure authenticity and societal trust. Existing detectors typically provide a binary classification for an entire passage; however, this is insufficient for human--LLM co-authored text, where the objective is to localize specific segments authored by humans or LLMs. To bridge this gap, we propose algorithms to segment text into human- and LLM-authored pieces. Our key observation is that such a segmentation task is conceptually similar to classical change point detection in time-series analysis. Leveraging this analogy, we adapt change point detection to LLM-generated text detection, develop a weighted algorithm and a generalized algorithm to accommodate heterogeneous detection score variability, and establish the minimax optimality of our procedure. Empirically, we demonstrate the strong performance of our approach against a wide range of existing baselines.

</details>


### [88] [Benchmarking Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models for Low-Resource Tajik Text Generation with the Tajik Web Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03742)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源塔吉克语（西里尔字母），创建了最大的开源语料库Tajik Web Corpus（319,298篇文档，约11.1亿字符），并在17种配置下系统比较了自回归、编码器-解码器和编码器-only三类模型与全微调、LoRA、QLoRA三种微调策略的性能。最优结果为Mistral 7B + QLoRA (r=16)，平均困惑度5.03；小型GPT-2全微调困惑度更低（3.48）但存在灾难性遗忘；XLM-RoBERTa表现最差（困惑度59.3）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塔吉克语作为低资源语言，缺乏足够的高质量数字化文本资源，且此前缺乏针对该语言的生成式大语言模型适应性以及参数高效微调（PEFT）策略的系统评估。

Method: 构建Tajik Web Corpus语料库，在10,000篇文档子样本上对17种配置进行基准测试，涵盖自回归（GPT-2系列、Mistral 7B等）、编码器-解码器（T5等）和编码器-only（XLM-RoBERTa）三类架构，使用全微调、LoRA和QLoRA（r=8和r=16）三种策略，通过困惑度、交叉熵损失、峰值GPU内存和训练时间进行评估。

Result: Mistral 7B + QLoRA (r=16) 取得最佳综合性能（平均困惑度5.03，标准差0.03）；LoRA秩从8提升至16改进不显著但增加内存消耗；GPT-2 Medium全微调困惑度最低（3.48）但出现灾难性遗忘；XLM-RoBERTa困惑度高达59.3。

Conclusion: 建议在塔吉克语文本生成任务中使用Mistral 7B结合QLoRA微调，可在计算成本与生成质量间取得良好平衡；大型模型使用PEFT策略优于全微调；为低资源语言的LLM适配提供了系统性的实验依据和实际选型建议。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the adaptation of generative large language models for the Tajik language, a low-resource language with Cyrillic script. To overcome the shortage of digital text resources, the author created and publicly released the Tajik Web Corpus, the largest open-access corpus of Tajik, comprising 319,298 documents (~1.11 billion characters). On a subsample of 10,000 documents, 17 configurations were benchmarked, covering autoregressive, encoder-decoder, and encoder-only models with three fine-tuning strategies: full fine-tuning, LoRA, and QLoRA (ranks 8 and 16). Quality was assessed via perplexity and cross-entropy loss; peak GPU memory and training time were also recorded. Best results were achieved by Mistral 7B with QLoRA (r=16): mean perplexity 5.03, standard deviation 0.03. Increasing rank from 8 to 16 gave statistically insignificant improvement while raising memory consumption. For small GPT-2 family models, full fine-tuning yielded lower perplexity (3.48 for GPT-2 Medium) than LoRA (7.60-8.42), but induced catastrophic forgetting. The encoder-only XLM-RoBERTa showed the worst results (perplexity 59.3). The novelty lies in creating the largest verified Tajik corpus and the first systematic analysis of PEFT effectiveness for Tajik text generation. Practical value lies in recommendations for architecture and fine-tuning strategy selection, optimizing computational costs without substantial quality loss.

</details>


### [89] [TriBench-Ko: Evaluating LLM Risks in Judicial Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03792)
*Haesung Lee,Gyubin Choi,Eun-Ju Lee,So-Min Lee,Youkang Ko,Dogyoon Lim,Sung-Kyoung Jang,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TriBench-Ko是一个针对韩国司法流程的LLM风险评估基准，涵盖四大核心任务及多种部署风险类别（不准确性、偏见、不一致性、司法越权），评估显示当前模型在判例检索和法律信息捕获方面存在显著风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM法律基准主要关注替代性任务（如律师资格考试、分类任务），无法反映日常司法流程中的实际性能与风险。需构建基于真实司法任务需求的评估基准以检测部署风险。

Method: 基于韩国真实司法判决，构建覆盖法理摘要、判例检索、法律争议点提取、证据分析四项核心任务的基准TriBench-Ko，系统评估不准确性（幻觉、遗漏、法律误用）、偏见（人口统计、过度合规）、不一致性（提示敏感性、非确定性）和司法越权四类部署风险。

Result: 多种当代LLM在判例检索任务中表现困难，频繁遗漏关键法律信息，暴露出显著的风险问题。

Conclusion: LLM在司法场景中的输出需要严格审查，本研究为识别和诊断这些风险提供了系统性的评估框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into legal workflows. However, existing benchmarks primarily address proxy tasks, such as bar examination performance or classification, which fail to capture the performance and risks inherent in day-to-day judicial processes. To address this, we publicly release TriBench-Ko, a Korean benchmark designed to evaluate potential deployment risks of LLMs within the context of verified judicial task requirements. It covers four core tasks: jurisprudence summarization, precedent retrieval, legal issue extraction, and evidence analysis. It jointly assesses model behavior across multiple deployment risk categories, including inaccuracy (hallucination, omission, statutory misapplication), biases (demographic, overcompliance), inconsistencies (prompt sensitivity, non-determinism), and adjudicative overreach. Each item is structured to systematically assess both task performance and a specific risk type based on real judicial decisions. Our evaluation of a range of contemporary LLMs reveals that many models frequently manifest significant risks, most notably struggling with precedent retrieval and failing to capture critical legal information. We provide a comprehensive diagnosis of these LLMs and pinpoint critical areas where LLM-generated outputs in judicial contexts necessitate rigorous inspection and caution. Our dataset and code are available at https://github.com/holi-lab/TriBench-Ko

</details>


### [90] [Natural Language Processing: A Comprehensive Practical Guide from Tokenisation to RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03799)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本预印本提出了一套系统化的研究型实践课程，涵盖从分词、向量化到大语言模型微调、检索增强生成和人类反馈强化学习的完整现代NLP流程，包含12个动手实验，强调可复现的研究制品和开放权重模型，并融入针对塔吉克语和鞑靼语等低资源语言的原创研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补现代NLP教育中缺乏系统性、可复现、端到端实践课程的空白，特别是针对低资源语言场景，推动开放科学和可复现研究文化。

Method: 设计12个结构化实践单元，每个单元结合精简理论与详细实现计划、形式化评估指标和透明评分标准；要求所有实验在公开仓库中发布代码、模型和报告；采用单一演化语料库；优先使用开源权重模型和Hugging Face生态系统；融入塔吉克语和鞑靼语的子词分词器、词嵌入、词汇数据库和音译基准等低资源语言资源。

Result: 形成了一套完整的、可复现的NLP实践课程框架，并在低资源语言（塔吉克语和鞑靼语）上验证了现代NLP技术对数据稀缺环境的适配能力。

Conclusion: 该实践课程可作为高年级本科生、研究生和从业者从传统机器学习到前沿LLM系统实现、比较和部署的综合指南，尤其适用于低资源语言场景。

Abstract: This preprint presents a systematic, research-oriented practicum that guides the reader through the entire modern NLP pipeline: from tokenisation and vectorisation to fine-tuning of large language models, retrieval-augmented generation, and reinforcement learning from human feedback. Twelve hands-on sessions combine concise theory with detailed implementation plans, formalised evaluation metrics, and transparent assessment criteria. The work is not a conventional textbook: it is designed as a reproducible research artefact where every session requires publishing code, models, and reports in public repositories. All experiments are conducted on a single evolving corpus, and the work advocates open-weight models over commercial APIs, with special attention to the Hugging Face ecosystem. The material is enriched by original research on low-resource languages, incorporating linguistic resources for Tajik and Tatar (subword tokenisers, embeddings, lexical databases, and transliteration benchmarks), demonstrating how modern NLP can be adapted to data-scarce environments. Designed for senior undergraduates, graduate students, and practising developers seeking to implement, compare, and deploy methods from classical ML to state-of-the-art LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [91] [Reproducing Complex Set-Compositional Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03824)
*Vincent Degenhart,Dewi Timman,Arjen P. de Vries,Faegheh Hasibi,Mohanna Hoveyda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过可重复性研究，发现在标准基准QUEST上，神经检索器效果远超BM25（Recall@100 >0.41 vs 0.20），但推理导向方法（如ReasonIR、Search-R1）并未普遍优于通用检索器；在引入的受控基准LIMIT+上，最佳QUEST方法效果从Recall@100≈0.42暴跌至0.02以下，而经典词法检索反而提升至~0.96；按组合深度分层后，密集方法性能崩溃，而稀疏和词法方法表现更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索范式是否真正满足集合组合查询（含合取、析取、排除）的约束，还是利用了预训练知识中的"语义捷径"尚不明确，因此需要系统性的可重复性研究和受控基准测试。

Method: 在QUEST和QUEST+Variants基准上对主要检索家族和推理导向方法进行可重复性研究；引入LIMIT+受控基准，使相关性依赖于任意属性谓词和约束满足，减少对预训练知识的依赖。按组合深度分层评估各方法性能。

Result: (i) QUEST上神经检索器Recall@100>0.41，是BM25(0.20)的两倍多，但推理方法并不普遍优于通用检索器；(ii) LIMIT+上最强QUEST方法从Recall@100≈0.42降至<0.02，而词法检索升至~0.96；(iii) 按组合深度分层，密集方法性能崩溃，稀疏和词法方法更稳定。

Conclusion: 现有神经检索和推理方法在受控组合查询场景下泛化能力不足，密集方法存在严重过拟合风险，而词法和稀疏方法更具鲁棒性，亟需更可控的评估基准来推动可靠进展。

Abstract: Complex information needs may involve set-compositional queries using conjunction, disjunction, and exclusion, yet it remains unclear whether current retrieval paradigms genuinely satisfy such constraints or exploit `semantic shortcuts'. We conduct a reproducibility study to benchmark major retrieval families and reasoning-targeted methods on QUEST and QUEST+Variants, and introduce LIMIT+, a controlled benchmark where relevance depends on arbitrary attribute predicates and constraint satisfaction, and less on pretrained knowledge. Our findings show that (i) on QUEST, the best neural retrievers achieve an effectiveness that is more than double what can be achieved with BM25 (Recall@100 ${>}$0.41 vs.\ 0.20), but reasoning-targeted methods like ReasonIR and Search-R1 do not outperform general-purpose retrievers uniformly; (ii) on LIMIT+, gains fail to transfer, where the strongest QUEST method collapses from Recall@100${\approx}$0.42 to below 0.02, while classic lexical retrieval gains to ${\sim}$0.96. Lastly, (iii) stratifying by compositional depth reveals a consistent degradation across all methods, where algebraic sparse and lexical methods show more stable performance while dense approaches collapse. We release code and LIMIT+ data generation scripts to support future reproducibility and controlled evaluation.

</details>


### [92] [TRACE: A Metrologically-Grounded Engineering Framework for Trustworthy Agentic AI Systems in Operationally Critical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03838)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE是一个面向关键操作领域的可信自主AI的跨领域工程框架，采用四层参考架构，通过L2a（经典ML）与L2b（LLM验证器）分离、状态化编排与升级策略（L3）、有限人类监督（L4）以及基于GUM/VIM/ISO 17025的计量学信任度量套件实现可信性；引入计算简约比（CPR）作为首要设计原则，并在临床决策、工业多域运营和司法AI助手三个领域验证了其跨治理语境的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可信AI工程缺乏跨领域、跨治理语境的统一架构，尤其是在关键操作领域中，大语言模型常被不加区分地作为默认设计选择，缺少对模型简约性（是否必须使用LLM）的量化评估与约束机制。

Method: 提出四层参考架构：L2a（经典ML模型）与L2b（LLM验证器）的显式分离、L3（状态化编排与升级策略）、L4（有限人类监督）；基于GUM/VIM/ISO 17025计量学标准设计信任度量套件；提出计算简约比（CPR）量化模型简约性原理；在三个不同治理语境（临床决策、工业多域运营、司法AI）中实例化验证。

Result: 在三个跨领域场景中成功迁移了同一架构与度量体系，证明了TRACE框架在不同治理语境下的通用性；通过L2a/L2b分离将LLM的使用从架构默认选择转变为有意识的设计决策；CPR作为量化工具实现了模型简约性的可度量评估。

Conclusion: TRACE框架为关键操作领域的可信AI工程提供了跨领域统一的架构与度量体系，将计算简约比（CPR）确立为可信AI工程中的一级设计原则，为在需要时审慎使用LLM而非默认使用LLM提供了工程方法论支撑。

Abstract: We introduce TRACE, a cross-domain engineering framework for trustworthy agentic AI in operationally critical domains. TRACE combines a four-layer reference architecture with an explicit classical-ML vs. LLM-validator split (L2a/L2b), a stateful orchestration-and-escalation policy (L3), and bounded human supervision (L4); a metrologically grounded trust-metric suite mapped to GUM/VIM/ISO 17025; and a Model-Parsimony principle quantified by the Computational Parsimony Ratio (CPR). Three instantiations--clinical decision support, industrial multi-domain operations, and a judicial AI assistant--transfer the samearchitecture and metrics across principally different governance contexts. The L2a/L2b separation makes the use of large language models a deliberate design decision rather than an architectural default, with parsimony quantified through CPR. TRACE introduces CPR as a first-class design principle in trustworthy-AI engineering.

</details>


### [93] [MCJudgeBench: A Benchmark for Constraint-Level Judge Evaluation in Multi-Constraint Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03858)
*Jaeyun Lee,Junyoung Koh,Zeynel Tok,Hunar Batra,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MCJudgeBench，一个用于多约束指令遵循中约束级评测的基准，揭示了LLM评判员在约束层面存在正确性与不一致性之间的复杂关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评判员的评估仅关注整体响应判断，忽略了在多约束指令遵循场景中，评判员是否能在每个独立约束上正确判断的能力未被充分研究。

Method: 构建MCJudgeBench基准，包含指令、候选响应、显式约束列表、每个约束的金标签（yes/partial/no）以及受控的响应侧扰动；评估方案包含多种提示变体以测试评判稳定性；使用正确性和不一致性两个指标，区分随机解码下的内在不一致性与提示/响应扰动下的过程不一致性。

Result: 强的整体性能不能保证在所有标签类别上同样可靠的检测，尤其是对稀有的partial和no类别；正确性更高的评判员不一定具有更低的不一致性；使用推理进行评判可提高正确性但并不能一致地提升稳定性。

Conclusion: LLM评判员的可靠性具有多个维度，需要在约束层面进行评估以研究这些失败模式。

Abstract: Multi-constraint instruction following requires verifying whether a response satisfies multiple individual requirements, yet LLM judges are often assessed only through overall-response judgments. We introduce MCJudgeBench, a benchmark for constraint-level judge evaluation in multi-constraint instruction following. Each instance includes an instruction, a candidate response, an explicit constraint list, per-constraint gold labels in {yes, partial, no}, and controlled response-side perturbations. The evaluation protocol further includes evaluation prompt variants to test judge stability. We evaluate proprietary and open-source LLM judges using both correctness and inconsistency metrics, distinguishing intrinsic inconsistency under stochastic decoding from procedural inconsistency under prompt and response perturbations. Our results show that judge reliability has multiple dimensions: strong overall performance does not guarantee equally reliable detection across label categories, especially for rarer partial and no cases. Judges with higher correctness do not always have lower inconsistency. Evaluation with reasoning improves correctness but does not uniformly improve stability. These findings motivate evaluating LLM judges at the constraint level to study these failure modes.

</details>


### [94] [CC-OCR V2: Benchmarking Large Multimodal Models for Literacy in Real-world Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03903)
*Zhipeng Xu,Junhao Ji,Zulong Chen,Zhenghao Liu,Qing Liu,Chunyi Peng,Zubao Qin,Ze Xu,Jianqiang Wan,Jun Tang,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CC-OCR V2是一个面向真实世界文档处理场景的综合性OCR基准测试，包含7093个高难度样本，涵盖5个主要OCR任务。实验表明当前最先进的LMMs在实际应用中性能显著下降，揭示了现有基准与现实需求之间存在的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR基准的任务范围与实际应用不匹配，且假设同质化的采集条件，导致LMMs在真实世界文档处理中的有效性未被充分探索。

Method: 构建CC-OCR V2基准，聚焦企业级文档处理任务，纳入先前基准中缺失的困难和边界情况，覆盖文本识别、文档解析、文档定位、关键信息提取和文档问答5个OCR核心赛道，包含7093个高难度样本，并在14个先进LMMs上进行评估。

Result: 当前最先进的LMMs在CC-OCR V2上表现不足，无法满足真实世界应用需求；即使在最强模型中，跨不同任务和场景的性能也出现显著下降。

Conclusion: 现有基准性能与真实世界应用效果之间存在显著差距，CC-OCR V2有助于推动LMMs在文档智能领域向实际应用方向发展。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently shown strong performance on Optical Character Recognition (OCR) tasks, demonstrating their promising capability in document literacy. However, their effectiveness in real-world applications remains underexplored, as existing benchmarks adopt task scopes misaligned with practical applications and assume homogeneous acquisition conditions. To address this gap, we introduce CC-OCR V2, a comprehensive and challenging OCR benchmark tailored to real-world document processing. CC-OCR V2 focuses on practical enterprise document processing tasks and incorporates hard and corner cases that are critical yet underrepresented in prior benchmarks, covering 5 major OCR-centric tracks: text recognition, document parsing, document grounding, key information extraction, and document question answering, comprising 7,093 high-difficulty samples. Extensive experiments on 14 advanced LMMs reveal that current models fall short of real-world application requirements. Even state-of-the-art LMMs exhibit substantial performance degradation across diverse tasks and scenarios. These findings reveal a significant gap between performance on current benchmarks and effectiveness in real-world applications. We release the full dataset and evaluation toolkit at https://github.com/eioss/CC-OCR-V2.

</details>


### [95] [Steer Like the LLM: Activation Steering that Mimics Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03907)
*Geert Heyman,Frederik Vandeputte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PSR框架，通过训练简单可解释模型来模仿提示工程的行为，从而弥合激活干预与提示工程之间的性能差距。PSR能从激活值中估计每个token的特定干预系数，在多个基准测试中优于现有激活干预方法，甚至在某些任务上与提示工程相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活干预方法在推理时控制大语言模型行为的效果往往不如提示工程方法，且不忠实于提示工程的工作原理（对不同token施加不同强度的干预）。作者希望弥合这一差距，同时保持激活干预的可解释性和简便性。

Method: 提出Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR)模型：1）分析提示工程与激活干预在token级别干预强度的差异；2）设计PSR模型，从激活值中估计token特定的干预系数；3）通过蒸馏成功提示工程的行为来训练PSR模型。

Result: 在三个基准测试和多个语言模型上，PSR模型优于现有激活干预方法，在控制高连贯性生成时表现尤为突出；在AxBench和人格引导任务上与提示工程效果相当。

Conclusion: 通过将提示工程的行为蒸馏为可解释的激活干预模型，能够有效缩小两者之间的性能差距，PSR框架是一种有前景的大语言模型行为控制方法。

Abstract: Large language models can be steered at inference time through prompting or activation interventions, but activation steering methods often underperform compared to prompt-based approaches. We propose a framework that formulates prompt steering as a form of activation steering and investigates whether distilling successful prompt steering behavior into simpler, interpretable models can close this gap. Our analysis reveals that popular activation steering methods are not faithful to the mechanics of prompt steering, which applies strong interventions on some tokens while barely affecting others. Based on these insights, we introduce Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR) models that estimate token-specific steering coefficients from the activations themselves and are trained to imitate prompt-based interventions. Experiments on three steering benchmarks across multiple language models show that PSR models outperform existing activation steering methods, especially when controlling for high-coherence completions, and also compare favorably to prompting on AxBench and persona steering.

</details>


### [96] [Atomic Fact-Checking Increases Clinician Trust in Large Language Model Recommendations for Oncology Decision Support: A Randomized Controlled Trial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03916)
*Lisa C. Adams,Linus Marx,Erik Thiele Orberg,Keno Bressem,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Denise Bernhardt,Markus Graf,Marcus R. Makowski,Stephanie E. Combs,Florian Matthes,Jan C. Peeken*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 原子事实核查（将AI治疗建议分解为可独立验证且链接到源指南文档的声明）相比传统可解释性方法，能显著提高临床医生对AI建议的信任度（Cohen's d=0.94），使信任比例从26.9%提升至66.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在医疗AI领域，传统的可解释性方法是否足以建立临床医生对AI治疗建议的信任，以及原子事实核查这一新型方法是否能更有效地提升信任水平。

Method: 随机对照试验，涉及356名临床医生，共收集7,476个信任评级。比较原子事实核查与传统透明机制（可解释性方法）对信任的影响，使用Cohen's d量化效应量。

Result: 原子事实核查产生大效应（Cohen's d=0.94），信任比例从26.9%升至66.5%；传统透明机制呈现剂量-反应梯度（d=0.25至0.50）。

Conclusion: 在高风险临床决策场景中，将AI建议分解为可独立验证且链接到源指南的声明，能比传统可解释性方法产生实质性更高的临床医生信任。

Abstract: Question: Does atomic fact-checking, which decomposes AI treatment recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guideline documents, increase clinician trust compared to traditional explainability approaches?
  Findings: In this randomized trial of 356 clinicians generating 7,476 trust ratings, atomic fact-checking produced a large effect on trust (Cohen's d = 0.94), increasing the proportion of clinicians expressing trust from 26.9% to 66.5%. Traditional transparency mechanisms showed a dose-response gradient of improvement over baseline (d = 0.25 to 0.50).
  Meaning: Decomposing AI recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guidelines produces substantially higher clinician trust than traditional explainability approaches in high-stakes clinical decisions.

</details>


### [97] [The Counterexample Game: Iterated Conceptual Analysis and Repair in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03936)
*Daniel Drucker,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨语言模型能否通过迭代分析与修复链（生成反例→修复定义→重复循环）执行哲学方法论中的概念分析任务。实验涵盖20个概念及数千个反例-修复周期，发现LM生成的反例虽多被判定无效，但LM评判者的接受率约是人类的2倍；迭代会产生更冗长但无更高准确率的定义；部分概念难以形成稳定定义。研究表明LM能参与哲学推理，但反例-修复循环的收益递减迅速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型是否能够执行哲学方法论中的核心任务——概念分析（提出定义并通过反例进行精炼），并评估其在这一高层次迭代推理任务中的表现能力与局限性。

Method: 采用迭代分析与修复链范式：一个LM实例生成对已有定义的反例，另一个LM实例根据反例修复定义，如此循环往复。实验覆盖20个哲学概念，进行数千次反例-修复周期。评估方式包括专家人类评判与LM评判。

Result: 1) LM生成的反例多数被判定无效，但LM评判者的接受率约为人类的两倍；2) 逐项有效性判断在人类之间以及人类与LM之间具有中等程度的一致性；3) 迭代次数的增加导致定义愈发冗长，但并未提升定义准确性；4) 部分概念始终难以形成稳定定义。

Conclusion: 语言模型能够在一定程度上参与哲学推理中的反例-修复循环，但这一过程收益递减迅速，难以持续提升定义质量。该任务可作为评估LM能否维持高层次迭代哲学推理能力的有效测试用例。

Abstract: Conceptual analysis -- proposing definitions and refining them through counterexamples -- is central to philosophical methodology. We study whether language models can perform this task through iterated analysis and repair chains: one model instance generates counterexamples to a proposed definition, another repairs the definition, and the process repeats. Across 20 concepts and thousands of counterexample-repair cycles, we find that, although many LM-generated counterexamples are judged invalid by both expert humans and an LM judge, the LM judge accepts roughly twice as many as humans do. Nonetheless, per-item validity judgments are moderately consistent across humans and between humans and the LM. We further find that extended iteration produces increasingly verbose definitions without improving accuracy. We also see that some concepts resist stable definitions in general. These findings suggest that while LMs can engage in philosophical reasoning, the counterexample-repair loop hits diminishing returns quickly and could be a fruitful test case for evaluating whether LMs can sustain high-level iterated philosophical reasoning.

</details>


### [98] [Feature-Augmented Transformers for Robust AI-Text Detection Across Domains and Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03969)
*Mohamed Mady,Johannes Reschke,Björn Schuller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于注意力机制的语言特征融合方法(FeatAttn)增强Transformer检测器对AI生成文本的跨域鲁棒性，在固定阈值协议下，DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn模型在M4基准上达到85.9%平衡准确率，优于零样本基线最高7.22个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成文本在各类域和生成管道中被大规模生产，使得对分布偏移的鲁棒性成为监督式二分类检测器的核心需求。现有检测器在域迁移下表现脆弱，亟需提升跨域泛化能力。

Method: 采用Transformer检测器（基于HC3 PLUS训练），通过最大化验证集平衡准确率校准单一决策阈值并固定用于所有下游测试；提出基于注意力的语言特征融合(FeatAttn)进行特征增强；在HC3 PLUS（域内）、M4（跨数据集迁移）和AI-Text-Detection-Pile（外部）上评估。

Result: 基础模型域内平衡准确率达99.5%，但分布偏移下性能脆弱且模型依赖性强；DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn在M4上达85.9%平衡准确率，多种子实验确认高稳定性；在固定阈值协议下超越强零样本基线最高7.22个百分点；消融实验显示可读性和词汇特征对鲁棒性贡献最大。

Conclusion: 特征增强与现代DeBERTa骨干显著优于早期BERT/RoBERTa模型；固定阈值协议为检测器实际鲁棒性提供了更真实、更具信息量的评估方式。

Abstract: AI-generated text is nowadays produced at scale across domains and heterogeneous generation pipelines, making robustness to distribution shift a central requirement for supervised binary detectors. We train transformer-based detectors on HC3 PLUS and calibrate a single decision threshold by maximising balanced accuracy on held-out validation; this threshold is then kept fixed for all downstream test distributions, revealing domain- and generator-dependent error asymmetries under shift. We evaluate in-domain on HC3 PLUS, under cross-dataset transfer to the multi-domain, multi-generator M4 benchmark, and on the external AI-Text-Detection-Pile. Although base models achieve near-ceiling in-domain performance (up to 99.5% balanced accuracy), performance under shift is brittle and strongly model-dependent. Feature augmentation via attention-based linguistic feature fusion improves transfer, with our best model (DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn) achieving 85.9% balanced accuracy on M4. Multi-seed experiments confirm high stability. Under the same fixed-threshold protocol, our model outperforms strong zero-shot baselines by up to +7.22 points. Category-level ablations further show that readability and vocabulary features contribute most to robustness under shift. Overall, these results demonstrate that feature augmentation and a modern DeBERTa backbone significantly outperform earlier BERT/RoBERTa models, while the fixed-threshold protocol provides a more realistic and informative assessment of practical detector robustness.

</details>


### [99] [Logical Consistency as a Bridge: Improving LLM Hallucination Detection via Label Constraint Modeling between Responses and Self-Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03971)
*Hao Mi,Qiang Sheng,Shaofei Wang,Beizhe Hu,Yifan Sun,Zhengjia Wang,Hengqi Zeng,Yang Li,Danding Wang,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LaaB框架，通过逻辑一致性桥接神经特征和符号判断，利用元判断过程将符号标签映射回特征空间，通过相互学习对齐和整合双视角信号，有效检测LLM事实性幻觉，在4个数据集、4种LLM和8个基线中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法仅从单一视角（微观层级的神经不确定性或宏观层级的符号推理）处理幻觉问题，未能利用两者之间的内在耦合关系与相互依赖，缺乏整体性视角。

Method: 提出LaaB框架，核心包含"元判断"过程，将符号标签映射回特征空间；利用响应与元判断标签的逻辑一致性（相同或相反）作为桥梁，通过相互学习对齐并整合神经特征和符号判断的双视角信号，增强幻觉检测。

Result: 在4个公开数据集、4种LLM和8个基线方法的对比实验中，LaaB展现出优越性能。

Conclusion: LaaB通过桥接神经特征与符号判断的双重视角，有效利用了二者的内在逻辑一致性，为幻觉检测提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are prone to factual hallucinations, risking their reliability in real-world applications. Existing hallucination detectors mainly extract micro-level intrinsic patterns for uncertainty quantification or elicit macro-level self-judgments through verbalized prompts. However, these methods address only a single facet of the hallucination, focusing either on implicit neural uncertainty or explicit symbolic reasoning, thereby treating these inherently coupled behaviors in isolation and failing to exploit their interdependence for a holistic view. In this paper, we propose LaaB (Logical Consistency-as-a-Bridge), a framework that bridges neural features and symbolic judgments for hallucination detection. LaaB introduces a "meta-judgment" process to map symbolic labels back into the feature space. By leveraging the inherent logical bridge where response and meta-judgment labels are either the same or opposite based on the self-judgment's semantics, LaaB aligns and integrates dual-view signals via mutual learning and enhances the hallucination detection. Extensive experiments on 4 public datasets, across 4 LLMs, against 8 baselines demonstrate the superiority of LaaB.

</details>


### [100] [EQUITRIAGE: A Fairness Audit of Gender Bias in LLM-Based Emergency Department Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03998)
*Richard J. Young,Alice M. Matthews*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EQUITRIAGE对5个LLM在37.4万次急诊分诊评估中进行公平性审计，发现所有模型性别翻转率超过5%阈值（9.9%-43.8%），两个模型存在女性低分诊偏向，去偏干预效果因模型而异，表明群体公平性、反事实不变性和性别校准是不同的公平属性，应在临床部署前进行逐模型反事实审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊分诊中存在持久的性别偏差，医院正在试点LLM作为分诊决策辅助工具，需要评估这些模型是再现还是减轻已知的性别偏差。

Method: 使用MIMIC-IV-ED数据集中的18,714个急诊 vignette，生成了9,346个性别交换的反事实对，在五种LLM（Gemini-3-Flash, Nemotron-3-Super, DeepSeek-V3.1, Mistral-Small-3.2, GPT-4.1-Nano）上进行374,275次评估，采用四种提示策略，包括人口统计信息盲化、年龄保留盲化和思维链提示。

Result: 所有模型翻转率超过5%（9.9%-43.8%）；DeepSeek和Gemini存在女性低分诊偏向（F/M比值分别为2.15:1和1.34:1）；人口统计信息盲化将Gemini翻转率降至0.5%；年龄保留盲化后DeepSeek残余F/M为1.25；思维链提示降低所有模型准确性；不同模型相同偏向表型背后机制不同。

Conclusion: 群体公平性、反事实不变性和性别校准是不同的公平属性；干预措施有效性因模型而异；临床部署前应进行逐模型反事实审计。

Abstract: Emergency department triage assigns patients an acuity score that determines treatment priority, and clinical evidence documents persistent gender disparities in human acuity assessment. As hospitals pilot large language models (LLMs) as triage decision support, a critical question is whether these models reproduce or mitigate known biases. We present EQUITRIAGE, a fairness audit of LLM-based ESI assignment evaluating five models (Gemini-3-Flash, Nemotron-3-Super, DeepSeek-V3.1, Mistral-Small-3.2, GPT-4.1-Nano) across 374,275 evaluations on 18,714 MIMIC-IV-ED vignettes under four prompt strategies. Of 9,368 originals, 9,346 are paired with a gender-swapped counterfactual. All five models produced flip rates above a pre-registered 5% threshold (9.9% to 43.8%). Two showed directional female undertriage (DeepSeek F/M 2.15:1, Gemini 1.34:1); two were near-parity; one had high sensitivity with weak male-direction asymmetry. DeepSeek's directional bias coexisted with a low outcome-linked calibration gap (0.013 against MIMIC-IV admission), a Chouldechova-style dissociation between within-group calibration and between-pair counterfactual invariance. Demographic blinding reduced Gemini's flip rate to 0.5%; an age-preserving blind variant left DeepSeek with residual F/M 1.25, implicating age as a residual channel. Chain-of-thought prompting degraded accuracy for all five models. A two-model ablation reveals opposite underlying mechanisms for the same directional phenotype: in Gemini the signal is emergent in the combined name+gender swap, while in DeepSeek the gender token alone carries it. EQUITRIAGE shows that group parity, counterfactual invariance, and gender calibration are distinct fairness properties, that intervention effectiveness is model-dependent, and that per-model counterfactual auditing should precede clinical deployment.

</details>


### [101] [Safety and accuracy follow different scaling laws in clinical large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04039)
*Sebastian Wind,Tri-Thien Nguyen,Jeta Sopa,Mahshad Lotfinia,Sebastian Bickelhaup,Michael Uder,Harald Köstler,Gerhard Wellein,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn,Andreas Maier,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SaFE-Scale框架和RadSaFE-200基准，系统评估临床LLM在不同规模、证据质量、检索策略和推理计算条件下的安全性，发现干净证据显著提升安全指标，而标准RAG和智能体RAG未能复现该安全性能，表明临床LLM的安全性并非规模扩展的被动结果，而是由部署设计决定的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床LLM通常通过增加模型规模、上下文长度、检索复杂度或推理计算来提升性能，隐含假设是更高准确率意味着更安全的行为。然而在医学领域，少量高置信度、高风险或与证据矛盾的错误可能比平均基准性能更重要，这一假设不完整。因此需要系统评估临床LLM安全性如何随不同扩展维度变化。

Method: 提出SaFE-Scale框架，并构建RadSaFE-200基准（200道放射学多选题，含医生定义的干净证据、冲突证据以及高风险错误、不安全答案和证据矛盾的选项级标签）。在34个本地部署的LLM上，评估六种部署条件：闭卷提示（零样本）、干净证据、冲突证据、标准RAG、智能体RAG和最大上下文提示。

Result: 干净证据带来最强改进：平均准确率从73.5%提升至94.1%，高风险错误从12.0%降至2.6%，证据矛盾从12.7%降至2.3%，危险过度自信从8.0%降至1.6%。标准RAG和智能体RAG未能复现该安全性能：智能体RAG虽提升了准确率并减少了矛盾，但高风险错误和危险过度自信仍较高。最大上下文提示增加延迟但未消除安全差距，额外推理计算仅带来有限收益。最坏情况分析显示高风险错误集中在少数问题上。

Conclusion: 临床LLM的安全性并非规模扩展的被动结果，而是由证据质量、检索设计、上下文构建和集体失败行为共同塑造的部署属性。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs are often scaled by increasing model size, context length, retrieval complexity, or inference-time compute, with the implicit expectation that higher accuracy implies safer behavior. This assumption is incomplete in medicine, where a few confident, high-risk, or evidence-contradicting errors can matter more than average benchmark performance. We introduce SaFE-Scale, a framework for measuring how clinical LLM safety changes across model scale, evidence quality, retrieval strategy, context exposure, and inference-time compute. To instantiate this framework, we introduce RadSaFE-200, a Radiology Safety-Focused Evaluation benchmark of 200 multiple-choice questions with clinician-defined clean evidence, conflict evidence, and option-level labels for high-risk error, unsafe answer, and evidence contradiction. We evaluated 34 locally deployed LLMs across six deployment conditions: closed-book prompting (zero-shot), clean evidence, conflict evidence, standard RAG, agentic RAG, and max-context prompting. Clean evidence produced the strongest improvement, increasing mean accuracy from 73.5% to 94.1%, while reducing high-risk error from 12.0% to 2.6%, contradiction from 12.7% to 2.3%, and dangerous overconfidence from 8.0% to 1.6%. Standard RAG and agentic RAG did not reproduce this safety profile: agentic RAG improved accuracy over standard RAG and reduced contradiction, but high-risk error and dangerous overconfidence remained elevated. Max-context prompting increased latency without closing the safety gap, and additional inference-time compute produced only limited gains. Worst-case analysis showed that clinically consequential errors concentrated in a small subset of questions. Clinical LLM safety is therefore not a passive consequence of scaling, but a deployment property shaped by evidence quality, retrieval design, context construction, and collective failure behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [eOptShrinkQ: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression Through Optimal Spectral Denoising and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02905)
*Pei-Chun Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Transformer注意力头中的KV缓存可分解为低秩共享上下文与全秩每词残差，据此提出eOptShrinkQ两阶段压缩流水线：最优奇异值收缩提取共享结构，残差经TurboQuant量化，理论基于随机矩阵论提供自动秩选择、近零内积偏置和坐标去局域化保证。实验在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上，~2.2 bits/entry在LongBench上超越TurboQuant的3.0 bits，多针检索中接近或超越FP16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer推理中KV缓存占用大量显存，现有量化方法需处理异常值和内积偏置带来额外开销，缺乏理论指导的压缩策略。

Method: 基于尖峰随机矩阵模型，提出eOptShrinkQ两阶段流水线：1) 最优奇异值收缩(eOptShrink)自动提取低秩共享结构；2) 残差满足薄壳性质且坐标去局域化，经TurboQuant进行近最优失真的每向量标量量化。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上，每头MSE和内积保真度方面eOptShrinkQ比TurboQuant节省近1 bit/entry；LongBench上~2.2 bits/entry优于TurboQuant 3.0 bits；多针检索中2.2 bits接近或超越FP16。

Conclusion: 谱去噪通过随机矩阵理论提供了无需异常值处理和内积偏置校正的压缩框架，在极低位宽下表现优异，且谱去噪在检索密集型任务中可作为有益的正则化器。

Abstract: We show that the key-value (KV) cache in transformer attention heads admits a natural decomposition into a low-rank \emph{shared context} component and a full-rank \emph{per-token} residual, well described by the spiked random matrix model. This observation leads to eOptShrinkQ, a two-stage compression pipeline: optimal singular value shrinkage (eOptShrink) automatically extracts the shared structure, and the residual -- which satisfies the \emph{thin shell property} with delocalized coordinates -- is quantized by TurboQuant~\citep{zandieh2025turboquant}, a recently proposed per-vector scalar quantizer with near-optimal distortion guarantees. By restoring the isotropy that scalar quantization assumes, spectral denoising eliminates the need for both outlier handling and dedicated inner product bias correction, freeing those bits for improved reconstruction.
  The theoretical grounding in random matrix theory provides three guarantees: automatic rank selection via the BBP phase transition, provably near-zero inner product bias on the residual, and coordinate delocalization ensuring near-optimal quantization distortion. Experimentally, we validate eOptShrinkQ on Llama-3.1-8B and Ministral-8B across three levels: per-head MSE and inner product fidelity, where eOptShrinkQ saves nearly one bit per entry over TurboQuant at equivalent quality; end-to-end on LongBench (16 tasks), where eOptShrinkQ at $\sim$2.2 bits per entry outperforms TurboQuant at 3.0 bits; and multi-needle retrieval, where eOptShrinkQ at 2.2 bits closely matches or exceeds uncompressed FP16, suggesting that spectral denoising can act as a beneficial regularizer for retrieval-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [103] [An End-to-End Framework for Building Large Language Models for Software Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02906)
*Jingkai He,Pengfei Chen,Chenghui Wu,Shuang Liang,Ye Li,Gou Tan,Xiadao Wen,Chuanfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpsLLM是一个面向软件运维领域的专用大语言模型，通过人工在环数据筛选、监督微调和领域过程奖励模型，在问答和根因分析任务上均优于现有开源和闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究因数据质量低、知识碎片化和学习不充分，未能实现高效端到端的智能运维，因此需要探索LLM在软件运维领域的潜力。

Method: 1) 引入人工在环机制从运维原始数据中筛选高质量数据构建微调数据集；2) 基于该数据进行监督微调获得基础模型；3) 在强化学习阶段引入领域过程奖励模型(DPRM)优化根因分析任务的准确性和可靠性。

Result: 在问答任务上准确率提升0.2%~5.7%，在根因分析任务上提升2.7%~70.3%，且表现出强迁移性。将开源7B、14B、32B三个版本及15K微调数据集。

Conclusion: OpsLLM有效学习并对齐了注入的运维领域知识，在各类难度任务上均优于现有开源和闭源LLM。

Abstract: In the field of software operations, Large Language Models (LLMs) have attracted increasing attention. However, existing research has not yet achieved efficient and effective end-to-end intelligent operations due to low-quality data, fragmented knowledge and insufficient learning. To explore the potential of LLMs in software operations, we propose OpsLLM, a domain-specific LLM that supports both knowledge-based question answering (QA) and root cause analysis (RCA). Moreover, we disclose the detailed workflow for building LLMs specifically in the software operations domain. First, a Human-in-the-Loop mechanism is introduced to curate highquality data from a large collection of operational raw data and construct a fine-tuning dataset. Then, based on the data, supervised fine-tuning is conducted to achieve a base model. Furthermore, we introduce a domain process reward model (DPRM) during the reinforcement learning stage to optimize the accuracy and reliability of the fine-tuned model on RCA tasks. Experimental results on the tasks with diverse difficulties demonstrate that OpsLLMs effectively learns and aligns with the operational domain knowledge infused, outperforming existing open-source and closed-source LLMs in accuracy with improvements of 0.2%~5.7% on QA tasks and 2.7% ~70.3% on RCA tasks, while exhibiting strong transferability. Moreover, we will open-source three versions of OpsLLM with 7B, 14B and 32B parameters, along with a 15K fine-tuning dataset.

</details>


### [104] [On the Invariants of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02907)
*Wonsuk Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现并验证了Softmax注意力机制中能量场（energy field）的行中心化注意力对数在不同模型、架构和输入下具有两类不变性：机制层面的不变性（零和约束、秩界、谱特征）和模型层面的规律性（能量方差去局域化），后者源于关键矩阵的"关键非相干性"。这些不变性有实际应用，包括将能量场限制在低维子空间以及提供逐头训练监控指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力映射生成的查询-键交互概率分布虽然被广泛使用，但其底层结构尚未被充分探索。本文旨在揭示并形式化注意力机制中隐藏的结构不变性。

Method: 定义能量场为行中心化的注意力对数，通过理论推导（机制层面）和实证验证（模型层面）在不同架构、不同长度和不同输入文本上分析其不变性质。

Result: 发现两类不变性：机制层面包括每行零和约束、由头维度决定的秩界及谱特征；模型层面包括能量方差在键位置上的去局域化分布，该性质源于关键矩阵的非相干性。

Conclusion: 这些不变性具有实际意义：秩界将能量场限制在低维子空间；关键非相干性可用于逐头训练监控。所有结果在不同上下文长度和输入文本上均得到验证。

Abstract: Softmax attention maps every query--key interaction into a probability distribution, but the underlying structure remains largely unexplored. We define the \emph{energy field}, the row-centered attention logit, and show that it exhibits invariant properties across models, architectures, and inputs.
  Two classes of invariants emerge. \emph{Mechanism-level} invariants follow from the algebraic structure of softmax attention. They include a per-row zero-sum constraint, a rank bound determined by the head dimension, and spectral signatures that follow from them. \emph{Model-level} regularities are not required by the mechanism, yet hold in every autoregressive language model we test, spanning several architecture families. The energy field distributes its variance over key positions without concentrating at a few. This delocalization traces to a property of the key matrix we call \emph{key incoherence}.
  These invariants have practical consequences. The rank bound confines the energy field to a low-dimensional subspace. Key incoherence yields a per-head training monitor. All results are verified at multiple context lengths and input texts.

</details>


### [105] [Delay, Plateau, or Collapse: Evaluating the Impact of Systematic Verification Error on RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02909)
*Kazuki Egashira,Mark Vero,Jasper Dekoninck,Florian E. Dorner,Robin Staab,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，系统性验证错误（特别是假阳性）对模型性能的影响，发现其影响远大于先前认为的随机噪声，且错误模式比整体错误率更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为验证错误类似随机噪声，仅会减缓训练速度而不影响最终性能。但实际验证器（如静态代码检查器）存在系统性错误，这可能导致模型学习到不良的持续行为，需要系统研究这种系统性错误对RLVR的影响。

Method: 在算术任务上进行受控实验，分别研究系统性假阴性错误和系统性假阳性错误对RLVR训练的影响，对比其与随机噪声的效果差异。

Result: 系统性假阴性与随机噪声效果类似；而系统性假阳性可能导致从次优平台到性能崩溃的广泛行为。关键发现是，这些结果并非由整体错误率决定，而是由具体错误模式决定。

Conclusion: 与先前结论不同，现实中的验证错误会严重影响RLVR结果，验证器质量需要超越样本级错误率进行更深入的理解。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). While RLVR is designed for tasks with verifiable ground-truth answers, real-world verifiers (e.g., static code checkers) can introduce errors into the reward signal. Prior analyses have largely treated such errors as random and independent across samples, concluding that errors merely slow training with limited effect on final performance. However, practical verifiers tend to exhibit systematic errors. This introduces a risk of models learning unwanted consistent behavior from a structurally incorrect reward signal. In this work, we study the impact of such systematic verification errors on RLVR. Through controlled experiments on arithmetic tasks, we show that systematic false negatives lead to similar effects as random noise. On the other hand, systematic false positives can cause a wide range of behaviors from sub-optimal plateaus to performance collapse. Crucially, these outcomes are not determined by the overall error rate but by the specific pattern of introduced errors, making pre-hoc mitigation difficult. Our results show that, in contrast to prior conclusions, realistic verification errors can critically shape RLVR outcomes and that verifier quality has to be understood beyond its sample-level error rate.

</details>


### [106] [Generate, Filter, Control, Replay: A Comprehensive Survey of Rollout Strategies for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02913)
*Rohan Surana,Gagan Mundada,Xunyi Jiang,Chuhan Wang,Zhenwei Tang,Difan Jiao,Zihan Huang,Yuxin Xiong,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Xintong Li,Raghav Jain,Nikki Kuang,Sizhe Zhou,Bowen Jin,Zhendong Chu,Tong Yu,Ryan Rossi,Kuan-Hao Huang,Jingbo Shang,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于强化学习的后训练中rollout策略的设计，提出了GFCR生命周期分类法（生成-过滤-控制-重放），将rollout流程分解为四个模块化阶段，并辅以可靠性、覆盖率、成本敏感度三个评价标准。涵盖了数学、代码、多模态推理、工具使用智能体等场景下的方法，并提供了rollout常见问题的诊断索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL已成为提升LLM推理能力的核心后训练工具，但rollout设计（轨迹采样策略）在文献中常被低估或未充分报告，缺乏系统性的分类和分析框架。

Method: 提出GFCR分类法（Generate-Filter-Control-Replay），将rollout管线分解为四个模块化阶段，并建立可靠性、覆盖率、成本敏感度三个评价标准。在此框架下综合梳理了各类rollout方法，并通过案例研究加以验证。

Result: 建立了统一的rollout策略理论框架，涵盖RL可验证奖励、过程监督、判别器门控、引导式/树状/分段rollout、自适应计算分配、早退与部分rollout、吞吐优化、重放与重组合等多种方法，并在多个领域进行了案例验证。

Conclusion: 提供了一个将常见rollout病理映射到GFCR模块及缓解杠杆的诊断索引，并指出了构建可复现、计算高效、可信赖的rollout管线所面临的开放性挑战。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central post-training tool for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs).
