In these systems, the rollout, the trajectory sampled from a prompt to termination, including intermediate reasoning steps and optional tool or environment interactions, determines the data the optimizer learns from, yet rollout design is often underreported. This survey provides an optimizer-agnostic view of rollout strategies for RL-based post-training of reasoning LLMs. We formalize rollout pipelines with unified notation and introduce Generate-Filter-Control-Replay (GFCR), a lifecycle taxonomy that decomposes rollout pipelines into four modular stages: Generate proposes candidate trajectories and topologies; Filter constructs intermediate signals via verifiers, judges, critics; Control allocates compute and makes continuation/branching/stopping decisions under budgets; and Replay retains and reuses artifacts across rollouts without weight updates, including self-evolving curricula that autonomously generate new training tasks. We complement GFCR with a criterion taxonomy of reliability, coverage, and cost sensitivity that characterizes rollout trade-offs. Using this framework, we synthesize methods spanning RL with verifiable rewards, process supervision, judge-based gating, guided and tree/segment rollouts, adaptive compute allocation, early-exit and partial rollouts, throughput optimization, and replay/recomposition for self-improvement. We ground the framework with case studies in math, code/SQL, multimodal reasoning, tool-using agents, and agentic skill benchmarks that evaluate skill induction, reuse, and cross-task transfer. Finally, we provide a diagnostic index that maps common rollout pathologies to GFCR modules and mitigation levers, alongside open challenges for building reproducible, compute-efficient, and trustworthy rollout pipelines.

</details>


### [107] [From Synthesis to Clinical Assistance: A Strategy-Aware Agent Framework for Autism Intervention based on Real Clinical Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02916)
*Junhong Lai,Shuzhong Lai,Yanhao Yu,Wanlin Chen,Chenyu Yan,Haifeng Li,Lin Yao,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASDAgent框架通过策略感知的O-T-A-C推理循环和概率行为建模，解决了AI辅助自闭症干预中的数据稀缺、策略崩溃和数据同质性问题，生成的对话与人类治疗师策略分布高度一致（KL散度0.083），并将专业知识有效蒸馏至小语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助早期密集行为干预（EIBI）受限于数据稀缺，且通用大语言模型难以严格遵循ABA标准化程序，导致策略不一致的交互问题。

Method: 提出ASDAgent框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) DoctorAgent，采用Observe-Think-Act-Correct (O-T-A-C)推理循环，显式可控地执行ABA策略；(ii) ChildAgent，利用概率行为建模模拟多样化、非确定性的ASD反应模式，缓解数据同质性。

Result: 生成对话与人类治疗师策略分布的KL散度为0.083；在真实干预中达到近80%的策略一致性；合成数据能有效将专业知识蒸馏到小语言模型中，显著提升其治疗能力。

Conclusion: ASDAgent通过策略显式化和多样化行为建模，有效解决了AI辅助ABA干预中的数据稀缺与策略一致性问题，并为小模型的知识蒸馏提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The development of AI-assisted Early Intensive Behavioral Intervention (EIBI) for Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is severely constrained by data scarcity. Furthermore, while Applied Behavior Analysis (ABA) serves as the gold standard for clinical intervention, general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to strictly adhere to its standardized procedures, often resulting in interactions that are linguistically fluent but strategically inconsistent. To address these challenges, we introduce \textsc{ASDAgent}, a strategy-aware framework designed to unify high-fidelity intervention dialogue synthesis and clinical decision support. \textsc{ASDAgent} incorporates two specialized components to solve distinct problems: (i) a \textsc{DoctorAgent} equipped with an Observe-Think-Act-Correct (O-T-A-C) reasoning loop, which resolves the issue of strategy collapse in LLMs by making ABA execution explicit and controllable; and (ii) a \textsc{ChildAgent} that utilizes probabilistic behavior modeling to mitigate data homogeneity, simulating diverse and non-deterministic ASD response patterns. Experiments demonstrate that dialogues generated by \textsc{ASDAgent} closely mirror the strategy distribution of human therapists (KL divergence: 0.083). In real autism intervention, \textsc{ASDAgent} achieves nearly 80\% strategic consistency with human experts. Moreover, we show that synthetic data produced by \textsc{ASDAgent} effectively distills professional clinical knowledge into small language models (SLMs), significantly enhancing their therapeutic capabilities.

</details>


### [108] [Mitigating the reconstruction-detection trade-off in VAE-based unsupervised anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02918)
*Agathe Senellart,Maëlys Solal,Stéphanie Allassonnière,Ninon Burgos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了β-VAE模型中重建质量与异常检测性能之间的权衡，并研究了β调度和稀疏VAE两种缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测中变分自编码器的模型选择问题尚未解决，超参数通常仅基于正常样本的重建误差来选择，缺乏对检测性能的考量。

Method: 分析了β-VAE模型中潜在空间约束程度对重建质量和检测性能的影响，评估了随机种子带来的性能变异性，并研究了β调度和稀疏VAE两种缓解重建-检测权衡的方法。

Result: 潜在空间受限的模型获得更高的检测指标但重建质量较低；随机种子导致的性能变异与正常和异常潜在分布间的距离相关；稀疏VAE在保持高重建质量的同时改善了检测性能。

Conclusion: β调度和稀疏VAE可以有效缓解重建质量与异常检测之间的权衡问题，其中稀疏VAE在保持重建质量的前提下提升了检测效果。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders are widely used for unsupervised anomaly detection. Model selection however remains an open-question: to remain fully unsupervised, hyperparameters are often chosen to minimize the reconstruction error on normal samples. In this paper, we reveal a trade-off between reconstruction quality and anomaly detection among $β$-VAE models. Models with constrained latent space reach higher detection metrics but lower reconstruction quality. We also assess the performance variability across random seeds and show it is linked to the distance between normal and abnormal latent distributions. From this analysis, we justify and investigate two methods to mitigate the reconstructiondetection tradeoff: beta-scheduling and the Sparse VAE. The latter especially shows an improvement in detection while maintaining high reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [109] [DeRelayL: Sustainable Decentralized Relay Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02935)
*Haihan Duan,Tengfei Ma,Yuyang Qin,Runhao Zeng,Wei Cai,Victor C. M. Leung,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出去中心化中继学习（DeRelayL），一种允许无许可参与者以中继方式协作训练并共享模型的新型学习范式，旨在解决大规模模型训练中普通用户参与门槛高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模模型训练需要高昂的财务和计算资源，仅有少数科技巨头和资金充裕的机构能够承担，而普通用户（如移动用户）作为有价值数据的真正创造者，往往因这些障碍被排除在受益范围之外。现有的大规模模型访问方法要么限制用户所有权，要么缺乏可持续性。因此亟需一种协作模型训练方法，让普通用户也能训练和共享模型。

Method: 提出去中心化中继学习（DeRelayL），一种可持续学习系统，允许无许可参与者以中继方式贡献模型训练并共享模型。具体包括：设计了DeRelayL的架构和工作流程；设计了激励机制以确保系统可持续性；进行了理论分析和数值模拟以验证有效性。

Result: 通过理论分析和数值模拟，证明了DeRelayL的有效性。

Conclusion: DeRelayL作为一种新型去中心化协作学习范式，能够有效解决现有协作训练方法（如联邦学习）对普通用户参与的限制问题，为大规模模型训练的民主化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In the era of big data, large-scale machine learning models have revolutionized various fields, driving significant advancements. However, large-scale model training demands high financial and computational resources, which are only affordable by a few technological giants and well-funded institutions. In this case, common users like mobile users, the real creators of valuable data, are often excluded from fully benefiting due to the barriers, while the current methods for accessing large-scale models either limit user ownership or lack sustainability. This growing gap highlights the urgent need for a collaborative model training approach, allowing common users to train and share models. However, existing collaborative model training paradigms, especially federated learning (FL), primarily focus on data privacy and group-based model aggregation. To this end, this paper intends to address this issue by proposing a novel training paradigm named decentralized relay learning (DeRelayL), a sustainable learning system where permissionless participants can contribute to model training in a relay-like manner and share the model. In detail, this paper presents the architecture and workflow of DeRelayL, designs incentive mechanisms to ensure sustainability, and conducts theoretical analysis and numerical simulations to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [110] [Proteo-R1: Reasoning Foundation Models for De Novo Protein Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02937)
*Fang Wu,Weihao Xuan,Heli Qi,Hanqun Cao,Heng-Jui Chang,Zeqi Zhou,Haokai Zhao,Ma Jian,Carl Ma,Yu-Chi Cheng,Kuan Pang,Xiangru Tang,Zehong Wang,Guanlue Li,Hanchen Wang,Kejun Ying,Pan Lu,Chiho Im,Seungju Han,Peng Xia,Tinson Xu,Yinxi Li,Deyao Zhu,Pheng-Ann Heng,Naoto Yokoya,Masashi Sugiyama,Li Erran Li,Jure Leskovec,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proteo-R1 提出了一种将分子理解与几何生成解耦的蛋白质设计框架，通过多模态大语言模型识别关键功能残基，再将这些残基作为硬约束传递给扩散生成模型进行条件共设计，实现了可解释、可控且模块化的推理引导蛋白质设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质从头设计方法缺乏 deliberative（审慎推理）能力，设计决策与连续采样动力学纠缠在一起，导致可解释性差、可控性不足，且难以系统性地复用生化知识。

Method: 采用双专家架构：(1) 理解专家——多模态大语言模型分析序列、结构和文本上下文，识别关键功能残基；(2) 生成专家——基于扩散模型，以识别出的残基作为硬约束进行条件共设计。

Result: 实现了稳定、可解释且模块化的 LLM 推理与几何生成模型的集成，将推理操作化为显式的残基级承诺而非隐式的文本引导。

Conclusion: Proteo-R1 通过将推理过程显式地解耦为残基级决策，为蛋白质设计提供了一种更接近人类专家工作方式的范式，增强了设计的可控性和可解释性。

Abstract: Deep learning in \emph{de novo} protein design has achieved atomic-level fidelity. However, existing models remain largely non-deliberative: they directly synthesize molecular geometries without explicitly reasoning about which residues or interactions are functionally essential. As a result, design decisions are entangled with continuous sampling dynamics, limiting interpretability, controllability, and systematic reuse of biochemical knowledge. We introduce \textbf{Proteo-R1}, a reasoning-guided protein design framework that explicitly decouples \emph{molecular understanding} from \emph{geometric generation}. Proteo-R1 adopts a dual-expert architecture in which a multimodal large language model (MLLM) serves as an \emph{understanding expert}, analyzing protein sequences, structures, and textual context to identify key functional residues that govern binding and specificity. These residue-level decisions are then passed as hard constraints to a separate diffusion-based \emph{generation expert}, which performs conditional co-design while respecting the fixed interaction anchors. This factorization mirrors how human experts approach molecular engineering: first, reasoning about critical interactions, then optimizing geometry subject to those constraints. By operationalizing reasoning as explicit residue-level commitments rather than latent textual guidance, Proteo-R1 achieves stable, interpretable, and modular integration of LLM reasoning with state-of-the-art geometric generative models. Code, data, and demos are available at https://smiles724.github.io/r1/.

</details>


### [111] [From Static Analysis to Audience Dissemination: A Training-Free Multimodal Controversy Detection Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02939)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AuDisAgent，一个无需训练的多智能体框架，将多模态争议检测从静态表示学习重构为动态传播过程，模拟观众群体对视频内容的讨论与分歧，在丰富评论和冷启动场景下均显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCD方法将争议检测视为静态表示学习任务，直接从视频和评论中提取特征，未能捕捉不同观众群体对内容的多样化视角和评价，忽略了争议内容在传播过程中涌现的动态特性。

Method: 提出AuDisAgent无训练多智能体框架：1）三个筛选智能体（视频、评论、交互）分别从视觉、文本和跨模态进行初步评估；2）无法达成共识时激活观众小组智能体，模拟不同背景立场的观众讨论；3）仲裁智能体基于完整推理链做出最终判决；4）针对冷启动场景，设计评论引导策略利用语义相似历史视频的评论作为初始上下文。

Result: 在公开数据集上，所提框架在丰富评论和有限评论场景下均显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 将MCD重新定义为动态传播过程，通过多智能体系统模拟观众群体的观点分歧，有效解决了传统静态方法无法捕捉多元视角的问题，并在冷启动场景下展现了良好的适应性。

Abstract: Multimodal controversy detection (MCD) identifies controversial content in videos and their associated user comments, to support risk management for social video platforms.Prior research frames MCD as a static representation learning task, where features are directly extracted from videos and their accompanying comments. However, these methods fail to capture the diverse perspectives and evaluations from different audience groups. Inspired by the real-world process of content dissemination among audiences, we propose AuDisAgent, a training-free multi-agent framework that reformulates MCD as a dynamic propagation process.Our framework explicitly models audience dissemination through a structured multi-agent system. First, three specialized Screening Agents (Video Agent, Comment Agent, and Interaction Agent) conduct initial assessments from visual, textual, and cross-modal perspectives, respectively. For samples where the three agents cannot reach a consensus, a Viewing Panel Agent is activated to simulate post-screening discussions among audiences with diverse backgrounds and stances. This mechanism models how different audience groups interpret and react to the same content, uncovering latent controversial content that may emerge during the dissemination process. Finally, an Arbitration Agent renders the final judgment based on the complete reasoning chain from the preceding steps.In addition, to address the "cold-start" scenario where newly released videos have few or no comments, we design a Comment Bootstrapping Strategy that leverages historical public comments from semantically similar videos as the initial comment context. Extensive experiments on a public dataset demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in both rich-comment and limited-comment scenarios.

</details>


### [112] [PrismAgent: Illuminating Harm in Memes via a Zero-Shot Interpretable Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02940)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismAgent是一个零样本、多智能体、可解释的模因有害内容检测框架，通过将任务建模为刑事案件调查流程（分析-调查-起诉-判决四个阶段），利用四个专业智能体协作完成检测，在三个公开数据集上显著优于现有零样本检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模因有害内容检测方法依赖大量人工标注数据，训练成本高且泛化能力有限，亟需一种无需标注数据、可解释性强的高效检测框架。

Method: 将有害模因检测建模为刑事案件调查流程，设计四个专业智能体：1) 分析师智能体对模因进行善意和恶意两种假设下的改写以探测潜在意图；2) 调查员智能体从未标注数据集中检索支持证据，构建模因及其变体的上下文解释；3) 检察官智能体将原始模因与三种解释分别配对，进行三项独立初步判断；4) 法官智能体综合所有证据做出最终裁决。整个流程的多阶段推理链使模型具有内在可解释性。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，PrismAgent显著优于现有的零样本检测方法。

Conclusion: PrismAgent通过多智能体协作和结构化流程设计，在无需标注数据的情况下实现了对有害模因的有效检测，同时保持了模型的可解释性，为应对模因的快速传播和有害内容检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: The rapid spread of memes makes harmful content detection increasingly crucial, as effective identification can curb the circulation of misinformation. However, existing methods rely heavily on high-volume annotated data, which leads to substantial training costs and limited generalization. To address these challenges, we propose PrismAgent, a zero-shot, multi-agent, interpretable framework. PrismAgent conceptualizes this task as a criminal case investigation, employing four specialized agents responsible for the analysis, investigation, prosecution, and judgment stages within a structured collaborative workflow. In the first stage, the analyst agent paraphrases each meme under benevolent and malicious assumptions to probe its underlying intent. The investigator agent then retrieves supporting evidence from an unannotated dataset and constructs contextual interpretations for the meme and its variants. Next, the prosecutor agent performs three independent preliminary judgments by pairing the original meme with each of the three interpretations. Finally, the judge agent deliberates across all evidence to render a final verdict. Moreover, PrismAgent's explicit multi-stage reasoning chain makes the model inherently interpretable, as every intermediate step is explicitly explained rather than only producing a final detection result. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that PrismAgent significantly outperforms existing zero-shot detection methods.

</details>


### [113] [A Framework for Exploring and Disentangling Intersectional Bias: A Case Study in Fetal Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02942)
*Aya Elgebaly,Joris Fournel,Benjamin Laine Jønch Jurgensen,Kamil Mikolaj,Anders Christensen,Martin Tolsgaard,Claes Ladefoged,Aasa Feragen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结构化的交叉性偏差检测框架，通过对94,000+张胎儿超声图像的分析，发现像素间距（PS）是驱动模型性能差异的关键因素，其影响部分由孕龄（GA）解释，但在不同BMI层级中持续存在，强调了在医学AI公平性研究中考虑采集条件和交互效应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI偏差研究过度聚焦于数据表征不平衡，忽视了图像质量、采集条件和操作者经验等与人口统计学特征相关的因素对性能差异的影响，缺乏系统化的交叉性偏差检测方法。

Method: 提出结构化框架，结合无监督切片发现（unsupervised slice discovery）、系统化因素分析（factor-wise analysis）和靶向交叉性评估（targeted intersectional evaluation），在94,000+张胎儿超声图像上分析深度学习模型和临床Hadlock标准。

Result: 像素间距（PS）是性能差异的一致驱动因素，较高PS在选定子群中带来高达24%的改进；PS效应部分由GA解释，但在不同BMI层级中持续存在，提示存在混杂效应。

Conclusion: 医学AI公平性研究需要纳入采集感知（acquisition-aware）和交互感知（interaction-aware）的评估方法，以揭示跨人口统计、临床和采集因素的交叉性偏差。

Abstract: Bias in medical AI is often framed as a problem of representation. However, in image-based tasks such as fetal ultrasound, performance disparities can arise even when representation is adequate, because predictive accuracy depends strongly on image quality. Image quality is shaped by acquisition conditions and operator expertise, as well as patient-dependent factors such as maternal body mass index (BMI), all of which may correlate with sensitive demographic features. Consequently, observed disparities may reflect the combined influence of demographic, clinical, and acquisition-related factors rather than data imbalance alone, and may obscure underlying interaction or confounding effects. We propose a structured framework to explore and detect intersectional bias, combining unsupervised slice discovery, systematic factor-wise analysis, and targeted intersectional evaluation. In a case study of over 94{,}000 ultrasound images for fetal weight estimation, we analyze bias in a state-of-the-art deep learning (DL) model and the clinical standard Hadlock, a regression formula using biometric measurements. Pixel spacing (PS) -- a parameter considered suboptimal in current acquisition protocols -- emerged as a consistent driver of performance differences, with higher PS associated with improvements of up to 24\% in selected subgroups for both models. Because PS is often adapted in cases of high BMI or low gestational age (GA), this effect carries a substantial risk of confounding. Our intersectional analysis revealed that part of the PS-associated signal is explained by GA, while PS-related improvements persist across BMI strata, highlighting the importance of acquisition-aware and interaction-aware evaluation in medical AI fairness research.

</details>


### [114] [Healthcare AI GYM for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02943)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TT-OPD框架解决医疗AI多轮强化学习中响应长度爆炸和工具使用频率下降问题，在18个基准测试中10个达到最佳性能，平均提升3.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床推理需要多轮交互，但现有强化学习环境缺乏统一的、覆盖广泛临床领域和专用工具的框架来训练可泛化的医疗AI智能体；且多轮强化学习存在训练不稳定、响应长度爆炸、工具使用频率下降等问题。

Method: 提出Gymnasium兼容的医疗AI训练环境（覆盖10个临床领域、3.6K+任务、135个领域专用工具、828K医疗知识库）；提出Turn-level Truncated On-Policy Distillation (TT-OPD)自蒸馏框架，利用无梯度EMA教师模型在每个对话轮次提供密集的、基于结果感知的KL正则化。

Result: TT-OPD在18个基准测试中10个达到最佳性能，平均提升3.9个百分点；收敛更快、响应长度受控、多轮工具使用持续；vanilla GRPO虽在部分基准上准确率高但存在训练不稳定、长度振荡和收敛周期长的问题。

Conclusion: TT-OPD通过在每个对话轮次提供密集结果感知的KL正则化，有效解决了多轮RL训练中的稀疏奖励与临床轨迹序列的错配问题，提升了训练效率和稳定性。

Abstract: Clinical reasoning demands multi-step interactions -- gathering patient history, ordering tests, interpreting results, and making safe treatment decisions -- yet a unified training environment provides the breadth of clinical domains and specialized tools to train generalizable medical AI agents through reinforcement learning remains elusive. We present a comprehensive empirical study of multi-turn agentic RL for medical AI, built on \gym{}, a gymnasium-compatible environment spanning 10 clinical domains with 3.6K+ tasks, 135 domain-specific tools, and a knowledge base of 828K medical passages. Our analysis reveals that agentic multi-turn structure degrades into verbose single-turn monologues, characterized by monotonic length explosion and a simultaneous erosion of tool-use frequency. We characterize how this collapse, alongside distillation instability, stems from the misalignment of sparse terminal rewards with sequential clinical trajectories. We find that vanilla GRPO achieves strong final accuracy on some benchmarks but suffers from training instability, evidenced by significant oscillations in response length and prolonged convergence periods. To improve training efficiency and stability, we propose Turn-level Truncated On-Policy Distillation (TT-OPD), a self-distillation framework where a gradient-free EMA teacher leverages outcome-privileged information to provide dense, outcome-aware KL regularization at every conversation turn. TT-OPD achieves the best performance on 10 of 18 benchmarks with an average +3.9~pp improvement over the non-RL baseline with faster early convergence, controlled response length, and sustained multi-turn tool use.

</details>


### [115] [Exploring Pass-Rate Reward in Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02944)
*Xin-Ye Li,Ren-Biao Liu,Yun-Ji Zhang,Hui Sun,Zheng Xie,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在无评论家强化学习（如GRPO和RLOO）中，测试用例通过率奖励虽然缓解了奖励稀疏性问题，但并未比二元奖励更可靠地提升代码生成性能。原因在于通过率奖励诱导的梯度更新不能一致地将概率质量移向完全通过的解决方案，且部分通过的解决方案在同一组内会产生相互抵消的冲突梯度方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RL从单元测试反馈中训练LLM时，全测试通过的二值奖励在难题上稀疏，无法提供学习信号。常用缓解措施是使用测试用例通过率作为替代奖励，但该方法的有效性尚不清楚。

Method: 在GRPO和RLOO等无评论家RL算法中，系统对比了通过率奖励与二元奖励在代码生成任务上的表现，并分析了奖励密度、梯度方向等优化动力学特性。

Result: 通过率奖励虽密度更高，但诱导的梯度更新不能一致地将概率质量移向完全正确的解；同一组内的部分通过解会产生冲突的梯度方向相互抵消；通过率奖励在受控实验中未能稳定优于二元奖励。

Conclusion: 在无评论家RL中，通过率奖励不足以改善代码生成，需要设计更好的奖励机制以更好地对齐优化目标与完全正确性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) from unit-test feedback has become a standard post-training recipe for improving large language models (LLMs) on code generation. However, the pass-all-tests binary reward can be sparse, yielding no learning signal on challenging problems where none of the sampled solutions passes all tests. A common remedy is to use the test-case pass rate as a surrogate reward.
  In this work, we study pass-rate rewards in critic-free RL for code generation (e.g., GRPO and RLOO) and report a consistent pattern across base models and algorithms: despite alleviating reward sparsity, pass-rate rewards do not reliably improve final performance over binary rewards in rigorous controlled experiments.
  To understand this discrepancy, we analyze reward density and the resulting gradient directions. We find that pass-rate rewards are denser, but the induced gradient updates do not consistently move probability mass toward full-pass solutions. This arises because test-case pass rate is a miscalibrated surrogate for progress toward full correctness, and partial-pass solutions within the same group can induce conflicting gradient directions that cancel out.
  Overall, our results suggest that, in critic-free RL, pass-rate rewards are insufficient to improve code generation and motivate reward designs that better align optimization with the goal of full correctness.

</details>


### [116] [RouteHijack: Routing-Aware Attack on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02946)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RouteHijack是一种针对MoE架构大语言模型的路由感知越狱攻击方法。它首先通过响应驱动的专家定位识别安全关键专家和有害专家，然后构建对抗性后缀，在推理时抑制安全专家、激活有害专家并防止早期拒绝。在7个MoE LLM上平均攻击成功率69.3%，超越先前优化攻击3.2倍，并能零样本迁移到5个MoE变体和3个MoE视觉语言模型，揭示了稀疏专家结构的根本性安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性攻击存在明显局限性：基于提示的越狱依赖启发式搜索且迁移性差；模型干预方法需要内部表示的特权访问；基于优化的输入攻击受限于MoE模型不可微路由机制而效果不佳。因此需要一种针对MoE架构特点、仅需输入访问权限的高效越狱方法。

Method: RouteHijack包含两个关键步骤：(1) 响应驱动的专家定位——通过对比安全拒绝和有害完成下的激活状态，定位安全关键专家和有害专家；(2) 构建路由感知的对抗性后缀——优化目标包括抑制安全专家、激活有害专家并防止生成早期阶段的拒绝行为。推理时将优化后的后缀附加到恶意提示后，仅需输入级访问。

Result: 在7个MoE LLM上平均攻击成功率(ASR)达69.3%，是先前优化攻击的3.2倍。零样本迁移到5个同族MoE变体，ASR从27.7%提升至61.2%；泛化到3个MoE视觉语言模型，ASR从2.47%提升至38.7%。

Conclusion: RouteHijack暴露了稀疏专家架构的根本性安全漏洞，表明MoE模型中安全行为集中在少数专家上，通过影响路由决策即可操控模型行为。现有仅关注输出级别的对齐防御不足以应对此类攻击，亟需面向路由机制的新型防御策略。

Abstract: Safety alignment is critical for the responsible deployment of large language models (LLMs). As Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly adopted to scale model capacity, understanding their safety robustness becomes essential. Existing adversarial attacks, however, have notable limitations. Prompt-based jailbreaks rely on heuristic search and transfer poorly, model intervention methods require privileged access to internal representations, and optimization-based input attacks remain output-centric and are fundamentally limited to MoE models due to the non-differentiable routing mechanism.
  In this paper, we present RouteHijack, a routing-aware jailbreak for MoE LLMs. Our key insight is that safety behavior is concentrated in a small subset of experts, creating an opportunity to steer model behavior by influencing routing decisions through input optimization. Building on this observation, RouteHijack first performs response-driven expert localization to identify safety-critical and harmful experts by contrasting activations under safe refusals and harmful completions. It then constructs adversarial suffixes with a routing-aware objective that suppresses safety experts, promotes harmful experts, and prevents early-stage refusal during generation. At inference time, the optimized suffix is appended to a malicious prompt, requiring only input access. Across seven MoE LLMs, RouteHijack achieves a 69.3\% average attack success rate (ASR), outperforming prior optimization-based attack by $3.2\times$. RouteHijack also transfers zero-shot across five sibling MoE variants, raising average ASR from 27.7\% to 61.2\%, and further generalizes to three MoE-based VLMs, increasing average ASR from 2.47\% to 38.7\%. These findings expose a fundamental vulnerability in sparse expert architectures and highlight the need for defenses beyond output-level alignment.

</details>


### [117] [AsymK-Talker: Real-Time and Long-Horizon Talking Head Generation via Asymmetric Kernel Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02948)
*Yuxin Lu,Qian Qiao,Jiayang Sun,Min Cao,Guibo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AsymK-Talker是一种基于扩散蒸馏的实时长时序说话人视频生成方法，通过KCLG因果分块生成、TRE时域参考编码和AKD非对称核蒸馏三大组件，解决了现有方法在实时推理、时序一致性和长程漂移方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在音频驱动说话人生成中面临三个关键限制：因果推理低效导致无法实时推理、与时序连贯条件不兼容、长时序生成中出现渐进式漂移，这些问题共同阻碍了其在实时应用中的部署。

Method: 提出AsymK-Talker，包含三个核心组件：(1) 核条件循环生成(KCLG)——基于因果分块生成范式，利用运动核实现时序一致传播；(2) 时域参考编码(TRE)——将静态身份参考转化为时域感知的潜在表示以增强音画同步；(3) 非对称核蒸馏(AKD)——教师模型基于真实运动核进行监督，学生模型学习从生成核进行生成，确保长序列生成的鲁棒性。

Result: 在视觉保真度和唇形同步指标上均取得了有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: AsymK-Talker通过扩散蒸馏和因果分块策略有效实现了实时、长时序的说话人视频生成，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have markedly enhanced the visual fidelity of audio-driven talking head generation. Nevertheless, existing methods are constrained by three critical limitations: causal inefficiency that impedes real-time inference, incompatibility with temporally coherent conditioning, and progressive drift over long-horizon generation, collectively hindering their deployment in real-time applications. To overcome these challenges, we introduce AsymK-Talker, a novel diffusion-distillation method designed for real-time and long-horizon talking head generation. AsymK-Talker comprises three key components: (1) Kernel-Conditioned Loop Generation (KCLG), a causal, chunk-wise generation paradigm that leverages motion kernels to enable temporally consistent propagation; (2) Temporal Reference Encoding (TRE), which converts a static identity reference into a time-aware latent representation to enhance audio-visual synchronization; and (3) Asymmetric Kernel Distillation (AKD), a teacher-student distillation framework wherein the teacher model conditions on ground-truth motion kernels for supervision, while the student learns to generate from generated kernels, thereby ensuring robustness during extended generation sequences. AsymK-Talker achieves promising results on both visual fidelity and lip synchronization metrics.

</details>


### [118] [Disease Is a Spectral Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02949)
*John D. Mayfield,Matthew S. Rosen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"生物标志物哈密顿量"的疾病转化理解方法，通过建模健康与疾病状态的协方差矩阵，利用谱分解的扰动分析实现分子层面和个体层面的疾病轨迹机理解释，并将患者投影到疾病判别本征模上作为预后统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对疾病从健康基线转化过程的生物标志物级可解释性，难以同时实现分子层面的机制解释和个体患者的精准预后。

Method: 定义生物标志物哈密顿量 H = X^T X / n，其本征向量定义正常模式，本征值量化模式能量。健康状态由参考哈密顿量 H_0 描述，疾病扰动通过加法算子 ΔH 作用，导致本征值偏移和本征向量旋转。将新患者投影到疾病判别本征模上得到预后统计量。

Result: 推导了疾病扰动下的谱变化公式，并证明患者协方差结构在疾病判别本征模上的投影可作为最优预后统计量，实现更精准的疾病预后。

Conclusion: 该框架具有广泛适用性，可应用于从癌症到神经退行性疾病等多种疾病领域，为疾病转化理解提供了统一的数学形式体系。

Abstract: We propose a novel method of understanding disease transformation from a healthy baseline with biomarker-level explainability. By modeling the biomarker covariance matrices of healthy controls and disease states, the perturbation can be individually characterized to accomplish mechanistic explanations of disease trajectories, both at a molecular level and for individual patients. Given a cohort of n patients each measured on p biomarkers, we define the biomarker "Hamiltonian" H = X^T X / n \in R^{p \times p}, where X \in R^{n \times p} is the covariant biomarker matrix. The eigenvectors of H define a set of normal modes of biomarker coordination, and the eigenvalues quantify the energy carried by each mode. In the healthy state, the reference Hamiltonian H_0 governs this structure where disease perturbs H_0 by an additive operator ΔH, thus shifting eigenvalues and rotating eigenvectors in proportion to the severity of pathological disruption. We formalize this framework, derive the spectral change given a disease perturbation, and demonstrate that the projection of a newly diagnosed patient's cumulative biomarker covariance structure onto disease-discriminant eigenmodes constitutes an optimal prognostic statistic for greater precision in disease prognosis. This work serves as a veritable white paper with application across a panoply of disease frameworks from cancer to neurodegenerative disorders.

</details>


### [119] [Calibration of the underlying surface parameters for urban flood using latent variables and adjoint equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02959)
*Yongfu Tian,Shan Ding,Guofeng Su,Jianguo Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于贝叶斯框架和最大似然原理的城市下垫面参数校准方法，通过引入机器学习中的潜变量表达不确定性，并构建伴随方程获取梯度信息，结合参数共享和局域化技术降低计算复杂度。简单案例验证方法快速收敛且对观测时间间隔不敏感，在Test 8A案例中校准城市道路曼宁系数，最大相对误差13.88%，最小1.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准城市下垫面参数对城市洪水模拟至关重要，现有方法在不确定性和计算效率方面存在不足。

Method: 将参数校准问题形式化为贝叶斯框架下的优化问题，采用最大似然原理；以城市洪水动力系统模型作为替代模型，引入机器学习中的潜变量表达更多不确定性；构建伴随方程获取梯度信息，提出参数共享技术和局域化技术降低伴随方程的计算复杂度。

Result: 简单案例验证方法快速收敛且对观测时间间隔不敏感；在Test 8A案例中校准城市道路曼宁系数，最大相对误差13.88%，最小1.16%。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效校准城市下垫面参数，具有收敛快、对观测时间间隔不敏感的优点，为城市洪水模拟的参数校准提供了新思路。

Abstract: Calibrating the urban underlying surface parameters is crucial for urban flood simulation. We formulate the parameter calibration problem into an optimization problem within the Bayesian framework using the maximum likelihood principle. We adopt the urban flood dynamical system model as the surrogate model and innovatively introduce latent variables inspired by machine learning to represent more uncertainties, which can also be compatible with common physical parameter calibration. For more efficient optimization, we construct the adjoint equation of the surrogate model to obtain gradient information and propose the parameter sharing technique and the localization technique to reduce the computation complexity of the adjoint equation. A simple case verifies the proposed method can converge quickly and is insensitive to the observation time interval. In the case derived from Test 8A, we calibrate Manning's coefficient of urban roads, with a maximum relative error of 13.88% and a minimum of 1.16%.

</details>


### [120] [ZeRO-Prefill: Zero Redundancy Overheads in MoE Prefill Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02960)
*Zhaoyuan Su,Olatunji Ruwase,Karthik Ganesan,Aurick Qiao,Samyam Rajbhandari,Juncheng Yang,Yue Cheng,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE模型在纯预填充（无自回归解码）场景下的服务效率问题，提出ZeRO-Prefill系统。其核心思想是用异步权重AllGather替代传统的激活AllToAll，通过在前向计算过程中并行流式传输专家权重，消除分布式执行中的通信和同步开销，结合前缀感知路由和真实FLOPs负载跟踪，显著提升吞吐量和模型FLOPs利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型在服务判别型任务（纯预填充工作负载）时，由于专家放置与同步激活路由的耦合设计（继承自解码时代），导致张量并行、专家并行和流水线并行等策略引入冗余计算、通信和同步开销，严重降低了预填充服务效率。瓶颈不在于计算能力，而在于分布式执行本身。

Method: 提出ZeRO-Prefill系统，其后端AsyncEP（异步专家并行）通过权重而非激活来路由专家——在预填充的逐层大batch前向计算窗口内，异步执行专家权重的AllGather操作，使其完全与计算重叠，从而替代原有的激活AllToAll通信。前端通过前缀感知路由和真实FLOPs负载跟踪，共同强制执行由物理特性导出的饱和阈值。

Result: 在Qwen3-235B-A22B模型上，跨越四种硬件/精度配置，ZeRO-Prefill在实际工作负载上相比最强分布式基线实现1.35-1.37倍吞吐量提升，在长上下文合成工作负载上最高达1.59倍，持续保持29.8-36.2%的每GPU模型FLOPs利用率。

Conclusion: 通过解耦专家放置与同步激活路由，利用预填充阶段计算密集的特性，将通信模式从激活AllToAll转为异步权重AllGather，能够显著提升MoE模型在纯预填充服务场景的效率。

Abstract: Production LLM workloads increasingly serve discriminative tasks, such as classification, recommendation, and verification, whose answers are read from the logits of a single prefill pass with no autoregressive decoding. Serving these prefill-only workloads on mixture-of-experts (MoE) models is bottlenecked not by compute but by the distributed execution required to fit the model: existing parallel strategies (tensor, expert, and pipeline parallelism) trade memory pressure for redundant computation, communication, and synchronization, severely degrading MoE prefill serving efficiency. We observe that these overheads stem from coupling expert placement with synchronous activation routing -- a design inherited from the decoding era. The long, compute-bound forward passes of large-batch prefill open a per-layer window wide enough to stream expert weights in the background, replacing per-layer activation AllToAll with asynchronous weight AllGather fully overlapped with computation. We propose ZeRO-Prefill, a prefill-only serving system whose backend, AsyncEP (Asynchronous Expert Parallelism), gathers experts by weight rather than routing them by activation, and whose frontend co-enforces a physically-derived saturation threshold through prefix-aware routing and true-FLOPs load tracking. On Qwen3-235B-A22B across four hardware/precision configurations, ZeRO-Prefill delivers 1.35-1.37x throughput over the strongest distributed baseline on real-world workloads and up to 1.59x on long-context synthetic workloads, sustaining 29.8-36.2% per-GPU model FLOPs utilization.

</details>


### [121] [ISAAC: Auditing Causal Reasoning in Deep Models for Drug-Target Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02962)
*Barbara Tarantino,Sun Kim,Yijingxiu Lu,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ISAAC框架，通过机制性扰动与虚假性扰动对冻结的DTI预测模型进行后验结构审计，揭示在AUROC相近（~3%差异）的模型中，推理分数存在约25%的相对差异，而这些差异无法被传统准确率指标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习DTI预测模型常依赖非机制性的分子特征取得良好基准性能，但标准准确率指标无法检测这种局限性，亟需一种独立于预测精度的结构审计方法。

Method: ISAAC框架通过匹配的机制性扰动与虚假性扰动对冻结模型的输入层进行干预，基于因果推理评估模型对先验相关结构的敏感性，不依赖预测准确率。

Result: 在Davis基准上对三种基于序列的DTI架构进行审计，发现AUROC仅相差约3%的模型，其推理分数相对差异达约25%，且该结果在训练种子、干预种子和两种扰动算子下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标无法检测模型在机制性推理上的差异，后验结构审计应作为科学机器学习中分子建模性能评估的补充手段。

Abstract: Deep learning models for drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction often achieve strong benchmark performance without necessarily relying on mechanistically meaningful molecular features, a limitation that standard accuracy-based evaluation cannot detect. We introduce ISAAC (Intervention-based Structural Auditing Approach for Causal Reasoning), a post-hoc framework that evaluates prior-relative structural sensitivity by probing frozen models through matched mechanistic and spurious input-level interventions, independently of predictive accuracy. Applied to three sequence-based DTI architectures on the Davis benchmark, ISAAC reveals approximately 25\% relative differences in reasoning scores across models with comparable AUROC (within around 3\%), stable across training and intervention seeds and two distinct perturbation operators. These discrepancies, undetectable under conventional accuracy metrics, motivate the use of post-hoc structural auditing as a complement to standard performance evaluation in scientific machine learning for molecular modeling.

</details>


### [122] [Joint Energy Management and Coordinated AIGC Workload Scheduling for Distributed Data Centers: A Diffusion-Aided Reward Shaping Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02965)
*Yang Fu,Peng Qin,Liming Chen,Zihao Zhang,Hao Yu,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向AIGC数据中心的联合能源管理与协同工作负载调度框架，通过显式服务质量建模、多步去噪的扩散模型奖励塑形方法，解决了模型异构性、隐性质量评估及调度决策稀疏奖励下的深度强化学习效率问题，在真实数据上实现了优于基准方法的系统效用与收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC服务在云数据中心产生快速增长的计算负载，服务提供商需在保证内容生成质量的同时降低能源成本。现有方法面临三个关键挑战：跨提供商的模型异构性、服务质量的隐性评估困难、复杂的推理过程控制，亟需新的调度框架来应对这些问题。

Method: 提出联合能源管理与协同AIGC工作负载调度框架，包含：(1) 显式数学化服务质量表征，支持作业跨提供商转移与细粒度推理配置；(2) 联合考虑数据中心多种能源资源以增强用电灵活性；(3) 构建系统效用最大化问题；(4) 设计扩散模型辅助的奖励塑形方法，通过多步去噪生成互补奖励信号，与深度强化学习结合以处理稀疏奖励下的调度策略学习。

Result: 基于真实模型和数据的实验表明，所提方案能够有效适应电价波动与AIGC模型异构性，在学习收敛速度和系统效用方面均优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的扩散模型奖励塑形与DRL相结合的调度框架，有效解决了AIGC数据中心中异构模型调度与稀疏奖励下的策略学习难题，为实现节能高效的内容生成服务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence-generated content (AIGC) has emerged as a transformative paradigm for automating the creation of diverse and customized content, giving rise to rapidly growing computational workloads in cloud data centers. It is imperative for AIGC service providers (ASPs) to strategically schedule AIGC workloads to reduce data center energy costs while guaranteeing high-quality content generation. However, the distinctive characteristics of AIGC services pose critical challenges, including model heterogeneity across ASPs, implicit service quality evaluation, and complex inference process control. To tackle these challenges, we propose a joint energy management and coordinated AIGC workload scheduling framework, which introduces an explicit mathematical characterization of service quality to promote both job transfer among ASPs and fine-grained inference process configuration. Moreover, various energy resources within data centers are jointly considered to enhance power usage flexibility. Subsequently, a system utility maximization problem is formulated to balance AIGC service revenue with operational penalties and costs. Nevertheless, the strong coupling among job scheduling decisions induces severe reward sparsity, which limits the effectiveness of existing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms. To address this issue, we develop a diffusion model-aided reward shaping approach to synthesize complementary reward signals through a multi-step denoising process. This approach is seamlessly integrated with DRL to enable efficient learning of scheduling policies under sparse environmental feedback. Experiments based on real-world models and datasets demonstrate that our scheme effectively accommodates electricity price fluctuations and AIGC model heterogeneity, while achieving superior learning convergence and system utility compared with benchmark methods.

</details>


### [123] [AutoRAGTuner: A Declarative Framework for Automatic Optimization of RAG Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02967)
*Xintan Zeng,Yongchao Liu,Yice Luo,Jiajun Zhen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoRAGTuner 是一个声明式、配置驱动的框架，能够自动化 RAG 生命周期（构建、执行、评估、优化），通过模块化架构、域-元素模型（DEM）和自适应贝叶斯优化引擎，在多种 RAG 流水线上优于默认基线，并将代码变更量减少高达 95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 虽能增强 LLM，但其性能对复杂架构设计和超参数配置高度敏感，当前依赖低效的人工调优，缺乏自动化和系统化的优化方案。

Method: 提出 AutoRAGTuner 框架，包含：(1) 模块化架构与组件注册机制以解耦流水线阶段；(2) 域-元素模型（DEM）将异构数据表示为带有双向指针的原子元素，支持节点、边和超边；(3) 自适应贝叶斯优化引擎用于端到端超参数调优。

Result: 在从常规到基于图的多种 RAG 流水线上，AutoRAGTuner 均优于默认基线；其声明式配置语言使架构调整的代码变更量减少高达 95%。

Conclusion: AutoRAGTuner 为构建可演化、可复用的 RAG 系统提供了系统化可优化的基础，显著降低了工程开销。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances LLMs, but performance is highly sensitive to complex architecture designs and hyper-parameter configurations, which currently rely on inefficient manual tuning. We present AutoRAGTuner, a declarative, configuration-driven framework that automates the RAG life cycle: construction, execution,evaluation, and optimization. AutoRAGTuner employs a modular architecture to decouple pipeline stages through a component registration mechanism. To unify heterogeneous data, we introduce the Domain-Element Model (DEM), representing objects as atomic elements with bidirectional pointers to support nodes, edges, and hyperedges. Furthermore, AutoRAGTuner integrates an adaptive Bayesian optimization engine for end-to-end hyper-parameter tuning. Experimental results demonstrate AutoRAGTuner's architectural generality: across diverse RAG pipelines, ranging from vanilla to graph-based, the framework consistently outperforms default baselines. Notably, AutoRAGTuner significantly mitigates engineering overhead, where its declarative configuration language enables a up to 95\% reduction in code churn for architectural adjustments. Overall, AutoRAGTuner provides a systematically optimizable foundation for building evolvable and reusable RAG systems.

</details>


### [124] [Finite-Size Gradient Transport in Large Language Model Pretraining: From Cascade Size to Intensive Transport Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02968)
*Ping Wang,Yan-Qi Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于五个可观测量的有限大小梯度传输框架，用于分析语言模型训练。通过在Pico-LM和Pythia两个模型系列上分析原始梯度数据，发现两者共享近统一的级联大小骨架但处于不同传输机制，且性能关联集中在特定通道上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型训练中缺乏一个统一的、可复用的梯度传输测量框架，难以量化分析不同规模、不同模型系列之间的梯度动态差异及其与训练性能的关系。

Method: 提出了基于五个可观测量(D,z,β,δ,v_rel)的有限大小梯度传输框架，分别捕获级联大小、持续时间、绝对传输和密集传输效率。在Pico-LM（4个规模，125个对齐步）和Pythia（5个规模，153个对齐检查点差分更新场）两个模型系列上分析原始梯度测量数据，并使用随机场控制实验验证结果。

Result: 两个模型系列共享近统一的级联大小骨架，但处于不同传输机制：Pico-LM呈现正持续时间缩放和负密集效率缩放，Pythia接近D=1基线且仅有弱正效率尺度依赖。随机场控制实验给出接近匹配的零基线，表明差异源于真实偏离而非不同零校准。性能关联是通道级别的，主要由v_rel和归一化级联持续时间承载。

Conclusion: 该梯度传输测量框架是可复用的，能够有效区分不同模型系列的传输机制差异，但未声称存在通用固定点或神经缩放定律的第一性原理推导。

Abstract: We introduce a finite-size gradient-transport framework for real language-model training, based on five observables $(D,z,β,δ,v_{\mathrm{rel}})$ that separate cascade size, duration, absolute transport, and intensive transport efficiency. We analyze direct raw-gradient measurements from Pico-LM across four scales and 125 aligned steps, together with a five-scale Pythia companion dataset built from 153 aligned checkpoint-difference update fields. The same algebraic closure holds in both families, and both share a near-unity cascade-size backbone, but they occupy distinct transport regimes: Pico-LM shows positive duration scaling and negative intensive-efficiency scaling, whereas Pythia remains near the $D=1$ baseline with only weak positive efficiency scale dependence. Randomized-field controls give nearly matched null floors in the intensive and duration channels, indicating that the contrast reflects different real departures from a shared null skeleton rather than different null calibrations. The families also differ in stepwise power-law compressibility: Pico-LM retains clean duration and efficiency power laws, whereas Pythia preserves the size backbone but shows weaker one-slope compressibility in those channels. External performance associations are correspondingly channel-level, carried mainly by $v_{\mathrm{rel}}$ and normalized cascade duration, while $D(t)$ acts as a shared size backbone without a significant exponent-level performance association. These results support a reusable transport measurement framework without claiming a universal fixed point or a first-principles derivation of neural scaling laws.

</details>


### [125] [Multilingual Safety Alignment via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02971)
*Ruiyang Qin,Qingzhuo Wang,Dongrui Liu,Qiang Li,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多语言自蒸馏（MSD）框架，通过跨语言安全迁移将高资源语言的安全能力转移至低资源语言，并引入双视角安全加权（DPSW）优化蒸馏目标，在无需目标语言响应数据的情况下显著提升多语言安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多语言环境下存在严重的安全对齐问题：高资源语言有强安全防护，但低资源语言极易受到越狱攻击。现有安全对齐方法依赖各目标语言的高质量响应数据，成本高昂且生成困难。

Method: 提出多语言自蒸馏（MSD）框架，包括on-policy MSD和off-policy MSD两种具体方法，仅需多语言查询即可实现跨语言安全迁移。进一步提出双视角安全加权（DPSW），联合考虑教师和学生模型的视角，自适应增加安全关键token的惩罚权重并降低非关键token的权重。

Result: 在多种代表性LLM和多种多语言越狱及实用基准测试中，方法始终实现优越的多语言安全性能，能有效泛化到更具挑战性的数据集和未见过的语言，同时保持模型的一般能力。

Conclusion: MSD框架通过跨语言自蒸馏成功将高资源语言的安全能力迁移至低资源语言，无需任何语言的响应数据，DPSW进一步优化了蒸馏过程，为多语言安全对齐提供了高效且经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit severe multilingual safety misalignment: they possess strong safeguards in high-resource languages but remain highly vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in low-resource languages. Current safety alignment methods generally rely on high-quality response data for each target language, which is expensive and difficult to generate. In this paper, we propose a cross-lingual safeguard transfer framework named Multilingual Self-Distillation (MSD). This framework transfers an LLM's inherent safety capabilities from high-resource (e.g., English) to low-resource (e.g., Javanese) languages, overcoming the need for response data in any language. Our framework is flexible and can be integrated with different self-distillation strategies. Specifically, we implement two concrete methods -- on-policy MSD and off-policy MSD -- both of which enable effective cross-lingual safety transfer using only multilingual queries. Furthermore, we propose Dual-Perspective Safety Weighting (DPSW), a divergence measure to optimize the distillation objective. By jointly considering the perspectives of both the teacher and the student, DPSW adaptively increases the penalty weights on safety-critical tokens while reducing the weights on non-critical tokens. Extensive experiments on representative LLMs across diverse multilingual jailbreak and utility benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently achieves superior multilingual safety performance. Notably, it generalizes effectively to more challenging datasets and unseen languages while preserving the model's general capabilities.

</details>


### [126] [Structured Diffusion Bridges: Inductive Bias for Denoising Diffusion Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02973)
*Eitan Kosman,Gabriele Serussi,Chaim Basking*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种扩散桥框架，通过对齐约束限制解空间，在无需完全配对数据的情况下实现模态翻译，并在未配对、半配对和配对场景下均表现出一致性能，特别是仅需少量配对即可达到接近完全配对的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模态翻译任务本质上是欠约束的，多种交叉模态映射可能产生相同的边缘分布。现有扩散桥方法依赖完全配对数据集，施加了单一的数据驱动约束，这限制了模型的灵活性和适用性。

Method: 提出扩散桥框架，通过对齐约束来表征和限制 admissible 解空间，将配对监督视为可选的启发式手段而非必要条件，从而在未配对、半配对和完全配对三种设定下均可工作。

Result: 在合成数据和真实模态翻译基准上，该方法在未配对、半配对和完全配对场景下均表现出一致的性能。特别地，在配对要求大幅放宽的情况下达到了接近完全配对的质量，且在完全未配对场景中仍然适用。

Conclusion: 扩散桥作为模态翻译的灵活基础框架，可以超越完全配对数据的限制，为实际应用中数据配对成本高昂的场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Modality translation is inherently under-constrained, as multiple cross-modal mappings may yield the same marginals. Recent work has shown that diffusion bridges are effective for this task. However, most existing approaches rely on fully paired datasets, thereby imposing a single data-driven constraint. We propose a diffusion-bridge framework that characterizes the space of admissible solutions and restricts it via alignment constraints, treating paired supervision as an optional heuristic rather than a prerequisite. We validate our method on synthetic and real modality translation benchmarks across unpaired, semi-paired, and paired regimes, showing consistent performance across supervision levels. Notably, \textbf{it achieves near fully-paired quality with a substantial relaxation in pairing requirements, and remaining applicable in the unpaired regime}. These results highlight diffusion bridges as a flexible foundation for modality translation beyond fully paired data.

</details>


### [127] [Mixed-Precision Information Bottlenecks for On-Device Trait-State Disentanglement in Bipolar Agitation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03039)
*Joydeep Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MP-IB提出将混合精度量化视为信息瓶颈，通过FP16特征头（1024比特）编码说话人身份、INT4状态头（128比特）捕获躁狂状态，实现8倍信息不对称，在边缘设备上实现躁狂症情绪监测。在Bridge2AI-Voice上rho=0.117，零样本迁移CREMA-D AUC=0.817，延迟23.4ms，模型仅617KB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在资源受限的边缘设备上解耦稳定的说话人特征与易变的情绪状态，需要一种轻量级框架实现临床级特质-状态分离。

Method: 将混合精度量化构建为信息瓶颈：FP16（1024比特）编码说话人身份，INT4（128比特）捕获躁狂状态，形成8倍信息不对称；引入动态精度调度和多尺度时间融合机制。

Result: Bridge2AI-Voice上rho=0.117，超越WavLM-Adapter（rho=-0.042）、beta VAE（rho=0.089）等基线2.8-15.9个点；零样本迁移CREMA-D AUC=0.817；身份泄露接近随机水平（EER=0.42, MIA-AUC=0.52）；延迟23.4ms，模型仅617KB。

Conclusion: MP-IB开创性地将数值精度本身作为容量控制手段，无需对抗训练即可实现特质-状态分离，且能在20美元以下设备上实现实时监测，为边缘端临床语音生物标志物监测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of bipolar disorder agitation via voice biomarkers requires disentangling stable speaker traits from volatile affective states on resource-constrained edge devices. We introduce MP-IB, the first framework to treat mixed-precision quantization as an information bottleneck for clinical trait-state separation. The core insight is that numerical precision itself controls capacity: an FP16 trait head (1,024 bits) encodes speaker identity, while an INT4 state head (128 bits) captures agitation, yielding 8x information asymmetry without adversarial training. We augment this with Dynamic Precision Scheduling and Multi-Scale Temporal Fusion. On Bridge2AI-Voice (N=833, 4 sessions/participant, strict speaker-independent CV), MP-IB achieves rho = 0.117 (95\% CI: [0.089, 0.145], p=0.003 vs. chance), outperforming 94M-parameter WavLM-Adapter with in-domain SSL continuation (rho = -0.042), beta VAE disentanglement (rho = 0.089), and hand-crafted prosody (rho = 0.031) by 2.8--15.9 points absolute. Zero-shot transfer to CREMA-D achieves AUC=0.817. Identity leakage is suppressed to near-random (EER=0.42, MIA-AUC=0.52). End-to-end latency is 23.4 ms with a 617 KB footprint, enabling real-time monitoring on sub 20 dollar devices.

</details>


### [128] [TCD-Arena: Assessing Robustness of Time Series Causal Discovery Methods Against Assumption Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03045)
*Gideon Stein,Niklas Penzel,Tristan Piater,Joachim Denzler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TCD-Arena，一个模块化、高度可定制的测试工具，用于评估时间序列因果发现算法在面对逐步严重的假设违反时的鲁棒性。通过约3000万次因果发现尝试的实证研究，揭示了33种不同假设违反下的细微鲁棒性特征，并发现集成方法有潜力提升整体鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现（CD）框架在实际应用中依赖强且往往无法验证的假设，且缺乏可靠的性能评估方法，限制了其实践采纳。为克服这些局限并推进实证评估，作者开发了TCD-Arena测试工具。

Method: TCD-Arena是一个模块化、高度可定制且可扩展的测试工具，通过逐步加剧假设违反程度的方式，系统评估时间序列因果发现算法的鲁棒性。研究进行了约3000万次独立的因果发现尝试。

Result: 在33种不同的假设违反条件下，揭示了时间序列CD算法细微的鲁棒性特征；同时发现CD集成方法有潜力提升整体鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TCD-Arena有助于推动开发在各种合成数据及潜在真实数据条件下均可靠的因果发现方法。

Abstract: Causal Discovery (CD) is a powerful framework for scientific inquiry. Yet, its practical adoption is hindered by a reliance on strong, often unverifiable assumptions and a lack of robust performance assessment. To address these limitations and advance empirical CD evaluation, we present TCD-Arena, a modularized, highly customizable, and extendable testing kit to assess the robustness of time series CD algorithms against stepwise more severe assumption violations. For demonstration, we conduct an extensive empirical study comprising around 30 million individual CD attempts and reveal nuanced robustness profiles for 33 distinct assumption violations. Further, we investigate CD ensembles and find that they have the potential to improve general robustness, which has implications for real-world applications. With this, we strive to ultimately facilitate the development of CD methods that are reliable for a diverse range of synthetic and potentially real-world data conditions.

</details>


### [129] [Neuron-Anchored Rule Extraction for Large Language Models via Contrastive Hierarchical Ablation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03058)
*Francesco Sovrano,Gabriele Dominici,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MechaRule，一种将规则提取与LLM电路机制相结合的流水线方法，通过定位稀疏的"激动子"神经元并验证其单调覆盖特性，实现高效、可解释的规则抽取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局规则提取方法缺乏对模型电路的机制性解释，而机械可解释性虽能连接行为与神经元，但依赖人工假设和昂贵的逐神经元干预。MechaRule旨在弥合这一鸿沟，实现机制可解释的规则提取。

Method: MechaRule基于两个实证发现：(1) 在固定基线/翻转机制下，稀疏激动子效应具有近似单调性和饱和性，可建模为自适应组测试，仅需Θ(k log(N/k) + k)次干预；(2) 通过数据分裂验证消融结果的忠实性可提升激动子定位可靠性。方法核心是定位并验证这些稀疏激动子神经元。

Result: 在算术和越狱任务中，MechaRule成功召回96.8%的高效应暴力搜索激动子；抑制定位到的激动子后，算术准确率下降最多71.1%，越狱成功率下降8.8%。

Conclusion: MechaRule通过机制可解释的方式实现了高效的规则提取，验证了激动子神经元的单调覆盖特性在不同任务和模型中的适用性，为LLM可解释性提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: A key goal of explainable AI (XAI) is to express the decision logic of large language models (LLMs) in symbolic form and link it to internal mechanisms. Global rule-extraction methods typically learn symbolic surrogates without grounding rules in model circuitry, while mechanistic interpretability can connect behaviors to neuron sets but often depends on hand-crafted hypotheses and expensive neuron-level interventions. We introduce MechaRule, a pipeline that grounds rule extraction in LLM circuits by efficiently localizing sparse neurons called agonists, whose activation neutralization disrupts rule-related behaviors. MechaRule rests on two empirical observations. First, within a fixed baseline/flip regime, sparse agonist effects can be approximately monotone and saturating: a few dominant neuron activations can overtop weaker ones at coarse scales, while overlapping neurons flip many of the same examples. This motivates viewing localization as adaptive group testing driven by a regime-conditional strength predicate with confidence-guided conservative pruning, yielding Theta(k log(N/k) + k) interventions over N candidates when k << N neurons are agonists under the monotone-overtopping abstraction. Second, agonists emerge more reliably when ablations are verified through data splits aligned with close-to-faithful rule behavior; spectral splits remain a useful rule-free fallback, while unfaithful splits degrade localization. Empirically, overtopping appears mainly in learned, task-aligned regimes: on arithmetic and jailbreak tasks across Qwen2 and GPT-J, MechaRule recalls 96.8% of high-effect brute-force agonists in completed comparisons, and suppressing localized agonists reduces arithmetic accuracy and jailbreak success by up to 71.1% and 8.8%, respectively.

</details>


### [130] [Adaptive Negative Scheduling for Graph Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03076)
*Adnan Ali,Jinlong Li,Syed Muhammad Israr,Ali Kashif Bashir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdNGCL框架，通过自适应负调度策略（HANS）在损失门控和预算约束下动态选择负样本，显著提升图对比学习的性能和效率，在9个基准数据集上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图对比学习方法依赖静态负采样，无法在训练过程中动态考量负样本的信息量和计算成本，导致效率低下、性能受限。

Method: 提出AdNGCL框架，核心是HANS调度器：将负样本按困难程度分为难、中、易三层，基于对比损失趋势在全局和每类预算约束下动态调整步长，并定期刷新样本以保持多样性。

Result: 在9个基准图数据集上，AdNGCL在7个数据集上取得最佳准确率，剩余2个数据集上取得第二优结果，同时实现了对计算成本的显式控制。

Conclusion: 预算感知、损失敏感的调度策略是提升计算智能应用中表示学习鲁棒性和效率的通用有效策略。

Abstract: Graph contrastive learning (GCL) has become a central paradigm for self-supervised representation learning in computational intelligence, with applications spanning recommendation, anomaly detection, and personalization. A key limitation of existing methods is their reliance on static negative sampling, which fails to account for the dynamic informativeness and computational cost of negatives during training. We propose AdNGCL, an adaptive negative scheduling framework with a hardness-aware scheduler (HANS) that formulates negative selection as a loss-gated, budget-constrained process across hard, intermediate, and easy strata. The scheduler dynamically adjusts step sizes based on contrastive loss trends under both global and per-category budgets, while periodically refreshing samples to maintain diversity without exceeding compute constraints. Experiments on nine benchmark graph datasets demonstrate that AdNGCL consistently advances state-of-the-art performance, achieving the best accuracy on seven datasets and second-best on the remaining two, while offering explicit control over computational cost. These results highlight the value of budget-aware, loss-sensitive scheduling as a general strategy for improving the robustness and efficiency of representation learning in emerging computational intelligence applications.

</details>


### [131] [Adaptive Data Compression and Reconstruction for Memory-Bounded EEG Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03085)
*Chengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADaCoRe是一种面向无监督个体持续学习（UICL）的记忆高效EEG分析方法，通过基于显著性的关键帧保护、有理多相压缩、伴随重建以及原型置信度选择，在严格缓冲约束下显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信号具有毫秒级时间分辨率，但受限于噪声大和个体差异大，在有限标注下难以实现鲁棒的个性化建模。现有UICL方法通常存储完整历史样本，这违背了持续学习避免重训练的核心目标。

Method: 提出ADaCoRe管线，包含四个关键设计：(1) 基于显著性的关键帧保护，(2) 有理多相压缩，(3) 伴随重建并在受保护索引上逐字覆盖，(4) 原型置信度选择实现自适应样例维护。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，ADaCoRe在严格缓冲约束下一致优于强基线方法：ISRUC数据集上提升至少+2.7 ACC，FACED数据集上提升至少+15.3 ACC。消融研究量化了压缩-保真度权衡，可视化结果证实了压缩重建过程中关键EEG形态的保留。

Conclusion: ADaCoRe通过利用EEG信号的结构化形态特性进行选择性压缩与重建，实现了记忆高效的UICL，在无需重训练的前提下有效平衡了存储压缩与信息保真度。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) signals provide millisecond-level temporal resolution but their analysis is limited by remarkable noise and inter-subject variability, making robust personalization difficult under limited annotations. Unsupervised Individual Continual Learning (UICL) has been proposed to address this practical challenge, where a model pretrained on a labeled cohort must adapt online to unlabeled subject streams under strict memory constraints. However, existing UICL methods typically store full past samples, which undermine the continual learning goal of avoiding retraining. Observing that EEG signals exhibit well-structured morphologies to be exploited via morphology-aware selection, compression, and reconstruction, here we propose Adaptive Data Compression and Reconstruction (ADaCoRe) for UICL. This is a memory-efficient pipeline composed of saliency-driven keyframe protection, rational polyphase compression, adjoint reconstruction with verbatim overwrite on protected indices, and prototype-confidence selection for adaptive exemplar maintenance. Across three representative benchmarks, ADaCoRe consistently outperforms recent strong baselines under tight buffer regimes (eg., the performance gains are at least +2.7 and +15.3 ACC on ISRUC and FACED datasets, respectively). Ablation studies quantify compression-fidelity trade-offs and highlight the contribution of each design, while visualizations confirm the preservation of key EEG morphology during compression and reconstruction.

</details>


### [132] [Attribution-Guided Masking for Robust Cross-Domain Sentiment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03091)
*Shubham Harkare,Arvind Yogesh Suresh Babu,Yash Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种注意力引导掩码（AGM）方法，通过在微调过程中动态检测和惩罚领域特定的虚假标记，有效提升了预训练Transformer模型在零样本域间情感分类中的泛化能力，同时提供标记级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer模型在域内情感分类中表现优异，但迁移到域外数据时性能严重下降。作者假设这种泛化差距源于模型过度依赖领域特定的虚假标记，并验证了事后标记级归因漂移无法预测该差距。

Method: 提出注意力引导掩码（AGM），核心为基于梯度的归因掩码损失（L_mask），可选配反事实对比损失以强制域不变表示。在微调时动态检测高归因的虚假标记并进行惩罚，无需目标域标签或人工标注。

Result: 在四个不同域、八种随机种子的严格零样本迁移设置下，AGM在最具挑战性的Sentiment140迁移中达到0.244的Δ值，与DANN（0.264）、DRO（0.248）、Fish（0.247）、IRM（0.238）等强基线相比具有竞争力，同时提供标记级可解释性。消融实验证实归因引导掩码是关键组件。

Conclusion: AGM能有效抑制@提及、话题标签和俚语等域特定标记的归因，将模型依赖转向域不变的情感标记，从而缩小泛化差距。归因引导掩码是提升域间泛化的核心机制。

Abstract: While pre-trained Transformer models achieve high accuracy on in-domain sentiment classification, they frequently experience severe performance degradation when transferring to out-of-domain data. We hypothesize that this generalization gap is driven by reliance on domain-specific spurious tokens. After demonstrating that post-hoc-token-level attribution drift fails to predict this gap, we propose Attribution-Guided Masking (AGM), a training time intervention that dynamically detects and penalizes highly attributed spurious tokens during fine-tuning. AGM's core component is a gradient based attribution masking loss ($\mathcal{L}_{mask}$), which can optionally be combined with a counterfactual contrastive loss to enforce domain-invariant representations, all without requiring target-domain labels or human annotation. Evaluated in a strict zero-shot transfer setting across four diverse domains with eight random seeds, AGM achieves competitive generalization compared to five strong baselines on the hardest transfer (Sentiment140): $Δ$ = 0.244 versus DANN (0.264), DRO (0.248), Fish (0.247), and IRM (0.238), while uniquely providing token-level interpretability into which features drive the generalization gap. Our qualitative analysis confirms that AGM suppresses attribution on domain-specific tokens such as @mentions, hashtags, and slang, shifting reliance toward domain-invariant sentiment markers. Our ablation study further confirms that attribution-guided masking is the critical component: removing it or replacing it with random token selection consistently degrades performance on difficult transfers.

</details>


### [133] [When Prompts Interact: Assessing Prompt Arithmetic for Deconfounding under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03096)
*Zhecheng Sheng,Yongsen Tan,Xiruo Ding,Trevor Cohen,Serguei Pakhomov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyPA (Hybrid Prompt Arithmetic) 通过结合任务提示和线性化混杂提示的算术运算，在参数高效的前提下有效缓解了分类任务中的虚假关联问题，提升了分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类模型常依赖混杂变量产生的虚假特征在分布内表现良好，但在分布偏移下性能严重下降。任务算术虽可通过减法移除不需要的信号，但需要全参数微调，计算成本高。提示微调是参数高效的替代方案，但其提示层面任务算术能否限制虚假特征依赖尚不清楚。

Method: 提出Hybrid Prompt Arithmetic (HyPA)，将任务提示（task prompts）与线性化混杂提示（linearized confounder prompts）通过提示算术组合，以抵消虚假相关性。具体做法是对抗混杂信号进行提示层面的加/减操作。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，HyPA在分布偏移下相比提示算术基线持续改善了鲁棒性-性能权衡。进一步分析发现，HyPA通过减少混杂信号对预测的影响或在表示中抑制混杂信号来缓解混淆效应。

Conclusion: HyPA建立了一种参数高效且有前景的方法，用于在混杂偏移下提升模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: In classification tasks, models may rely on confounding variables to achieve strong in-distribution performance, capturing spurious features that fail under distribution shift. This shortcut behavior leads to substantial degradation in out-of-distribution settings. Task arithmetic offers a potential solution by removing unwanted signals via subtraction of secondary model updates, but it typically requires full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive. Prompt tuning provides a parameter-efficient alternative by adapting models through a small set of trainable virtual tokens. Task arithmetic on the resulting prompts presents an appealing alternative to operations on entire models, but the extent to which this approach can limit reliance on spurious features remains to be established. In this work, we study whether composing soft prompts through task arithmetic improves robustness to confounding shifts. We propose Hybrid Prompt Arithmetic (HyPA), which combines task prompts with linearized confounder prompts to counteract spurious correlations. Across multiple benchmarks, HyPA consistently improves the robustness-performance trade-off relative to prompt-arithmetic baselines under distribution shift. We further analyze how HyPA affects hidden representations and find evidence consistent with it mitigating confounding either by reducing the influence of confounder signals on predictions or by suppressing them in the representation. These results establish HyPA as a parameter-efficient and promising approach for improving robustness under confounding shifts in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [134] [Gated Subspace Inference for Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03109)
*Stephen J. Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过利用每层token激活流形的低有效秩来加速Transformer语言模型推理的方法。该方法将激活向量分解为子空间分量和残差，通过缓存的低秩权重图像以降低内存带宽计算子空间分量的线性层输出，并使用逐token门控决定是否计算残差修正。在GPT-2 124M、GPT-J 6B和OPT 6.7B上，线性层权重读取实现3.0倍至10.5倍加速，困惑度比率低于1.00，top-1 token一致性高于98%。无需重新训练或架构修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型推理时，线性层的权重读取受限于内存带宽，成为性能瓶颈。利用激活流形的低有效秩特性，可以显著减少内存访问开销，同时保持输出分布质量。

Method: 将每个激活向量分解为子空间分量和残差；子空间分量通过缓存的低秩权重图像以降低内存带宽计算；引入逐token门控机制，根据可容忍误差决定是否计算残差修正，从而保证输出分布在可控容差内。

Result: 在AMD MI300X上，GPT-2 124M、GPT-J 6B和OPT 6.7B的线性层权重读取加速3.0x-10.5x，困惑度比率<1.00，top-1 token一致性>98%。GPT-J 6B在k=256, ε=0.05时输出与基线逐字符一致。

Conclusion: 该方法无需重新训练、架构修改或近似注意力机制，即可在保持输出质量的前提下显著加速Transformer推理，是一种实用且高效的推理加速方案。

Abstract: A method is presented for accelerating inference in transformer language models by exploiting the low effective rank of the token activation manifold at each layer. The method decomposes each activation vector into a subspace component and a residual, computes the linear-layer output on the subspace component via a cached low-rank weight image at reduced memory bandwidth, and applies a per-token gate that determines whether the residual correction is computed or skipped. The gate ensures that the output distribution is preserved to within a controllable tolerance. Validation on three model families (GPT-2 124M, GPT-J 6B, OPT 6.7B) on AMD MI300X demonstrates effective speedups of 3.0x to 10.5x on linear-layer weight reads with perplexity ratios below 1.00 and top-1 token agreement above 98%. The method requires no retraining, no architectural modification, and no approximation of the attention mechanism. At the operating point (k = 256, ε = 0.05) on GPT-J 14 6B, the accelerated model produces character-for-character identical output to the baseline.

</details>


### [135] [Taming the Curses of Multiagency in Robust Markov Games with Large State Space through Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03125)
*Jingchu Gai,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有线性函数近似的分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈，提出了首个突破维度灾难（curse of multiagency）的样本高效算法，适用于大状态空间（包括无限状态空间）的两种设定（生成模型和在线交互）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈（RMG）的可证明数据高效算法局限于表格型设定（有限状态和动作空间），无法应对大状态空间场景；而唯一超越表格设定的工作使用线性函数近似但依赖严格限制（vanish minimal value假设）且仍受维度灾难困扰。

Method: 针对由全变差距离定义的不确定集，在生成模型设定和新提出的在线交互设定下，设计了可证明数据高效的算法，利用线性函数近似打破维度灾难。

Result: 首次在具有大（可能无限）状态空间的分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈中，不考虑不确定集构造方式的前提下，打破了样本复杂度的维度灾难。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了分布鲁棒MARL在大状态空间场景下理论样本效率的空白，为后续鲁棒多智能体强化学习的数据高效算法设计提供了基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) holds great potential but faces robustness challenges due to environmental uncertainty. To address this, distributionally robust Markov games (RMGs) optimize worst-case performance when the environment deviates from the nominal model within a uncertainty set. Beyond robustness, an equally urgent goal for MARL is data efficiency -- sampling from vast state and action spaces that grow exponentially with the number of agents potentially leads to the curse of multiagency. However, current provably data-efficient algorithms for RMGs are limited to tabular settings with finite state and action spaces, which are only computationally manageable for small-scale problems, leaving RMGs with large-scale (or infinite) state spaces largely unexplored. The only existing work beyond tabular settings focuses on linear function approximation (LFA) for a restrictive class of RMGs using vanish minimal value assumption and still suffers from sample complexity with the curse of multiagency. In this work, we focuses on general RMGs with LFA. For uncertainty sets defined by total variation distance, we develop provably data-efficient algorithms that break the curse of multiagency in both the generative model setting and a newly proposed online interactive setting. To our knowledge, our results are the first to break the curse of multiagency of sample complexity for RMGs with large (possibly infinite) state spaces, regardless of the uncertainty set construction.

</details>


### [136] [Instance-Level Costs for Nuanced Classifier Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03135)
*Kabir Kang,Stephen Mussmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化超额成本（NEC）指标，用于在内容审核、医疗筛查等场景中按样本重要性加权分类错误。实验发现模型错误集中于模糊低代价样本（错误率5%时NEC仅1.8%），但将代价纳入训练仅在代价可从输入特征预测时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类指标平等对待所有错误，但在内容审核、医疗筛查等安全关键应用中，清晰案例上的错误远比模糊案例上的错误代价更高，现有指标无法反映这种差异。

Method: 提出归一化超额成本（NEC）指标，通过每样本代价加权分类错误（代价可来自标注者投票差距、决策阈值距离或置信度评分），并在文本、图像、表格数据上评估NEC与传统错误率的差异，同时测试将代价融入训练的三种策略（损失加权、采样策略、回归）。

Result: NEC通常远低于错误率（如5%错误率对应1.8%NEC），表明错误集中在模糊低代价样本。但代价敏感训练仅当代价可从输入特征预测时（合成数据）才有效，真实数据集上效果参差不齐或收益甚微。

Conclusion: NEC框架提供了一种从标注数据中推导实例级误分类代价的实用方法，即使代价敏感训练收益有限，该指标仍可用于更精准地评估模型在安全关键场景中的实际表现。

Abstract: Standard classification treats all errors equally, but in content moderation, medical screening, and safety-critical applications, mistakes on clear-cut cases are far more costly than errors on ambiguous ones. We propose normalized excess cost (NEC), a metric that weights classification errors by per-example costs and reduces to standard error rate when costs are uniform. Costs can derive from annotator vote margins, distance from decision thresholds, or confidence ratings. Across text, image, and tabular benchmarks, we find that NEC is often substantially lower than error rate -- models with 5\% error rate can achieve 1.8\% NEC -- revealing that most mistakes concentrate on ambiguous, low-cost examples. However, incorporating costs into training via loss weighting, sampling strategies, or regression yields inconsistent benefits: improvements appear only when costs are predictable from input features, as in our synthetic control, while real-world datasets show mixed or negligible gains. Our framework provides a practical methodology for deriving and evaluating instance-level misclassification costs, even when cost-sensitive training offers limited benefit.

</details>


### [137] [OCRR: A Benchmark for Online Correction Recovery under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03153)
*Adrian Grassi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OCRR基准，用于评测分类系统在流式分布偏移下的在线恢复速度。实验表明，所提出的Substrate方法在同等内存预算下，新类准确率（88.7%）和原分布准确率（95.4%）远超现有持续学习基线（+32.6百分点）和LoRA微调（+84.6百分点），且对检索不完美具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态基准无法衡量真实系统中分布偏移（新类别、查询改写等）下的在线恢复能力，缺乏评估系统通过用户纠错流进行在线恢复速度的标准化指标。

Method: 提出OCRR基准，通过流式语料分类系统、应用oracle或随机纠错，报告新类准确率和原分布准确率随纠错次数的变化曲线。评测了9种基线算法（5个家族）和7种有界存储变体，包括在线学习（river）、持续学习（EWC/A-GEM/LwF）、检索/参数混合（kNN-LM）、参数高效微调（LoRA on DeBERTa-v3-large）及哈希链追加式Substrate。

Result: 在Banking77和CLINC150数据集上，Substrate在新类准确率（88.7±2.9%）和原分布准确率（95.4±0.8%）上均最优，同等内存预算下比持续学习基线高32.6百分点，比LoRA微调高84.6百分点。分类准确率在10k到10M规模下稳定在99%，即使ANN召回率从0.69降至0.23。

Conclusion: Substrate的边界带多数投票机制对检索不完美具有鲁棒性，纯top-k召回率无法预测这一特性。代码和数数据开源。

Abstract: Static benchmarks measure a model frozen at training time. Real systems face distribution shift: new categories, paraphrased queries, drift: and must recover online via user corrections. No existing benchmark measures recovery speed under correction streams. We introduce OCRR (Online Correction Recovery Rate): a benchmark that streams a corpus through a classification system, applies oracle or stochastic corrections to wrong predictions, and reports two curves: novel-class accuracy and original-distribution accuracy versus correction count. We evaluate the substrate alongside nine baseline algorithms from five families plus seven bounded-storage variants of the substrate for the Pareto sweep, including standard online-learning baselines (river), continual-learning methods (EWC, A-GEM, LwF), retrieval/parametric hybrids (kNN-LM), parameter-efficient fine-tuning of a 1.5 B-parameter encoder (LoRA on DeBERTa-v3-large), and a hash-chained append-only substrate (Substrate). On Banking77 and CLINC150, under oracle and sparse correction policies, the substrate is the only system that simultaneously recovers novel-class accuracy (88.7 +/- 2.9 %) and retains original-distribution accuracy (95.4 +/- 0.8 %) beating the next-best published continual-learning baseline by 32.6 percentage points at equal memory budget, and beating LoRA-on-DeBERTa-v3-large by 84.6 percentage points on retention. We further find that classification accuracy remains stable at 99 % even as approximate-nearest-neighbour recall@5 degrades from 0.69 to 0.23 across 10 k to 10 M corpus scales, suggesting the substrate's margin-band majority vote is robust to retrieval imperfection in a way that pure top-k recall metrics do not predict. Code and data are available at https://github.com/adriangrassi/ocrr-benchmark.

</details>


### [138] [Pairwise matrices for sparse autoencoders: single-feature inspection mislabels causal axes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03160)
*Michael A. Riegler,Birk Sebastian Frostelid Torpmann-Hagen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出成对矩阵协议（pairwise matrix protocol），通过联合调节多个特征的调控系数来改进标准稀疏自编码器（SAE）可解释性协议。在Qwen3-1.7B-Instruct和Gemma-2-2B-it上的实验揭示了标准单特征协议遗漏的三个重要发现：1）"AI自我免责声明"特征在系数扫描下呈现倒U型行为；2）三个近正交的聚类特定特征联合抑制会破坏接地理性构建；3）匹配几何比较显示损伤模式依赖方向而非幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE可解释性协议仅对单个特征进行激活上下文标注和调控验证，忽略了特征间相互作用对模型行为的联合影响。本文旨在开发能捕捉多特征协同效应的更完整的可解释性方法。

Method: 提出成对矩阵协议，联合调控多个特征的引导系数并观察模型的输出变化。在Qwen3-1.7B-Instruct和Gemma-2-2B-it上对特征进行单特征扫描、联合调控和匹配几何比较（控制范数和余弦相似度），并在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上定位因果特征。

Result: 三个主要发现：1）AI自我免责声明特征在系数c=+500时出现倒U型行为，模型输出从免责声明转为沉思哲学家声音；2）三个近正交的哲学心智特征在联合抑制时同时破坏接地理性构建和元认知提示；3）匹配几何比较显示单特征、联合和随机方向扰动产生三种不同的输出模式，损伤取决于方向模式而非幅度。

Conclusion: 标准单特征协议遗漏了重要的多特征交互效应，成对矩阵协议能揭示更丰富的因果结构。所有三个发现在Gemma上复现，匹配几何控制获得约10倍置信区间分离，证明了方法的稳健性。

Abstract: The standard sparse-autoencoder (SAE) interpretability protocol labels each feature from its top-activating contexts and validates by single-feature steering. We propose the pairwise matrix protocol, co-varying steering coefficient with joint condition, and report three findings the standard one-corner protocol misses on Qwen3-1.7B-Instruct, replicated on Gemma-2-2B-it. First, a feature labelled "AI self-disclaimer" from its top contexts produces an inverted U-shape under a coefficient sweep: at c=+500 the model substitutes a fluent contemplative-philosopher voice for the disclaimer. Two further features anchor the criterion (one monotonic, one pure breakdown). Second, three near-orthogonal cluster-specific features that individually steer a philosophy-of-mind register, jointly suppressed at c=-500, damage grounded composition on recipes and engine explanations as well as introspective prompts; single-feature suppression at the same magnitude leaves controls intact. Third, a matched-geometry comparison of single-feature, joint, and random-direction perturbations (norm ~1.55, cosine ~0.64) yields three distinct output regimes: single-feature substitutes strategy filler, random direction substitutes diverse content, joint suppression alone produces placeholder text. Coherence loss is direction-pattern-dependent, not magnitude-dependent. All three findings reproduce on Gemma with model-specific damage signatures; the matched-geometry control is CI-separated by ~10x. The pipeline also locates a top causally responsible feature in Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct.

</details>


### [139] [Global and Local Topology-Aware Attention with Persistent Homology and Euler Biases for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03163)
*Usef Faghihi,Amir Saki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种拓扑感知注意力框架，通过持久同调、欧拉特征变换和核-希尔伯特通道将预测性几何结构（连通性、循环、壳状几何、方向变化、非线性邻域）注入注意力对数，经验证门控局部残差选择性地保留拓扑信号。在三种架构（轻量注意力/Ridge、PatchTST、TimeSeriesTransformer）上评估，在CO2、S&P 500、NASA IMS轴承数据集上，拓扑感知模型在几何结构可预测时均表现出正向配对效果（RMSE降低12.5%-47.8%，p<1e-3），支持拓扑作为验证选择、架构兼容的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准点积注意力无法显式表示时序数据中蕴含的预测性几何结构（如连通性、循环、壳状几何、方向变化和非线性邻域），需要一种能显式编码这些拓扑特征的注意力机制。

Method: 提出拓扑感知注意力框架，通过持久同调（H0-H2）、锚定欧拉特征变换和核-希尔伯特通道向注意力对数注入几何结构；采用验证门控局部残差（Zeng风格局部H0分量）仅在验证数据支持时保留拓扑校正；在无泄漏协议下评估精确Vietoris-Rips计算和平滑拓扑替代方法。

Result: 在轻量注意力/Ridge（46/63单元改善，RMSE降低12.5%，p=7.2e-4）、PatchTST（33单元改善，20单元持平，降低23.5%，p=3.5e-5）、TimeSeriesTransformer（47单元改善，降低47.8%，p<1e-4）三种架构上均观察到正向配对效果，改善幅度因数据集和架构而异。

Conclusion: 拓扑结构可作为验证选择的、与多种架构兼容的有效归纳偏置，在几何结构具有预测性时显著提升时序预测性能。

Abstract: Scientific time series often encode predictive geometric structure, including connectivity, cycles, shell-like geometry, directional changes, and nonlinear neighborhoods, that standard dot-product attention does not explicitly represent. We introduce a topology-aware attention framework that adds such structure to attention logits using persistent homology (H0-H2), anchored Euler characteristic transforms, and kernel-Hilbert channels. A validation-gated local residual captures local topological signals, including a Zeng-style local H0 component, only when held-out validation data support the correction. Exact Vietoris-Rips computations and smooth topological surrogates are evaluated under a no-leakage protocol with train-only calibration, validation-only selection, and test-only reporting.
  We evaluate guarded topology-aware variants across three architecture families: lightweight attention/Ridge, PatchTSTForRegression, and TimeSeriesTransformerForPrediction. Experiments include synthetic benchmarks isolating higher-order topology and real datasets covering CO2, S&P 500 return-window geometry, and NASA IMS bearing degradation. The audit uses matched paired comparisons across seven dataset units, three random seeds, and three chronological splits, giving 63 paired units per architecture and 189 paired units overall. Topology-aware models show positive paired effects when geometry is predictive, with heterogeneous magnitude across datasets and architectures. Lightweight attention/Ridge improves in 46 of 63 units, with mean relative RMSE reduction of 12.5% and paired randomization p=7.2e-4; PatchTST improves in 33 units and retains the baseline in 20 units, with 23.5% reduction and p=3.5e-5; and TimeSeriesTransformer improves in 47 units, with 47.8% reduction and p<1e-4. The results support topology as a validation-selected, architecture-compatible inductive bias.

</details>


### [140] [Moral Sensitivity in LLMs: A Tiered Evaluation of Contextual Bias via Behavioral Profiling and Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03217)
*Yash Aggarwal,Atmika Gorti,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Krishnaprasad Thirunarayan,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出道德敏感指数(MSI)用于量化LLM在七级压力测试中的偏见概率，并通过行为分析和机制验证两个阶段揭示：指令微调可消除偏见，但推理蒸馏会重新激活偏见至小模型水平，且社会性加载的线索会激活相同的偏见驱动电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见评估将模型输出简单地划分为"有偏见"或"无偏见"的二元框架，忽略了偏见逐渐、上下文敏感地出现的特性。

Method: 分两阶段：(1)行为分析阶段，提出MSI指标，在七级压力测试（从抽象数值问题到历史社会经济不公正场景）中量化偏见概率，评估Claude 3.5、Qwen 3.5、Llama 3和Gemini 1.5；(2)机制验证阶段，选取刑事偏见场景作为探针，对六种模型（SLMs、指令微调基础模型、推理蒸馏变体）应用logit lens、注意力分析、激活补丁和语义探测进行电路级分析。

Result: Gemini 1.5在社会经济框架下Tier 5的MSI达72.7%，Claude则表现抑制；电路分析揭示偏见的U形曲线：SLM表现强刑事偏见，指令微调消除偏见，推理蒸馏将偏见恢复到SLM水平（参数数量相同），表明蒸馏压缩了推理痕迹并重新激活了浅层统计关联。

Conclusion: 社会性加载的线索驱动高MSI分数，并激活机制上识别的相同偏见驱动电路，验证了跨阶段的一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings that require nuanced ethical reasoning, yet existing bias evaluations treat model outputs as simply "biased" or "unbiased." This binary framing misses the gradual, context-sensitive way bias actually emerges. We address this gap in two stages: behavioral profiling and mechanistic validation. In the behavioral stage, we introduce the Moral Sensitivity Index (MSI), a metric that quantifies the probability of biased output across a graduated, seven-tier stress test ranging from abstract numerical problems to scenarios rooted in historical and socioeconomic injustice. Evaluating four leading models (Claude 3.5, Qwen 3.5, Llama 3, and Gemini 1.5), we identify distinct behavioral signatures shaped by alignment design: for instance, Gemini 1.5 reaches 72.7% MSI by Tier 5 under socioeconomic framing, while Claude exhibits sharp suppression consistent with identity-based safety training. We then verify these behavioral patterns mechanistically. We select criminal-bias scenarios, which produced the highest MSI scores across models, as probes and apply logit lens, attention analysis, activation patching, and semantic probing to a controlled set of six models spanning three capability tiers: small language models (SLMs), instruction-tuned base models, and reasoning-distilled variants. Circuit-level analysis reveals a U-curve of bias: SLMs exhibit strong criminal bias; scaling to instruction-tuned models eliminates it; reasoning distillation reintroduces bias to SLM-like levels despite identical parameter counts, suggesting distillation compresses reasoning traces in ways that reactivate shallow statistical associations.
