Critically, the socially loaded cues that drive high MSI scores activate the same bias-driving circuits identified mechanistically, providing cross-stage validation.

</details>


### [141] [Task Vector Geometry Underlies Dual Modes of Task Inference in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03780)
*Hao Yan,Haolin Yang,Yiqiao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过可控合成实验，证明了Transformer内部存在两种推理模式：分布内任务通过贝叶斯检索实现（任务向量凸组合），分布外泛化通过外推学习实现（表征位于与任务向量子空间近乎正交的子空间），揭示了任务向量几何、训练分布与泛化行为之间的紧密联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究发现了任务向量（task vectors），但缺乏严格的理论基础来解释任务向量的几何结构如何被训练分布塑造，以及何种几何结构能实现分布外（OOD）泛化。

Method: 在可控合成环境中从头训练小型Transformer，使用潜在任务序列分布，进行原则性的数学刻画。通过分析任务向量的几何结构（凸组合 vs. 正交子空间）来区分两种推理模式。

Result: 分布内行为通过贝叶斯任务检索实现，内部表现为学习到的任务向量的凸组合；分布外行为通过外推任务学习实现，其表征位于与任务向量子空间近乎正交的子空间。两种推理模式可共存于单一模型中。

Conclusion: 任务向量几何、训练分布与泛化行为之间存在密切关联，为理解Transformer的上下文学习机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Transformers are effective at inferring the latent task from context via two inference modes: recognizing a task seen during training, and adapting to a novel one. Recent interpretability studies have identified from middle-layer representations task-specific directions, or task vectors, that steer model behavior. However, a lack of rigorous foundations hinders connecting internal representations to external model behavior: existing work fails to explain how task-vector geometry is shaped by the training distribution, and what geometry enables out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. In this paper, we study these questions in a controlled synthetic setting by training small transformers from scratch on latent-task sequence distributions, which allows a principled mathematical characterization. We show that two inference modes can coexist within a single model. In-distribution behavior is governed by Bayesian task retrieval, implemented internally through convex combinations of learned task vectors. OOD behavior, by contrast, arises through extrapolative task learning, whose representations occupy a subspace nearly orthogonal to the task-vector subspace. Taken together, our results suggest that task-vector geometry, training distributions, and generalization behaviors are closely related.

</details>


### [142] [Self-Mined Hardness for Safety Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03226)
*Prakhar Gupta,Garv Shah,Donghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需人工对抗数据集的安全微调方法：根据模型自身 rollout 被判定为有害的频率来评分每个候选提示的难度，然后在最难的提示上使用模型自身的非越狱 rollout 进行微调。在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 和 Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct 上，该方法将 WildJailbreak 攻击成功率从 11.5% 和 20.1% 降至 1-3%，但将越狱形良性提示的拒绝率从 14-22% 推高至 74-94%。通过将困难提示与对抗性框架的良性提示按 1:1 交错混合，可将拒绝率降低至 30-72%，但攻击成功率上升 2-6 个百分点。在混合训练中，选择最难的半数样本而非随机半数，可将剩余 ASR 再降低 35-50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全微调方法通常需要精心策划的对抗数据集，成本高昂且难以泛化。本文旨在探索一种无需人工标注对抗数据的替代方法，利用模型自身的行为信号自动识别困难提示并进行安全微调。

Method: 1) 对每个候选提示，通过目标模型多次 rollout 中被判定为有害的频率来定义"难度"；2) 选择最难（即最可能产生有害响应）的提示进行微调，配对该模型自身的非越狱（安全）rollout；3) 为解决过度拒绝问题，将困难提示与对抗性框架的良性提示（外观像越狱但意图良性的提示）按 1:1 交错混合训练；4) 在混合训练中进一步比较选择最困难半数 vs 随机半数的效果。

Result: 在 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 上，攻击成功率从 11.5% 降至 1-3%，但在越狱形良性提示上的拒绝率从 14-22% 飙升至 74-94%。混合训练将拒绝率降至 30-51%（8B）和 52-72%（3B），攻击成功率仅上升 2-6 个百分点。在混合训练中选择最困难半数而非随机半数，可将剩余 ASR 降低 35-50%（约 3 个百分点）。

Conclusion: 基于模型自身 rollout 有害性的难度评分是一种有效的自动对抗样本选择策略，能显著提升安全微调效果，但会引入过度拒绝问题。通过混合对抗性框架的良性提示可以部分缓解过度拒绝，但存在安全性与可用性之间的权衡。在混合训练中优先选择最困难的样本比随机选择更优。

Abstract: Safety fine-tuning of language models typically requires a curated adversarial dataset. We take a different approach: score each candidate prompt's difficulty by how often the target model's own rollouts are judged harmful, then fine-tune on the hardest prompts paired with the model's own non-jailbroken rollouts. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, this approach cuts the WildJailbreak attack success rate from 11.5% and 20.1% down to 1-3%, but pushes refusal on jailbreak-shaped benign prompts from 14-22% to 74-94%. Interleaving the same hard prompts 1:1 with adversarially-framed benign prompts (prompts that look like jailbreaks but have benign intent) cuts that refusal back down to 30-51% on 8B and 52-72% on 3B, at a cost of 2-6 percentage points of attack success rate. Within the mixed regime, training on the hardest half of the eligible pool rather than a random half cuts the remaining ASR by 35-50% (about 3 percentage points) on both models.

</details>


### [143] [Transformers with Selective Access to Early Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03953)
*Skye Gunasekaran,Téa Wright,Rui-Jie Zhu,Jason Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SATFormer提出了一种选择性访问机制，将早期表示复用视为检索问题而非连接问题，通过上下文相关的门控控制对第一层值路径的访问，在130M到1.3B参数规模上持续改善验证损失和零样本准确率，尤其对检索密集型任务提升显著（比静态值残差高约1.5个平均点），同时保持接近基线Transformer的吞吐量和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer架构中，随着残差流在深度中反复变换，低层特征（尤其是第一层值投影V_1）可能变得难以恢复。虽然静态值残差方法通过固定的混合系数暴露V_1，但V_1的有用性在不同token、头和上下文间并不恒定，需要更细粒度、自适应的访问控制。

Method: 将早期表示复用建模为检索问题，设计上下文相关的门控机制（Selective Access Transformer），在保留第一层值路径的同时，通过依赖上下文的门控控制对不同位置、头和上下文的访问权限，实现选择性而非均匀的早期表示重用。

Result: 在130M到1.3B参数规模上，SATFormer在验证损失和零样本准确率方面持续优于静态值残差和基线Transformer；在检索密集型基准上比静态值残差提升约1.5个平均点，且吞吐量和内存使用接近基线Transformer。门控分析显示稀疏、深度依赖、头部特定和类别敏感的访问模式。

Conclusion: SATFormer通过上下文相关的选择性访问机制，实现了对早期表示的高效、细粒度复用，验证了将早期表示重用视为检索问题的有效性，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了性能。

Abstract: Several recent Transformer architectures expose later layers to representations computed in the earliest layers, motivated by the observation that low-level features can become harder to recover as the residual stream is repeatedly transformed through depth. The cheapest among these methods add static value residuals: learned mixing coefficients that expose the first-layer value projection V_1 uniformly across tokens and heads. More expressive dense or dynamic alternatives recover finer-grained access, but at higher memory cost and lower throughput. The usefulness of V_1 is unlikely to be constant across tokens, heads, and contexts; different positions plausibly require different amounts of access to early lexical or semantic information. We therefore treat early-representation reuse as a retrieval problem rather than a connectivity problem, and introduce Selective Access Transformer (SATFormer), which preserves the first-layer value pathway while controlling access with a context-dependent gate. Across models from 130M to 1.3B parameters, SATFormer consistently improves validation loss and zero-shot accuracy over the static value-residual and Transformer baselines. Its strongest gains appear on retrieval-intensive benchmarks, where it improves over static value residuals by approximately 1.5 average points, while maintaining throughput and memory usage close to the baseline Transformer. Gate analyses suggest sparse, depth-dependent, head-specific, and category-sensitive access patterns, supporting the interpretation that SATFormer learns selective reuse of early representations rather than uniform residual copying. Our code is available at https://github.com/SkyeGunasekaran/SATFormer.

</details>


### [144] [Text-Conditional JEPA for Learning Semantically Rich Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03245)
*Chen Huang,Xianhang Li,Vimal Thilak,Etai Littwin,Josh Susskind*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TC-JEPA提出一种基于文本条件化的图像特征预测框架，通过引入图像描述来降低掩码位置的特征预测不确定性，从而学习更语义化的视觉表征。该方法在多项下游任务上优于对比学习方法，尤其在需要细粒度视觉理解的任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I-JEPA在掩码位置进行特征预测时面临固有的视觉不确定性，导致难以学习到语义化的表征。作者希望通过引入文本条件来降低这种预测不确定性，从而提升表征质量。

Method: 提出Text-Conditional JEPA (TC-JEPA)，在预测被掩码块的特征时，使用细粒度的文本调节器对输入文本token计算稀疏交叉注意力，从而将预测的patch特征调制为文本的函数，使其更具语义性。

Result: TC-JEPA在下游性能、训练稳定性方面均有提升，展现出良好的扩展性。在多种任务上超越对比学习方法，尤其是在需要细粒度视觉理解和推理的任务上优势明显。

Conclusion: TC-JEPA提供了一种基于纯特征预测的视觉-语言预训练新范式，有效解决了遮挡特征预测中的不确定性问题，为自监督视觉表征学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Image-based Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (I-JEPA) offers a promising approach to visual self-supervised learning through masked feature prediction. However with the inherent visual uncertainty at masked positions, feature prediction remains challenging and may fail to learn semantic representations. In this work, we propose Text-Conditional JEPA (TC-JEPA) that uses image captions to reduce the prediction uncertainty. Specifically, we modulate the predicted patch features using a fine-grained text conditioner that computes sparse cross-attention over input text tokens. With such conditioning, patch features become predictable as a function of text, thus are more semantically meaningful. We show TC-JEPA improves downstream performance and training stability, with promising scaling properties. TC-JEPA also offers a new vision-language pretraining paradigm based on feature prediction only, outperforming contrastive methods on diverse tasks, especially those requiring fine-grained visual understanding and reasoning.

</details>


### [145] [Posterior-First Neural PDE Simulation: Inferring Hidden Problem State from a Single Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03247)
*Wenshuo Wang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"后验优先"的神经PDE模拟范式：首先推断问题状态的后验分布，再基于该后验进行预测。理论证明了确定性预测存在模糊性屏障，实验表明该方法在PDEBench任务上将滚动RMSE从0.175降至0.132，缩小了59.4%的直推到预言机的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单次观测部署场景下，传统的"场到未来"确定性预测器会将不同的潜在问题状态坍缩到相同的确定性界面，丢失了不确定性信息，导致滚动预测和下游决策不可靠。

Method: 提出后验优先的神经PDE模拟方法：先推断关于最小任务充分问题状态的后验分布，再基于该后验进行条件预测。理论框架连接了对象、学习目标和失败模式，证明贝叶斯下游值可通过后验分解，精炼标签可通过适当评分规则使其可学习，确定性坍缩在真实后验非狄拉克时会产生模糊性屏障。

Result: 在合成精确模糊性实验中，点估计与后验方法的差距跟踪了理论预测的屏障。在元数据隐藏的PDEBench任务上，后验恢复方法将汇总滚动nRMSE从0.175降低到0.132，缩小了59.4%的直推到预言机之间的差距。

Conclusion: 单观测神经PDE模拟应采用后验优先范式，而非传统的确定性场到未来预测，其性能提升显著且具有理论支撑。

Abstract: Neural PDE simulators often receive only a single observed field at deployment. In this setting, a field-to-future predictor can collapse distinct latent problem states into the same deterministic interface, losing the ambiguity needed for reliable rollout and downstream decisions. We propose posterior-first neural PDE simulation: first infer a posterior over the minimal task-sufficient problem state, then condition prediction on that posterior. The resulting theory connects the object, the learning target, and the failure mode: Bayes downstream values factor through this posterior, refinement labels make it learnable by proper scoring rules, and deterministic collapse incurs an ambiguity barrier whenever the true posterior is non-Dirac. Synthetic exact-ambiguity experiments show that point-versus-posterior gaps track the predicted barrier. On metadata-hidden PDEBench tasks, posterior recovery reduces pooled rollout nRMSE from 0.175 to 0.132, closing 59.4% of the direct-to-oracle gap. These results suggest that single-observation neural PDE simulation should be posterior-first rather than monolithic field-to-future prediction.

</details>


### [146] [Ortho-Hydra: Orthogonalized Experts for DiT LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03252)
*Seunghyun Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散Transformer（DiT）在多风格数据上进行LoRA微调时出现的"风格泄露"问题（单一低秩残差无法表示不同艺术家特征，优化器收敛到平均值），本文提出Ortho-Hydra：一种重参数化方法，将OFT风格的Cayley正交共享基与从预训练权重前(Er)个左奇异向量中提取的、每个专家不相交的输出子空间相结合。不相交性使得路由器在步骤0时每个专家的得分非退化，从而在任一专家训练前就能获得梯度信号。实验表明，零初始化的HydraLoRA基线在前1000步无法离开均匀先验，而Ortho-Hydra在几百步内就开始去均匀化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA对扩散Transformer进行多风格数据微调时存在"风格泄露"问题——单一低秩残差无法同时表示多种艺术风格，优化器只能收敛到所有风格的均值。Mixture-of-Experts LoRA（HydraLoRA）虽将上投影替换为多个专家头，但每个专家零初始化时，路由器从每个头接收相同梯度，停留在均匀先验，专家间排列对称演化，退化为单秩r LoRA但训练成本增加E倍。

Method: 提出Ortho-Hydra：1) 采用OFT风格的Cayley正交共享基（可学习的小扰动乘性）；2) 为每个专家分配不相交的输出子空间，这些子空间来自预训练权重前(Er)个左奇异向量；3) 不相交性使得步骤0时路由器的每专家得分非退化，梯度信号在训练前即可传递，打破零初始化时的排列对称性。

Result: 在DiT pipeline上对比：零初始化共享基变体HydraLoRA和原σ=0.1高斯抖动缓解方法在前1000步均无法离开均匀先验；Ortho-Hydra在几百步内就开始去均匀化。端到端生成质量超出范围，主要报告构造、冷启动机制及其改变的路由动态。

Conclusion: Ortho-Hydra通过正交共享基和专家不相交输出子空间的重参数化，有效解决了HydraLoRA中因零初始化导致的路由器冷启动死锁问题，使得专家在训练早期就能实现专业化分工，避免了排列对称演化带来的无效训练。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning of diffusion transformers (DiT) on multi-style data suffers from \emph{style bleed}: a single low-rank residual cannot represent several distinct artist fingerprints, and the optimizer converges to their average. Mixture-of-experts LoRA in the HydraLoRA style replaces the up-projection with $E$ heads under a router, but when every expert is zero-initialized the router receives identical gradient from each head and remains at the uniform prior. The experts then evolve permutation-symmetrically, and the network trains as a single rank-$r$ LoRA at $E{\times}$ the cost. We present \textbf{Ortho-Hydra}, a re-parameterisation that combines an OFT-style Cayley-orthogonal shared basis with per-expert \emph{disjoint output subspaces} carved from the top-$(Er)$ left singular vectors of the pretrained weight. Disjointness makes the router's per-expert score non-degenerate at step~$0$, so specialization receives gradient signal before any expert has trained. We test the predicted deadlock on a DiT pipeline by comparing two HydraLoRA baselines, a zero-initialized shared-basis variant and the original $σ{=}0.1$ Gaussian-jitter mitigation, against Ortho-Hydra under a matched optimiser, dataset, and step budget. Neither baseline leaves the uniform prior within the first $1\text{k}$ steps; Ortho-Hydra begins de-uniformising within the first few hundred. End-task generation quality on multi-style data is out of scope; we report the construction, the cold-start mechanism, and the routing dynamics it changes. Code: https://github.com/sorryhyun/anima_lora.

</details>


### [147] [Do LLMs have core beliefs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03255)
*Anna Sokol,Marianna B. Ganapini,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对抗性对话树（ADTs）框架，发现大多数大语言模型（LLMs）缺乏人类认知中的核心信念（core commitments），即在对话压力下无法维持稳定的世界观，即使是最新模型最终也会在关键承诺上失败，表明当前所有模型都缺乏人类水平认知的关键组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关于LLMs是否具有人类水平认知的辩论中，很少有人关注人类认知的一个结构性组成部分：核心信念——那些为构建世界观提供基础、通常抵抗反驳的真理。本文旨在探究LLMs是否持有类似的核心承诺。

Method: 提出一种名为对抗性对话树（Adversarial Dialogue Trees, ADTs）的探测框架，在五个领域（科学、历史、地理、生物学和数学）中对LLMs进行测试，通过对话压力检验模型是否能维持核心承诺。

Result: 大多数LLMs未能维持稳定的世界观；虽然一些较新的模型表现出改进的稳定性，但它们最终仍在对话压力下未能维持关键承诺。

Conclusion: 研究结果记录了模型世代间论证技能的提升，但表明当前所有模型都缺乏人类水平认知的一个关键组成部分——稳定的核心信念。

Abstract: The rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has sparked debate about whether these systems exhibit human-level cognition. In this debate, little attention has been paid to a structural component of human cognition: core beliefs, truths that provide a foundation around which we can build a worldview. These commitments usually resist debunking, as abandoning them would represent a fundamental shift in how we see reality. In this paper, we ask whether LLMs hold anything akin to core commitments. Using a probing framework we call Adversarial Dialogue Trees (ADTs) over five domains (science, history, geography, biology, and mathematics), we find that most LLMs fail to maintain a stable worldview. Though some recent models showed improved stability, they still eventually failed to maintain key commitments under conversational pressure. These results document an improvement in argumentative skills across model generations but indicate that all current models lack a key component of human-level cognition.

</details>


### [148] [A Universal Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space from Polynomial Alignment and IMQ Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03262)
*Taha Bouhsine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Yat核函数，是一种有理隐单元基元，其单元为Mercer部分，在共享输入/权重空间上具有半正定性、通用性和特征性。通过偏置的二次有限差分，可从三个正偏置Yat原子精确恢复任意IMQ原子，且训练后的Yat层在固定通用特征RKHS中进行有限中心扩展，具有闭式范数和明确的Rademacher泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核方法中，逆多二次(IMQ)核虽具有通用性和特征性，但缺乏非径向对齐通道，限制了其在高维方向性特征学习中的表达能力。Yat核旨在通过有理函数形式结合多项式分子，引入方向性远场迹，克服IMQ的径向限制，同时保持Mercer核的优良性质。

Method: Yat核定义为 $k_{b,\varepsilon}(\mathbf{w},\mathbf{x})=\frac{(\mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x}+b)^2}{\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{w}\|^2+\varepsilon}$，其中 $b\ge 0,\varepsilon>0$。证明了对于 $b\ge 0$ 核是PSD的；当 $b>0$ 时，在Loewner序下支配缩放IMQ核，从而获得固定核通用性、特征性和严格正定性。通过偏置的二次有限差分，三个正偏置Yat原子可精确重构任意IMQ原子。远场迹 $T_\infty g_\varepsilon(\cdot;\mathbf{w},b)(\mathbf{u})=(\mathbf{u}^\top\mathbf{w})^2$ 揭示了非径向对齐能力。

Result: Yat核在Loewner序下支配缩放IMQ核，具有固定核通用性、特征性和严格正定性。代数上，任意IMQ原子可由三个正偏置Yat原子精确恢复。训练后的共享$(b,\varepsilon)$ Yat层等价于固定通用特征RKHS中的有限中心扩展，具有闭式范数 $\boldsymbolα^\top\mathbf{K}\boldsymbolα$ 和显式对角项 $(\|\mathbf{x}\|^2+b)^2/\varepsilon$，可推导Rademacher泛化界。

Conclusion: Yat核提供了一种兼具有理函数灵活性和Mercer核理论保障的核结构，在保持通用性和特征性的同时，通过多项式分子引入方向性对齐能力，且与IMQ核存在精确代数关系。其闭式范数和显式泛化界使其在理论和应用上均具有吸引力。

Abstract: We introduce the Yat kernel $$k_{b,\varepsilon}(\mathbf{w},\mathbf{x})=\frac{(\mathbf{w}^\top\mathbf{x}+b)^2}{\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{w}\|^2+\varepsilon},\qquad b\ge 0,\ \varepsilon>0,$$ a rational hidden-unit primitive whose units are Mercer sections over a shared input/weight space. For $b\ge 0$ the kernel is PSD; for $b>0$ it dominates a scaled inverse-multiquadric (IMQ) in the Loewner order, yielding fixed-kernel universality, characteristicness, and strict positive definiteness on every compact domain. The polynomial numerator opens nonradial alignment channels absent from finite IMQ expansions, witnessed by the directional far-field trace $T_\infty g_\varepsilon(\cdot;\mathbf{w},b)(\mathbf{u})=(\mathbf{u}^\top\mathbf{w})^2$. Algebraically, a second finite difference in the bias recovers any IMQ atom from three positive-bias Yat atoms exactly, sharp at three atoms in every dimension at exact pointwise equality. A trained shared-$(b,\varepsilon)$ Yat layer is therefore a finite learned-center expansion in a fixed universal characteristic RKHS, with closed-form norm $\boldsymbolα^\top\mathbf{K}\boldsymbolα$ and explicit diagonal $(\|\mathbf{x}\|^2+b)^2/\varepsilon$ driving a Rademacher generalization bound.

</details>


### [149] [Partially Observed Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03268)
*Turan Orujlu,Jordan Matelsky,Martin V. Butz,Charley M. Wu,Konrad P. Kording*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出部分观测结构因果模型（POSCMs），将结构因果模型推广至内生图情景，允许节点级、边级上下文和内生变量干预，采用Kolmogorov-Arnold-Sprecher边函数分解实现精确边干预，并在视网膜模拟器中验证了可识别性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构因果模型（SCMs）假设因果图是固定的外生结构，无法处理潜在上下文同时影响图结构和观测变量机制的现实场景，亟需一个能刻画内生图且支持边级干预的统一因果建模框架。

Method: 1) 提出POSCMs框架，扩展SCMs支持内生图；2) 采用Kolmogorov-Arnold-Sprecher边函数分解定理，将每个节点机制表示为父节点单变量函数之和，实现边干预的显式参数化；3) 建立可识别性理论，阐明哪些干预族足以解耦结构形成与机制；4) 在虚拟人视网膜模拟器中设计干预协议进行实证验证。

Result: 1) 当上下文潜伏且无上下文干预时，复现了不可识别性；2) 在潜在边条件下仅观测节点干预时，出现结构-机制混杂；3) 通过目标节点干预成功恢复突触输入-输出关系，与正核可识别性结果一致。

Conclusion: POSCMs将SCMs推广至更接近真实世界的内生图场景，为理解复杂因果系统中结构与机制的相互作用提供了形式化工具和可识别性条件。

Abstract: Here we introduce Partially Observed Structural Causal Models (POSCMs) that formalize causal systems where latent contexts co-determine both the interaction structure and downstream mechanisms on observed variables. POSCMs provide an extension of structural causal models (SCMs), as a self-contained causal modeling framework for endogenous graphs, allowing for an intervention hierarchy spanning node- and edge-level context and endogenous variable interventions. To enable surgical edge interventions, we adopt a Kolmogorov-Arnold-Sprecher edge-functional decomposition, an existence theorem for representing each node mechanism as a sum of univariate functions of its parents, yielding an explicit parametrization of dyadic functional contributions. We provide an identifiability theory that clarifies which intervention families would suffice to disentangle structure formation from mechanisms. We empirically validate these predictions in a biophysically detailed virtual human retina simulator, constructing intervention protocols that (i) reproduce the non-identifiability predicted when context is latent and no context-level interventions are available, (ii) exhibit structure-mechanism confounding under latent edges when only node interventions are observed, and (iii) recover synaptic input-output relationships via targeted node interventions, consistent with our positive kernel identifiability result. Our work generalizes SCMs in a way that allows it to work in a world closer to the one we live in.

</details>


### [150] [Stable Multimodal Graph Unlearning via Feature-Dimension Aware Quantile Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03303)
*Jingjing Zhou,Yongshuai Yang,Qing Qing,Ziqi Xu,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Ivan Lee,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FDQ框架，通过自适应识别高维输入投影层并应用分位数阈值，解决多模态图遗忘学习中因统一参数选择导致的效用退化问题，在保持遗忘效果的同时显著提升模型效用保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图遗忘策略对所有GNN层采用统一的参数选择和编辑方式，这对多模态图尤其有害，因为高维输入投影层编码了关键的跨模态知识，过度编辑这些敏感层会导致遗忘后效用严重退化，破坏稳定学习和隐私保护效果。

Method: 提出FDQ框架，核心包括：(1) 自适应识别高维输入投影层；(2) 基于FDQ引导的分位数阈值对敏感层构建更保守的抑制集；(3) 保持底层重要性估计机制不变；(4) 与基于对角线敏感性的参数重要性分析无缝集成，支持节点和边的遗忘。

Result: 在Ele-Fashion和Goodreads-NC数据集上，FDQ在保持强遗忘效果（抵御成员推理攻击）的同时，显著提升了模型效用保持能力。

Conclusion: FDQ为高维多模态图系统中的隐私感知遗忘提供了一种有原则且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph unlearning remains a critical technique for supporting privacy-preserving and sustainable multimodal graph learning. However, we observe that existing unlearning strategies tend to apply uniform parameter selection and editing across all graph neural network (GNN) layers, which is especially harmful for multimodal graphs where high-dimensional input projections encode dominant cross-modal knowledge. As a result, over-editing these sensitive layers often leads to catastrophic utility degradation after forgetting, undermining both stable learning and effective privacy protection. To address this gap, we propose FDQ, a Feature-Dimension Aware Quantile framework for multimodal graph unlearning. FDQ adaptively identifies high-dimensional input projection layers and applies more conservative, FDQ-guided quantile thresholds when constructing suppression sets, while keeping the underlying importance estimation mechanism unchanged. FDQ is seamlessly integrated with diagonal sensitivity-based parameter importance analysis to enable efficient node and edge unlearning under general forget requests. Through extensive experiments on Ele-Fashion and Goodreads-NC, we demonstrate that FDQ consistently achieves strong utility preservation while maintaining effective forgetting against membership inference attacks. Overall, FDQ offers a principled and robust solution for privacy-aware unlearning in high-dimensional multimodal graph systems.

</details>


### [151] [Distributed Learning with Adversarial Gradient Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03313)
*Nawapon Sangsiri,Yufei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式学习中，客户端返回受距离约束的对抗性梯度扰动时，凸且L-光滑函数学习的最小可实现次优性间隙及查询复杂度，建立了紧的可行性阈值并给出相应算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式学习中，隐私担忧常导致客户端故意篡改梯度信息。本文旨在探究在对抗性梯度扰动下，学习凸且L-光滑函数时两个基本问题：(i) 最小的可实现次优性间隙（即优化中的超额误差）是多少；(ii) 保证给定次优性间隙需要多少查询。

Method: 建立了次优性间隙的紧可行性阈值，并设计了能够达到这些阈值且具有可证明查询复杂度保证的算法。

Result: 给出了次优性间隙的紧阈值，并提供了达到这些阈值的算法及其查询复杂度上界。

Conclusion: 本文为对抗性梯度扰动下的分布式凸优化提供了理论基础，阐明了隐私扰动下可实现的最优精度与所需查询量之间的基本权衡。

Abstract: Privacy concerns in distributed learning often lead clients to return intentionally altered gradient information. We consider the problem of learning convex and $L$-smooth functions under adversarial gradient perturbation, where a client's gradient reply to a server query can deviate arbitrarily from the true gradient subject to a distance bound. Our study focuses on two fundamental questions: (i) what is the smallest achievable sub-optimality gap (i.e., excess error in optimization) under such responses, and (ii) how many queries are sufficient to guarantee a given sub-optimality gap? We establish tight feasibility thresholds on the sub-optimality gap and provide algorithms that achieve these thresholds with provable query complexity guarantees.

</details>


### [152] [DGPO: Distribution Guided Policy Optimization for Fine Grained Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03327)
*Hongbo Jin,Rongpeng Zhu,Zhongjing Du,Xu Jiang,Jingqi Tian,Qiaoman Zhang,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分布引导策略优化（DGPO），一种无评论家的强化学习框架，将分布偏差重新解释为引导信号而非刚性惩罚，以解决序列级信用分配和KL散度梯度不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习算法（如GRPO）存在序列级信用分配粗粒度、无法隔离关键推理步骤的问题，且标准无界KL散度惩罚导致梯度不稳定和模式搜索保守性，限制了新推理轨迹的发现。

Method: 提出分布引导策略优化（DGPO），一种无评论家的强化学习框架，将分布偏差重新解释为引导信号而非刚性惩罚。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果。

Conclusion: DGPO通过将分布偏差作为引导信号而非惩罚，有望解决信用分配和梯度不稳定问题，促进新推理轨迹的探索。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is crucial for aligning large language models to perform complex reasoning tasks. However, current algorithms such as Group Relative Policy Optimization suffer from coarse grained, sequence level credit assignment, which severely struggles to isolate pivotal reasoning steps within long Chain of Thought generations. Furthermore, the standard unbounded Kullback Leibler divergence penalty induces severe gradient instability and mode seeking conservatism, ultimately stifling the discovery of novel reasoning trajectories. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Distribution Guided Policy Optimization, a novel critic free reinforcement learning framework that reinterprets distribution deviation as a guiding signal rather than a rigid penalty.

</details>


### [153] [LLM-ADAM: A Generalizable LLM Agent Framework for Pre-Print Anomaly Detection in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03328)
*Ahmadreza Eslaminia,Chuhan Cai,Cameron Smith,Ruo-Syuan Mei,Shichen Li,Rajiv Malhotra,Klara Nahrstedt,Chenhui Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-ADAM，一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的通用框架，用于增材制造(AM)中的打印前G代码异常检测。该框架将任务分解为三个角色：Extractor-LLM提取G代码参数、Reference-LLM将打印机与材料文档转化为操作范围、Judge-LLM判断缺陷类别。在200个FFF G代码样本上评估，最优配置达87.5%准确率，远高于单LLM基线的59.5%，表明结构化分解是主要改进来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFF打印的普及让非专业用户也能使用增材制造，但这些用户缺乏制造专业知识，容易生成含有热学或几何错误的分层配置文件，或通过微小的G代码编辑引入问题。打印前G代码筛查可避免材料和机器时间的浪费，但现有方法缺乏通用性和结构化能力。

Method: 提出LLM-ADAM框架，将G代码异常检测分解为三个LLM角色：(1) Extractor-LLM将G代码映射为结构化工艺参数；(2) Reference-LLM将打印机和材料文档转化为可对齐的操作范围；(3) Judge-LLM基于偏差表和G代码证据判定缺陷类别。打印机、材料和LLM主干均可替换，作为测试条件而非固定假设。

Result: 在N=200的FFF G代码语料库上评估，涵盖两种桌面打印机、两种材料和五类样本（无缺陷、欠挤出、过挤出、翘曲、拉丝）。最优配置达87.5%准确率，而最强单LLM基线仅59.5%。缺陷类别识别接近或达到上限，残余错误主要集中在对无缺陷样本的保守误报。

Conclusion: 结构化任务分解（而非单纯增强LLM主干能力）是性能提升的主导因素。LLM-ADAM框架通过多角色协作实现了高效、可泛化的打印前异常检测，显著优于单LLM基线。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing (AM) continues to transform modern manufacturing by enabling flexible, on-demand production of complex geometries across diverse industries. Fused filament fabrication (FFF) has extended AM to laboratories, classrooms, and small production environments, but this accessibility shifts process-planning responsibility to users who may lack manufacturing expertise. A syntactically valid slicer profile can still encode thermally or geometrically harmful settings, and subtle G-code edits can alter extrusion, cooling, or adhesion before a print begins. Pre-print G-code screening catches accidental or adversarial machine-program errors before material or machine time is wasted. This paper proposes LLM-ADAM as a generalizable LLM framework for pre-print anomaly detection in AM. The framework decomposes the task into three roles: Extractor-LLM maps a G-code file to a structured process-parameter schema; Reference-LLM converts printer and material documentation into aligned operating ranges; and Judge-LLM interprets a deterministic deviation table and G-code evidence to decide whether a part is non-defective or belongs to an anomaly class. Printers, materials, and LLM backbones are interchangeable test conditions, not fixed assumptions. We evaluate the framework on an N=200 FFF G-code corpus spanning two desktop printer families, two materials, and five classes including non-defective, under-extrusion, over-extrusion, warping, and stringing. The best framework configuration reaches 87.5% accuracy, compared with 59.5% for the strongest engineered single-LLM baseline. The results show that structured decomposition, rather than backbone strength alone, is the dominant source of improvement, with defect classes identified at or near ceiling for leading configurations while residual errors concentrate on conservative false alarms for non-defective samples.

</details>


### [154] [Toward Structural Multimodal Representations: Specialization, Selection, and Sparsification via Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03348)
*Hahyeon Choi,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S3提出了一种结构化多模态学习框架，通过语义专家分解、选择性路由和稀疏化剪枝，在MultiBench上取得优于对比学习和InfoMax方法的性能，并发现中间稀疏度达到最佳性能的倒U型规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态学习通常将所有信号编码为固定嵌入，缺乏结构化的语义分解能力，难以适应不同任务的特异性需求。

Method: S3框架包含三个步骤：Specialization（在共享潜空间中形成概念级语义专家）、Selection（根据任务需求自适应路由专家）、Sparsification（剪枝低效用路径以获得紧凑的信息最小化表示）。

Result: 在四个MultiBench基准上，S3提升了准确率，并展现出稳定的倒U型稀疏-性能趋势，在中间稀疏度达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: 将多模态表示结构化为可选择的语义组分是一种实用且有原则的替代方案，优于对比学习和InfoMax驱动的方法。

Abstract: We propose S3 (Specialization, Selection, Sparsification), a framework that rethinks multimodal learning through a structural perspective. Instead of encoding all signals into a fixed embedding, S3 decomposes multimodal inputs into semantic experts and selectively routes them for each task. Specialization forms concept-level experts in a shared latent space, Selection adapts routing for task-specific needs, and Sparsification prunes low-utility paths to yield compact, information-minimal representations. Across four MultiBench benchmarks, S3 improves accuracy and shows a consistent reverse U-shaped sparsity-performance trend, with peak performance at intermediate sparsity. These results suggest that structuring multimodal representations as selectable semantic components provides a practical and principled alternative to contrastive learning or InfoMax-driven approaches.

</details>


### [155] [Fully Automatic Trace Gas Plume Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03372)
*Vít Růžička,David R. Thompson,Jay E. Fahlen,Amanda M. Lopez,Steven Lu,Chuchu Xiang,Holly Bender,Daniel Jensen,Philip G. Brodrick,Jake Lee,Brian Bue,Daniel H. Cusworth,Luis Guanter,Adam Chlus,Andrew Thorpe,Robert O. Green*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合机器学习形态分析与物理光谱拟合的全自动框架，用于在EMIT成像光谱仪数据中检测痕量气体羽流（CH₄、NH₃、NO₂、CO），运行于"每日摘要"自动检测与"回溯分析"两种模式，可自动检测大部分大型羽流且假阳性极低，并发现约25%的羽流在人工审查中被遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来成像光谱仪数据量将呈数量级增长，需要无需人工参与的全自动痕量气体点源检测方法，以克服人工审查的瓶颈和遗漏问题。

Method: 将基于机器学习的形态学分析与基于物理的光谱拟合相结合，构建全自动检测框架。在EMIT数据上运行两种模式：每日摘要（自动处理所有下行数据，标记最大事件）和回溯分析（识别此前人工审查遗漏的羽流）。

Result: 每日摘要模式能以极低假阳性率自动检测大部分大型羽流；回溯分析表明至少25%的羽流被人工审查遗漏；除甲烷外，扩展检测了NH₃、NO₂以及EMIT影像中首次观测到的CO羽流。

Conclusion: 该全自动框架可有效替代人工审查进行痕量气体羽流检测，显著提高检测覆盖率，并扩展了可检测的气体种类。

Abstract: Future imaging spectrometers will increase data volumes by orders of magnitude, requiring automated detection of trace gas point sources. We present a fully automated framework that combines machine learning-based morphological analysis with physics-based spectroscopic fitting to detect plumes without human participation. Applied to EMIT imaging spectrometer data, the system operates in two modes: "daily digest" that runs automatically on all downlinked data, flagging the largest events for immediate response, and a retrospective analysis that identifies plumes missed by prior human review. The daily digest demonstrates that a significant fraction of the largest plumes can be detected automatically with negligible false positives, while retrospective analysis suggests at least 25% of plumes may have been overlooked. In addition to the previously observed methane point sources, we extend detection to three understudied trace gases: NH3, NO2 and the first observations of carbon monoxide (CO) plume in EMIT imagery.

</details>


### [156] [Learning Dynamics of Zeroth-Order Optimization: A Kernel Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03373)
*Zhe Li,Bicheng Ying,Zidong Liu,Haibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过神经正切核（NTK）分析揭示了零阶随机梯度下降（ZO SGD）在大语言模型微调中有效性的理论依据：ZO SGD的学习动力学由经验神经正切核（eNTK）控制，且该核的近似误差取决于模型输出维度而非参数维度，从而解释了ZO方法在高维参数空间中的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典优化理论认为零阶算法因维度依赖而收敛缓慢，与近年来ZO方法成功微调数十亿参数LLM的实践相矛盾，亟需理论解释。

Method: 推导ZO SGD的单步学习动力学，发现eNTK自然出现为控制学习行为的关键项；通过Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理证明ZO eNTK的近似误差主要由扰动次数决定，且依赖于模型输出大小而非参数维度。

Result: ZO eNTK的保真度由扰动数量主导，近似误差与模型输出维度相关，与参数维度无关，呈现维度无关性。

Conclusion: 该维度无关性为ZO方法在LLM微调中的可扩展性提供了理论正当性，并提出了理解ZO方法学习动力学的新视角。

Abstract: Classical optimization theory establishes that zeroth-order (ZO) algorithms suffer from a dimension-dependent slowdown, with convergence rates typically scaling with the model dimension compared to first-order methods. However, in contrast to these theoretical expectations, a growing body of recent work demonstrates the successful application of ZO methods to fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) with billions of parameters. To explain this paradox, we derive the one-step learning dynamics of ZO SGD, where the empirical Neural Tangent Kernel (eNTK) naturally emerges as the key term governing the learning behavior. Inspection of the eNTK produced by ZO SGD reveals that each element corresponds to the inner product of neural tangent vectors projected onto a random low-dimensional subspace. Thus, by invoking the Johnson-Lindenstrauss Lemma, our analysis shows that the fidelity of the ZO eNTK is governed primarily by the number of perturbations. Crucially, the approximation error depends on the model output size rather than the massive parameter dimension. This dimension-free property provides a theoretical justification for the scalability of ZO methods to LLMs finetuning tasks. We believe that this kernel-based framework offers a novel perspective for understanding ZO methods within the context of learning dynamics.

</details>


### [157] [GRAFT: Auditing Graph Neural Networks via Global Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03377)
*Rishi Raj Sahoo,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRAFT框架，一种后置全局解释方法，通过多样性引导的示例选择、集成梯度归因和聚合，为GNN生成类别级特征重要性画像，并利用大语言模型将之转化为自然语言规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN全局解释器仅从结构层面识别重复子图模式，而无法在输入节点属性层面全局解释模型行为，缺乏对特征级重要性的全局理解。

Method: 结合多样性引导的示例选择、基于集成梯度的归因和聚合，构建每个类别的全局特征影响视图，再通过带自优化的大语言模型生成简洁的自然语言规则。

Result: 在多个数据集、架构和实验设置下证明了该方法能有效捕获模型相关特征、支持偏差分析以及实现特征高效的迁移学习；引入结构化人工评估协议验证了生成规则的可解释性。

Conclusion: GRAFT为分析GNN中的特征级行为提供了一种实用且可解释的方法，弥合了定量归因与人类可理解解释之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve strong performance on node classification tasks but remain difficult to interpret, particularly with respect to which input features drive their predictions. Existing global GNN explainers operate at the structural level identifying recurring subgraph motifs, but none explain model behaviour globally at the level of input node attributes. We propose GRAFT, a posthoc global explanation framework that identifies class-level feature importance profiles for GNNs. The method combines diversity-guided exemplar selection, Integrated Gradients-based attribution, and aggregation to construct a global view of feature influence for each class, which can be further expressed as concise natural language rules using a large language model with self-refinement. We evaluate GRAFT across multiple datasets, architectures, and experimental settings, demonstrating its effectiveness in capturing model-relevant features, supporting bias analysis, and enabling feature-efficient transfer learning. In addition, we introduce a structured human evaluation protocol to assess the interpretability of generated rules along dimensions such as accuracy and usefulness. Our results suggest that GRAFT provides a practical and interpretable approach for analysing feature-level behaviour in GNNs, bridging quantitative attribution with human-understandable explanations.

</details>


### [158] [Local Truncation Error-Guided Neural ODEs for Large Scale Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03386)
*Xiao Zhang,Yafei Li,Ruixiang Wang,Wei Wei,Shuo He,Mingliang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LTE-ODE，通过将局部截断误差（LTE）重新利用为无监督前向归纳偏置，生成动态空间注意力掩码，在稳定区域保持高精度连续ODE演化，同时在突变点自适应触发离散补偿分支，解决了神经ODE在时空预测中因Lipschitz连续性约束导致的过度平滑与异常感知缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经ODE在物理系统时空预测中面临"连续性-突变"困境：其Lipschitz连续性约束在遭遇离散、不可预测的微观突变时会导致严重过平滑；现有物理信息方法通过惩罚数值积分误差来强制流形平滑，但数学上会引发梯度冲突和"注意力崩溃"，降低模型对异常的敏感性。

Method: 将局部截断误差（LTE）重新利用为无监督前向归纳偏置，映射为动态空间注意力掩码：在稳定区域保持高精度连续ODE演化，在突变点自适应触发离散补偿分支。模型纯端到端训练，无需流形惩罚项。

Result: 在多个大规模基准数据集上达到最先进性能，对高度非线性波动表现出卓越鲁棒性；消融实验表明模型在积分步长方面具有高部署灵活性，可无缝适应不同硬件内存约束。

Conclusion: LTE-ODE通过将数值误差从"需消除的噪声"重新定义为"可指导的归纳偏置"，有效统一了连续宏观节律与离散微观突变的建模，为解决时空预测中的连续性-突变困境提供了新范式。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal forecasting in physical systems, such as large-scale traffic networks, requires modeling a dual dynamic: continuous macroscopic rhythms and discrete, unpredictable microscopic shocks. While Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) excel at capturing smooth evolution, their inherent Lipschitz continuity constraints inevitably cause severe over-smoothing when confronting abrupt anomalies. Recent physics-informed methods attempt to bypass this by penalizing numerical integration errors to enforce manifold smoothness. However, we mathematically reveal that such rigid regularization inherently triggers gradient conflicts and ``attention collapse,'' stripping the model of its sensitivity to anomalies. To resolve this continuity-shock dilemma, we propose Local Truncation Error-Guided Neural ODEs (LTE-ODE). Rather than treating numerical error as a nuisance to be eliminated, we innovatively repurpose the Local Truncation Error (LTE) as an unsupervised forward inductive bias. By mapping the LTE into a dynamic spatial attention mask, our architecture gracefully preserves high-precision continuous ODE evolution in stable regions, while adaptively triggering a discrete compensation branch exclusively at shock points. Trained purely end-to-end without manifold penalties, LTE-ODE achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple large-scale benchmarks, exhibiting exceptional robustness against highly non-linear fluctuations. Furthermore, our ablation on integration steps demonstrates high deployment flexibility, allowing the model to seamlessly adapt to varying hardware memory constraints in real-world applications.

</details>


### [159] [Structured Analytic Coherent Point Drift for Non-Rigid Point Set Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00934)
*Wei Feng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Analytic-CPD，一种结构化解析变体的相干点漂移非刚性配准方法。该方法保留CPD的后验对应层，但用有限维结构化解析映射估计器替代点索引高斯核位移场M-step。通过重心恒等式将后验概率凝聚为加权软目标点，将CPD软对应目标转化为加权解析拟合问题。变形由向量值函数的截断多元泰勒映射表示，变形参数数量由环境维度和解析阶数控制。引入度数延续策略，通过逐步激活高阶解析模式稳定大变形配准。实验表明，在大变形场景下，Analytic-CPD比标准CPD实现更低误差和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CPD使用点索引高斯核表示位移场，其变形参数数量受限于M×M核系统，在大变形场景下计算效率低且可解释性差。本文旨在用结构化解析映射替代核方法，实现更紧凑、可解释且高效的变形表示。

Method: 1) 保留CPD的高斯混合模型后验对应层；2) 通过重心恒等式将后验概率凝聚为加权软目标点，将软对应目标转化为加权解析拟合；3) 用截断多元泰勒映射表示变形场，参数数量仅由维度d和解析阶数k控制（O(d^k)而非O(M²)）；4) 引入度数延续策略，逐步激活高阶项以稳定大变形配准。

Result: 在二维解析变形和三维平滑非解析变形数据集上，Analytic-CPD相比标准CPD在大变形场景中实现更低的最终配准误差和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CPD风格的概率对应与结构化解析映射的结合，为核方法非刚性配准提供了紧凑且可解释的替代方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce Analytic-CPD, a structured analytic variant of coherent point drift for non-rigid point set registration. The method retains the CPD posterior correspondence layer, but replaces the point-indexed Gaussian-kernel displacement-field M-step with a finite-dimensional structured analytic mapping estimator. Posterior probabilities from the Gaussian mixture model are condensed through a barycentric identity into weighted soft target points, converting the CPD pairwise soft-correspondence objective into a weighted analytic fitting problem. The deformation is represented by a truncated multivariate Taylor mapping of a vector-valued function, so the number of deformation parameters is controlled by the ambient dimension and the analytic order rather than by an M-by-M kernel system over the moving points. A degree-continuation strategy is further introduced to stabilize large-deformation registration by progressively activating higher-order analytic modes. Experiments on two-dimensional analytic deformations and three-dimensional smooth non-analytic deformations show that Analytic-CPD achieves lower final errors and faster convergence than standard CPD in representative large-deformation settings. The results suggest that CPD-style probabilistic correspondences and structured analytic mappings provide a compact and interpretable alternative to kernel-based non-rigid registration. Code is available at https://github.com/monge-ampere/Analytic-CPD.

</details>


### [160] [PODiff: Latent Diffusion in Proper Orthogonal Decomposition Space for Scientific Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03399)
*Onkar Jadhav,Tim French,Matthew Rayson,Nicole L. Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PODiff提出了一种基于本征正交分解（POD）系数空间的扩散模型框架，用于高维空间场的概率超分辨率重建。通过利用POD模态的正交性构建方差排序的潜空间，该方法在显著降低计算成本的同时，保持了与像素空间扩散相当的重建精度，并生成了更可靠的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间场的概率超分辨率重建中，直接在像素空间运行扩散模型的计算成本过高，需要一种能够在保持重建质量的同时大幅降低计算开销的方法。

Method: 提出PODiff框架，将扩散过程从像素空间转移到固定、方差排序的本征正交分解（POD）系数空间中执行。利用POD模态的正交性构建可解释、方差排序的潜在几何结构。

Result: 在澳大利亚西海岸海面温度降尺度任务和受控的对流扩散基准测试中，PODiff实现了与像素空间扩散相当的重建精度，同时显著降低了内存需求，并生成了比确定性方法和蒙特卡洛Dropout基线更可靠的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: PODiff通过POD系数空间的扩散设计，实现了高效集成生成、保持主导空间结构，并以更低计算成本提供空间可解释、良好校准的不确定性，是一种有效的结构化条件生成框架。

Abstract: Probabilistic super-resolution of high-dimensional spatial fields using diffusion models is often computationally prohibitive due to the cost of operating directly in pixel space. We propose PODiff, a structured conditional generative framework that performs diffusion in a fixed, variance-ordered Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) coefficient space, exploiting the orthogonality of POD modes to impose an interpretable, variance-ordered latent geometry. This design enables efficient ensemble generation, preserves dominant spatial structure, and yields spatially interpretable, well-calibrated uncertainty at substantially lower computational cost. We evaluate PODiff on sea surface temperature downscaling over the West Australian coast and on a controlled advection-diffusion benchmark. PODiff achieves reconstruction accuracy comparable to pixel-space diffusion while requiring significantly less memory and producing more reliable uncertainty estimates than deterministic and Monte Carlo Dropout baselines.

</details>


### [161] [Discovering Reinforcement Learning Interfaces with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03408)
*Akshat Singh Jaswal,Ashish Baghel,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LIMEN框架，利用大语言模型引导的进化方法，从原始模拟器状态中自动联合发现强化学习任务接口（观测映射和奖励函数），实验表明联合设计优于单独优化任一组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建强化学习系统所需的观测和奖励函数接口通常需要大量人工工作，现有自动化方法只关注奖励设计而假设观测固定，缺乏从原始状态中合成完整任务接口的方案。

Method: 提出LIMEN框架，利用大语言模型引导的进化算法，将候选接口表示为可执行程序，并通过策略训练反馈迭代优化，联合进化观测映射和奖励函数。

Result: 在离散网格世界任务和连续控制域（ locomotion和manipulation）中，联合进化观测和奖励能够基于轨迹级成功指标发现有效接口；单独优化任一组分在至少一个域上失败。

Conclusion: 从原始状态自动构建RL接口能大幅减少人工工程，且观测和奖励组件的协同设计至关重要，单组件优化会导致灾难性失败。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning systems rely on environment interfaces that specify observations and reward functions, yet constructing these interfaces for new tasks often requires substantial manual effort. While recent work has automated reward design using large language models (LLMs), these approaches assume fixed observations and do not address the broader challenge of synthesizing complete task interfaces. We study RL task interface discovery from raw simulator state, where both observation mappings and reward functions must be generated. We propose LIMEN (Code available at https://github.com/Lossfunk/LIMEN), a LLM guided evolutionary framework that produces candidate interfaces as executable programs and iteratively refines them using policy training feedback. Across novel discrete gridworld tasks and continuous control domains spanning locomotion and manipulation, joint evolution of observations and rewards discovers effective interfaces given only a trajectory-level success metric, while optimizing either component alone fails on at least one domain. These results demonstrate that automatic construction of RL interfaces from raw state can substantially reduce manual engineering and that observation and reward components often benefit from co-design, as single-component optimization fails catastrophically on at least one domain in our evaluation suite.

</details>


### [162] [Learning to Theorize the World from Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03413)
*Doojin Baek,Gyubin Lee,Junyeob Baek,Hosung Lee,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Learning-to-Theorize范式及其实例化模型NEO，通过将世界理解转化为构建可执行、组合性的潜在程序（作为学到的思维语言），从原始非文本观测中归纳显式解释性理论，实现解释驱动的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常将理解操作化为在潜在空间或观测空间中的准确未来预测，而发展认知科学表明人类理解通过构建关于世界如何运作的内部理论而涌现。受这一理论构建认知观的启发，作者希望从原始非文本观测中归纳显式解释性理论。

Method: 提出Learning-to-Theorize学习范式，并实例化为Neural Theorizer (NEO)——一个概率神经模型，它诱导潜在程序作为学到的思维语言，并通过共享的转移模型执行这些程序。理论被表示为可执行的组合性程序，其学到的基元可以系统地重新组合以解释新现象。

Result: 实验表明该公式能够实现解释驱动的泛化，使观测能够根据生成它们的程序来理解。

Conclusion: 从认知科学中的理论构建视角出发，将世界理解建模为从原始观测中学习显式可组合的潜在程序理论，能够实现更好的解释驱动泛化，为世界模型提供了不同于纯预测范式的新路径。

Abstract: What does it mean to understand the world? Contemporary world models often operationalize understanding as accurate future prediction in latent or observation space. Developmental cognitive science, however, suggests a different view: human understanding emerges through the construction of internal theories of how the world works, even before mature language is acquired. Inspired by this theory-building view of cognition, we introduce Learning-to-Theorize, a learning paradigm for inferring explicit explanatory theories of the world from raw, non-textual observations. We instantiate this paradigm with the Neural Theorizer (NEO), a probabilistic neural model that induces latent programs as a learned Language of Thought and executes them through a shared transition model. In NEO, a theory is represented as an executable, compositional program whose learned primitives can be systematically recombined to explain novel phenomena. Experiments show that this formulation enables explanation-driven generalization, allowing observations to be understood in terms of the programs that generate them.

</details>


### [163] [FIBER: A Differentially Private Optimizer with Filter-Aware Innovation Bias Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03425)
*Duc Dm,Thao Do,Minh Son Hoang,Anh Le Duc Tran,Daeyoung Kim,Huy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FiBeR是一种为时间滤波私有化梯度设计的差分隐私优化器，通过创新空间去噪、解耦观测几何与创新增益、以及滤波感知的二阶矩校准，在视觉和语言任务上显著超越了现有最先进的DP优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DP训练方法在梯度中加入噪声以保护隐私，但噪声与自适应优化器（如AdamW）存在非平凡交互。时间滤波虽能降低方差，却改变了DP噪声统计特性，导致针对未滤波噪声的偏差校正（如减去sigma_w^2）失效。

Method: FiBeR提出三种核心设计：(i) 在创新空间中进行去噪，通过滤波残差流并积分形成滤波梯度估计；(ii) 将两点观测几何与创新增益解耦，实现独立调参；(iii) 引入滤波感知的二阶矩校准，减去衰减后的DP噪声贡献A(omega) sigma_w^2，其中A(omega)对创新滤波器有闭式解，对一般稳定线性滤波器可计算。

Result: 在视觉和语言基准任务上，FiBeR在相同隐私约束下持续显著提升DP优化器性能，多个任务上超越当前最优结果。

Conclusion: FiBeR通过创新空间滤波和解耦设计有效解决了时间滤波与DP噪声之间的校准问题，为差分隐私优化提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) training protects individual examples by adding noise to gradients, but the injected noise interacts nontrivially with adaptive optimizers. Recent DP methods temporally filter privatized gradients to reduce variance; however, filtering also changes the DP noise statistics seen by AdamW's second-moment accumulator. As a result, bias corrections derived for unfiltered DP noise, such as subtracting sigma_w squared, can become miscalibrated when filtering is present.
  We propose FiBeR, a DP optimizer designed for temporally filtered privatized gradients. FiBeR (i) performs denoising in innovation space by filtering the residual stream and integrating it to form the filtered gradient estimate, (ii) decouples the two-point observation geometry from the innovation gain to enable independent tuning, and (iii) introduces a filter-aware second-moment calibration that subtracts the attenuated DP noise contribution A(omega) sigma_w squared, where A(omega) is derived in closed form for the innovation filter and can be computed for general stable linear filters.
  Across vision and language benchmarks, FiBeR consistently demonstrates substantial improvements in the performance of DP optimizers, surpassing state-of-the-art results under equivalent privacy constraints on multiple tasks.

</details>


### [164] [DynaTab: Dynamic Feature Ordering as Neural Rewiring for High-Dimensional Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03430)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Gianfranco Doretto,Donald A. Adjeroh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaTab提出了一种动态特征排序机制，通过神经重连算法为高维表格数据学习最优特征顺序，结合位置编码、重要性门控和掩码注意力层，在36个真实数据集上击败45个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维表格数据缺乏天然的特征顺序，限制了置换敏感性深度学习模型（如Transformer）的应用效果，现有方法通常依赖固定或随机特征排列，无法自适应数据内在复杂度。

Method: 提出轻量级准则预测特征置换对数据集的收益（基于内在复杂度量化）；通过神经重连算法动态重排特征顺序；设计包含位置嵌入、重要性门控和掩码注意力的顺序感知组合架构；引入动态特征排序（DFO）损失和分散损失进行端到端训练。

Result: 在36个真实表格数据集上对比45个SOTA基线方法，在高维数据集上取得统计显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: DynaTab为高维表格深度学习提供了一种新的范式，通过动态特征排序有效克服了固定顺序的局限性。

Abstract: High-dimensional tabular data lacks a natural feature order, limiting the applicability of permutation-sensitive deep learning models. We propose DynaTab, a dynamic feature ordering-enabled architecture inspired by neural rewiring. We introduce a lightweight criterion that predicts when feature permutation will benefit a dataset by quantifying its intrinsic complexity. DynaTab dynamically reorders features via a neural rewiring algorithm and processes them through a compact, dynamic order-aware combination of separate learned positional embedding, importance-based gating, and masked attention layers, compatible with any sequence-sensitive backbone. Trained end-to-end with bespoke dynamic feature ordering (DFO) and dispersion losses, DynaTab achieves statistically significant gains, particularly on high-dimensional datasets, where it is benchmarked against 45 state-of-the-art baselines across 36 different real-world tabular datasets. Our results position DynaTab as a compelling new paradigm for high-dimensional tabular deep learning.

</details>


### [165] [Quantum Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning via Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03434)
*Yu-Ting Lee,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Fu-Chieh Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于选项-评论家架构的混合分层强化学习智能体，用量子变分电路替代经典组件（特征提取器、选项价值函数、终止函数、内部选项策略）。实验表明，使用量子特征提取器的混合智能体在节省高达66%可训练参数的情况下超越了经典基线，但量子选项价值估计会严重降低性能。消融研究揭示了量子电路架构选择对性能的影响，为参数高效的混合分层智能体建立了设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子参数化计算在非分层强化学习中已显示出优势，但这些优势能否扩展到分层决策场景仍是一个关键未解问题。因此，本文旨在探究量子计算在分层强化学习中的适用性。

Method: 基于选项-评论家架构开发混合分层智能体，用量子变分电路替换特征提取器、选项价值函数、终止函数和内部选项策略，并在标准基准环境中进行评估。

Result: 量子特征提取器的混合智能体超越经典基线并节省66%可训练参数；量子选项价值估计严重降低性能；消融研究揭示了量子电路架构选择的影响。

Conclusion: 建立了参数高效的混合分层智能体的设计原则，指出量子特征提取器是有效组件，而量子选项价值估计存在瓶颈。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is one of the most challenging learning paradigms where efficacy and efficiency gains are extremely valuable. Hierarchical reinforcement learning is a variant that leverages temporal abstraction to structure decision-making. While parametrized quantum computations have shown success in non-hierarchical reinforcement learning, whether these advantages adapt to hierarchical decision-making remains a critical open question. In this work, we develop a hybrid hierarchical agent based on the option-critic architecture. This hybrid agent substitutes classical components with variational quantum circuits for feature extractors, option-value functions, termination functions, and intra-option policies. Evaluated on standard benchmarking environments, results show that a hybrid agent utilizing a quantum feature extractor can outperform classical baselines while saving up to 66\% trainable parameters. We also identify an architectural bottleneck that quantum option-value estimation severely degrades performance. Further ablation studies reveal how architectural choices of the quantum circuits affect performance. Our work establishes design principles for parameter-efficient hybrid hierarchical agents.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Generalizable Action Representations via Pre-training AEMG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03462)
*Zhenghao Huang,Huilin Yao,Kaikai Wang,Lin Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AEMG，首个大规模自监督EMG表征学习框架，将肌电信号重新概念化为跨设备生理语言，通过神经肌肉收缩分词器将肌电信号转化为结构化语言，在零样本跨被试和少样本跨设备任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMG信号在解码人类运动意图中发挥关键作用，但其跨被试、跨设备、跨任务的泛化能力受到数据异质性、标签稀缺以及缺乏统一表征框架的严重限制。

Method: 提出AEMG框架，核心包括：1) 神经肌肉收缩分词器(NCT)，将离散肌肉收缩翻译为结构词，将时间激活模式转化为连贯句子；2) 构建迄今最大的跨设备EMG信号词汇表，实现任意通道拓扑和采样率下的无缝迁移；3) 采用自监督表征学习范式进行大规模预训练。

Result: 在零样本留一被试(LOSO)设置下，相较六个SOTA基线提升5.79%-9.25%准确率；仅用5%目标用户数据即可实现90%以上的少样本适应性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出了EMG信号作为跨设备生理语言的概念，从海量数据中学习其语法，为单次训练、通用可用的EMG基础模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: A fundamental role in decoding human motor intent and enabling intuitive human-computer interaction is played by electromyography (EMG). However, its generalization capability across subjects, devices, and tasks remains substantially limited by data heterogeneity, label scarcity, and the lack of a unified representational framework. To bridge this gap, we propose Any Electromyography (AEMG), the first large-scale, self-supervised representation learning framework for EMG. AEMG reconceptualizes neuromuscular dynamics linguistically, utilizing a novel Neuromuscular Contraction Tokenizer (NCT) to translate discrete muscle contractions into structural words and temporal activation patterns into coherent sentences. Furthermore, we compile the largest cross-device EMG signal vocabulary to date, enabling seamless transfer across arbitrary channel topologies and sampling rates. Experiments demonstrate that AEMG improves the zero-shot leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) accuracy by 5.79-9.25% compared to six state-of-the-art baselines, and achieves more than 90% few-shot adaptation performance with only 5% of target user data. Our work has proposed the concept of EMG signals as a cross-device physiological language, learned their grammar from massive amounts of data, and laid the groundwork for a single-training, universally applicable EMG foundation model.

</details>


### [167] [Bandits on graphs and structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03493)
*Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究序贯决策问题中的结构性质，重点关注图结构（图赌博机）和大动作空间（核赌博机、多拟阵赌博机等），旨在将理论解推向实际应用，并作为作者在图与结构赌博机领域贡献的综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了将序贯决策问题的理论解推向实际应用，需要深入挖掘问题的结构性质，特别是图表示的结构和大规模（指数级甚至无穷）动作空间的结构。

Method: 第一部分研究图赌博机，涵盖奖励平滑性（谱赌博机）、侧观察和影响力最大化；第二部分研究大规模结构化域，涵盖核赌博机、多拟阵赌博机、未知平滑性函数优化赌博机和无穷多臂赌博机。

Result: 对图结构与大规模结构化赌博机问题进行了系统性的结构分析，并提出相应方法使理论解更贴近实际应用。

Conclusion: 该论文是对作者在图赌博机和结构赌博机领域研究成果的综述，强调了利用问题结构性质来桥接理论与实践的重要性。

Abstract: The goal of this thesis is to investigate the structural properties of certain sequential problems in order to bring the solutions closer to a practical use. In the first part, we put a special emphasis on structures that can be represented as graphs on actions. In the second part, we study the large action spaces that can be of exponential size in the number of base actions or even infinite. For graph bandits, we consider the settings of smoothness of rewards (spectral bandits), side observations, and influence maximization. For large structured domains, we cover kernel bandits, polymatroid bandits, bandits for function optimization (including unknown smoothness), and infinitely many-arms bandits. The thesis aspires to be a survey of the author's contributions on graph and structured bandits.

</details>


### [168] [Adaptive graph-based algorithms for conditional anomaly detection and semi-supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03495)
*Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于图的半监督学习方法，包括一种快速近似在线标签传播算法（通过局部代表点压缩和正则化谐波解），以及一种基于图的条件异常检测方法（解决边缘点和孤立点问题），并在医院临床行为异常检测中通过15位专家进行了人工评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的半监督学习方法在大规模数据或流数据场景下面临计算和存储瓶颈；同时，标准异常检测框架无法有效处理条件异常中的边缘点和孤立点问题。

Method: 1) 快速近似在线算法：将附近点压缩为局部代表点以最小化失真，并正则化谐波解以提升稳定性。2) 条件异常检测：基于图连通性分析和软谐波解的非参数图方法，解决边缘点和孤立点问题。

Result: 理论和实验均表明所提方法具有良好的性能；条件异常检测方法由15位重症监护专家进行了人工评估。

Conclusion: 基于图的方法在半监督学习和条件异常检测中均有效，特别是通过代表点压缩和正则化可实现高效在线学习，且条件异常检测可用于医院临床错误预警。

Abstract: We develop graph-based methods for semi-supervised learning based on label propagation on a data similarity graph. When data is abundant or arrive in a stream, the problems of computation and data storage arise for any graph-based method. We propose a fast approximate online algorithm that solves for the harmonic solution on an approximate graph. We show, both empirically and theoretically, that good behavior can be achieved by collapsing nearby points into a set of local representative points that minimize distortion. Moreover, we regularize the harmonic solution to achieve better stability properties. We also present graph-based methods for detecting conditional anomalies and apply them to the identification of unusual clinical actions in hospitals. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to the past patients may be due to errors and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. Conditional anomaly detection extends standard unconditional anomaly framework but also faces new problems known as fringe and isolated points. We devise novel nonparametric graph-based methods to tackle these problems. Our methods rely on graph connectivity analysis and soft harmonic solution. Finally, we conduct an extensive human evaluation study of our conditional anomaly methods by 15 experts in critical care.

</details>


### [169] [Bandits attack function optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03496)
*Philippe Preux,Rémi Munos,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为同时乐观优化（SOO）的确定性算法，将函数优化建模为预算约束下的序贯决策问题，通过域划分平衡探索与利用，并在CEC'2014测试集上验证了其数值效率与理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将函数优化问题视为预算约束下的序贯决策问题，该约束限制了优化过程中允许的目标函数评估次数。需要解决探索（域内准均匀搜索）与利用（潜在全局最大值附近的局部优化）之间的权衡。

Method: 提出同时乐观优化（SOO）算法，一种基于域划分的确定性算法，受连续型多臂赌博机问题启发，通过领域划分来平衡探索与利用。

Result: SOO算法在CEC'2014单目标实数参数数值优化竞赛测试套件上进行了实证评估，展示了其数值效率。

Conclusion: SOO算法的优势在于对返回解有理论保证以及数值效率高，是一种有效的机器学习优化方法。

Abstract: We consider function optimization as a sequential decision making problem under budget constraint. This constraint limits the number of objective function evaluations allowed during the optimization. We consider an algorithm inspired by a continuous version of a multi-armed bandit problem which attacks this optimization problem by solving the tradeoff between exploration (initial quasi-uniform search of the domain) and exploitation (local optimization around the potentially global maxima). We introduce the so-called Simultaneous Optimistic Optimization (SOO), a deterministic algorithm that works by domain partitioning. The benefit of such approach are the guarantees on the returned solution and the numerical efficiency of the algorithm. We present this machine learning approach to optimization, and provide the empirical assessment of SOO on the CEC'2014 competition on single objective real-parameter numerical optimization test-suite.

</details>


### [170] [GRIFDIR: Graph Resolution-Invariant FEM Diffusion Models in Function Spaces over Irregular Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03497)
*James Rowbottom,Elizabeth L. Baker,Nick Huang,Ben Adcock,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Alexander Denker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限元函数表示图卷积核的新型函数空间扩散模型架构，能够在非结构化网格和复杂几何域上实现分辨率不变的高保真函数采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的函数空间扩散模型在实践中未能充分发挥理论优势，如傅里叶神经算子等骨干架构偏向于规则网格，无法推广到复杂域拓扑结构。

Method: 将广义图卷积核表示为有限元函数，使模型能够自然处理非结构化网格和复杂几何结构。

Result: 在非凸和多连通域等多种几何形状上进行无条件与条件采样实验，方法保持了分辨率不变性，并在非平凡几何上实现了对函数分布的高保真捕捉。

Conclusion: 提出的架构有效弥合了函数空间扩散模型的理论优势与实践实现之间的差距，为复杂几何上的函数数据建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models in infinite-dimensional function spaces provide a mathematically principled framework for modelling function-valued data, offering key advantages such as resolution invariance and the ability to handle irregular discretisations. However, practical implementations have struggled to fully realise these benefits. Existing backbones like Fourier neural operators are often biased towards regular grids and fail to generalise to complex domain topologies. We propose a novel architecture for function-space diffusion models that represents generalised graph convolutional kernels as finite element functions, enabling the model to naturally handle unstructured meshes and complex geometries. We demonstrate the efficacy of our network architecture through a series of unconditional and conditional sampling experiments across diverse geometries, including non-convex and multiply-connected domains. Our results show that the proposed method maintains resolution invariance and achieves high fidelity in capturing functional distributions on non-trivial geometries.

</details>


### [171] [A Hierarchical Sampling Framework for bounding the Generalization Error of Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03499)
*Dario Filatrella,Ragnar Thobaben,Mikael Skoglund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分层联邦学习（HFL）框架，利用Wasserstein距离推导了期望泛化界。通过引入分层数据采样的树状结构模型及超样本构造方法，在损失函数满足Lipschitz假设下得到了泛化界，并证明该界在有限损失情形下严格优于现有CMI界，同时可与差分隐私结合恢复基于隐私的泛化界。在高斯位置模型下验证了该界的渐近紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习泛化界多基于互信息或均匀稳定性，难以处理HFL中层级化的数据依赖结构。作者旨在提出一种基于Wasserstein距离的通用框架，以刻画分层采样带来的数据依赖，并得到更紧的泛化界。

Method: 采用多层树状结构建模HFL中数据的分层采样过程；通过超样本构造（supersample construction）衡量算法对单个节点变化的敏感性；利用Wasserstein距离在Lipschitz损失假设下推导泛化界；与差分隐私结合推导基于隐私的泛化界；在高斯位置模型下分析渐近紧致性。

Result: 推导出基于Wasserstein距离的HFL泛化界，该界在有限损失情形下严格优于现有CMI界；与差分隐私结合可恢复已知的隐私泛化界；在高斯位置模型中恢复实际渐近泛化误差率。

Conclusion: 提出的Wasserstein距离方法为HFL提供了一种更紧且更通用的泛化分析框架，能够统一和改进现有基于CMI和差分隐私的泛化界，并在经典模型中验证了其紧致性。

Abstract: We study expected generalization bounds for the Hierarchical Federated Learning (HFL) setup using Wasserstein distance. We introduce a generalized framework in which data is sampled hierarchically, and we model it with a multi-layered tree structure that induces dependencies among the clients' datasets. We derive generalization bounds in terms of Wasserstein distance under the Lipschitz assumption on the loss function, by applying a supersample construction that allows us to measure the sensitivity of the algorithm to the change of a single node in the sampling tree. By leveraging the FL structure, we recover and strictly imply existing state-of-the-art conditional mutual information (CMI) bounds in the case of bounded losses. We also show that our bound can be applied together with Differential Privacy assumptions, to recover generalization bounds based on algorithmic privacy. To assess the tightness of our bounds, we study the Gaussian Location Model (GLM) and show that we recover the actual asymptotic rate of the generalization error.

</details>


### [172] [Understanding Self-Supervised Learning via Latent Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03517)
*Fabian A Mikulasch,Friedemann Zenke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将自监督学习统一为潜分布匹配（LDM）框架，通过对齐（最大化潜变量在假设模型下的对数概率）和均匀性（最大化潜熵以防止坍塌）来解释各类SSL方法，并基于此推导出非线性、无采样的贝叶斯滤波模型，证明预测性LDM在温和假设下可实现可识别潜表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习虽在复杂数据中提取通用的潜在表征方面表现优异，但缺乏统一的理论框架来解释现有方法并指导新方法的设计。

Method: 将SSL形式化为潜分布匹配（LDM）：通过对齐（最大化潜变量在假设模型下的对数概率）和均匀性（最大化潜熵）两个目标统一ICA、对比式、非对比式和预测式SSL方法；推导了基于卡尔曼预测器的非线性、无采样贝叶斯滤波模型用于高维时间序列。

Result: LDM统一了独立成分分析、对比式、非对比式和预测式SSL方法（包括stop-gradient方法）；证明预测性LDM在非线性预测器下仍能实现可识别的潜表示。

Conclusion: LDM框架阐明了现有SSL方法背后的假设，为开发新方法提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) excels at finding general-purpose latent representations from complex data, yet lacks a unifying theoretical framework that explains the diverse existing methods and guides the design of new ones. We cast SSL as latent distribution matching (LDM): learning representations that maximize their log-probability under an assumed latent model (alignment), while maximizing latent entropy to prevent collapse (uniformity). This view unifies independent component analysis with contrastive, non-contrastive, and predictive SSL methods, including stop gradient approaches. Leveraging LDM, we derive a nonlinear, sampling-free Bayesian filtering model with a Kalman-based predictor for high-dimensional timeseries. We further prove that predictive LDM yields identifiable latent representations under mild assumptions, even with nonlinear predictors. Overall, LDM clarifies the assumptions behind established SSL methods and provides principled guidance for developing new approaches.

</details>


### [173] [PerFlow: Physics-Embedded Rectified Flow for Efficient Reconstruction and Uncertainty Quantification of Spatiotemporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03548)
*Hao Zhou,Rui Zhang,Han Wan,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerFlow是一种基于整流流（Rectified Flow）的物理嵌入生成模型，通过解耦观测条件与物理约束，实现了对稀疏不规则测量数据的PDE场高效重建与不确定性量化，在保持物理一致性的同时，速度相比引导扩散基线提升高达320倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏和不规则测量中重建PDE控制的时空场具有病态性；现有确定性替代模型难以处理稀疏测量和不确定性量化，而生成式方法在采样时需同时处理数据一致性和PDE约束，导致推理缓慢且不稳定。

Method: PerFlow将观测条件与物理约束解耦：通过将观测数据直接馈入整流流动力学实现无引导条件化，同时通过约束保持投影（如不可压缩性或守恒律）嵌入硬物理约束。理论上建立了不变性保证，确保采样轨迹始终位于物理一致流形上。

Result: 在多种PDE系统上的实验表明，PerFlow在保持良好物理一致性的同时实现了竞争性的重建精度，仅需50步条件采样，推理速度相比2000步引导扩散基线提升高达320倍。

Conclusion: PerFlow通过解耦观测条件与物理约束，有效解决了生成式PDE重建中推理慢、不稳定的问题，在效率与物理一致性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Reconstructing PDE-governed fields from sparse and irregular measurements is challenging due to their ill-posed nature. Deterministic surrogates are trained on dense fields that struggle with limited measurements and uncertainty quantification. Generative models, by learning distributions over spatiotemporal fields, can better handle sparsity and uncertainty. However, existing generative approaches enforce data consistency and PDE constraints simultaneously via sampling-time gradient guidance, resulting in slow and unstable inference. To this end, we propose PerFlow, a Physics-embedded rectified Flow for efficient sparse reconstruction and uncertainty quantification of spatiotemporal dynamics. PerFlow decouples observation conditioning from physics enforcement, performing guidance-free conditioning by feeding observations into rectified-flow dynamics while embedding hard physics via a constraint-preserving projection (e.g., incompressibility or conservation). Theoretically, we establish invariance guarantees to ensure that trajectories remain on the physics-consistent manifold throughout sampling. Experiments on various PDE systems demonstrate competitive reconstruction accuracy with sound physics consistency, while enabling efficient conditional sampling (e.g., 50 steps) and up to 320 faster inference than 2000-step guided diffusion baselines.

</details>


### [174] [Disentangling Shared and Task-Specific Representations from Multi-Modal Clinical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03570)
*He Lyu,Huolin Zeng,Junren Wang,Huazhen Yang,Linchao He,Yong Chen,Zhirui Li,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer,Huan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于正交任务分解（OrthTD）的多任务框架，通过统一Transformer融合多模态临床数据，将患者表示分解为共享子空间和任务特定子空间，并施加几何正交约束以减少冗余。在12,430名手术患者数据集上对四个结局进行预测，平均AUC达87.5%，平均AUPRC达37.2%，显著优于现有方法，尤其在不平衡数据中的罕见事件识别上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实临床数据本质上是多模态的，需要联合评估多个相关结局。现有方法在平衡共享表示学习与任务特定建模之间存在不足：硬参数共享可能因任务梯度冲突导致负迁移，而灵活共享仍可能纠缠共享信号与任务特定信号。

Method: 基于统一的Transformer进行多模态融合，并提出正交任务分解（OrthTD），将患者表示分解为共享子空间和任务特定子空间，通过几何正交约束减少冗余并隔离任务特定信号。

Result: 在12,430名手术患者数据集上预测四个结局，平均AUC达87.5%，平均AUPRC达37.2%，一致优于先进的表格数据方法和多任务方法，尤其在不平衡数据中显著提升了AUPRC。

Conclusion: 强制非冗余的共享与任务特定表示能够提升多模态临床数据的多结局预测性能。

Abstract: Real-world clinical data is inherently multimodal, providing complementary evidence that mirrors the practical necessity of jointly assessing multiple related outcomes. Although multi-task learning can improve efficiency by sharing information across outcomes, existing approaches often fail to balance shared representation learning with outcome-specific modeling. Hard parameter sharing can trigger negative transfer when task gradients conflict, while flexible sharing may still entangle shared and task-specific signals. To address this, we propose a multi-task framework built on a unified Transformer for multimodal fusion, augmented with Orthogonal Task Decomposition (OrthTD) to split patient representations into shared and task-specific subspaces and impose a geometric orthogonality constraint to reduce redundancy and isolate task-specific signals. We evaluated OrthTD on a real-world cohort of 12,430 surgical patients for predicting four outcomes. OrthTD achieved average AUC (area under the receiver operating characteristic curve) of 87.5% and average AUPRC (area under the precision-recall curve) of 37.2%, consistently outperformed advanced tabular and multi-task methods. Notably, OrthTD achieves substantial gains in AUPRC, indicating superior performance in identifying rare events within imbalanced clinical data. These results suggest that enforcing non-redundant shared and task-specific representations can improve multi-outcome prediction from multimodal clinical data.

</details>


### [175] [Flow Matching on Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03588)
*Francesco Ruscelli,Ferdinando Zanchetta,Rita Fioresi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在李群对称空间上训练流匹配模型的通用框架，通过将对称空间上的流匹配问题线性化到李代数子空间，简化了测地线处理，并在实Grassmann流形上展示了应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法在处理黎曼对称空间（如球面、双曲空间、Grassmann流形）时面临几何结构复杂、测地线处理困难的问题，亟需一种能够利用对称空间代数结构的通用框架来简化建模过程。

Method: 利用对称空间的代数结构，将对称空间上的流匹配问题重新表述为其等距群李代数子空间上的流匹配问题，从而线性化问题并简化测地线处理。

Result: 在实Grassmann流形 SO(n)/SO(k)×SO(n-k) 上成功展示了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为对称空间上的生成建模提供了通用且高效的工具，有望推广到更广泛的流形类别。

Abstract: We introduce a general framework for training flow matching models on Riemannian symmetric spaces, a large class of manifolds that includes the sphere, hyperbolic space and Grassmannians. We exploit their algebraic structure to reformulate flow matching on symmetric spaces as flow matching on a subspace of the Lie algebra of their isometry group, thus linearizing the problem and greatly simplifying the handling of geodesics. As an application, we showcase our framework on the real Grassmannians $\operatorname{SO}(n) / \operatorname{SO}(k) \times \operatorname{SO}(n-k)$.

</details>


### [176] [A Few-Step Generative Model on Cumulative Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03623)
*Zhiqi Li,Duowen Chen,Yuchen Sun,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于累积流映射（cumulative flow maps）的统一少步生成框架，通过连接局部瞬时更新与有限时间传输，支持少步甚至单步生成，在保持合成质量的同时降低推理成本，适用于多种生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流模型通常需要大量迭代步骤才能完成从噪声到数据的传输，推理成本高。受物理传输中流映射技术的启发，作者希望建立一个统一的少步生成框架，在不增加模型容量的前提下减少推理步骤。

Method: 提出了累积流映射（cumulative-flow）抽象，将局部瞬时更新与有限时间传输连接起来，并基于累积传输和累积参数化构建统一框架。仅需对时间嵌入和训练目标进行最小修改，无需增加模型容量。

Result: 在图像生成、几何分布建模、联合预测、SDF生成等多种任务上验证了有效性，实现了少步甚至单步生成，同时保持合成质量，推理成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 累积流映射提供了一种统一、通用的视角来理解扩散和流模型，能够在不牺牲质量的前提下实现少步/单步生成，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We propose a unified, few-step generative modeling framework based on \emph{cumulative flow maps} for long-range transport in probability space, inspired by flow-map techniques for physical transport and dynamics. At its core is a cumulative-flow abstraction that connects local, instantaneous updates with finite-time transport, enabling generative models to reason about global state transitions. This perspective yields a unified few-step framework built on cumulative transport and \revise{cumulative} parameterization that applies broadly to existing diffusion- and flow-based models without being tied to a specific prediction \revise{instantiation}. Our formulation supports few-step and even one-step generation while preserving synthesis quality, requiring only minimal changes to time embeddings and training objectives, and no increase in model capacity. We demonstrate its effectiveness across diverse tasks, including image generation, geometric distribution modeling, joint prediction, and SDF generation, with reduced inference cost.

</details>


### [177] [Uni-OPD: Unifying On-Policy Distillation with a Dual-Perspective Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03677)
*Wenjin Hou,Shangpin Peng,Weinong Wang,Zheng Ruan,Yue Zhang,Zhenglin Zhou,Mingqi Gao,Yifei Chen,Kaiqi Wang,Hongming Yang,Chengquan Zhang,Zhuotao Tian,Han Hu,Yi Yang,Fei Wu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni-OPD，一个统一的在线策略蒸馏框架，通过从学生和教师双视角优化（学生探索平衡+教师顺序一致性校准），解决LLM和MLLM蒸馏中的探索不充分和教师监督不可靠问题，在5个领域16个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在整合专家模型能力方面取得经验成功，但对其何时能产生可靠改进的条件理解不足。本文识别出两个根本瓶颈：学生探索信息状态的不足以及学生rollout中教师监督的不可靠性。

Method: 提出Uni-OPD统一框架，采用双视角优化策略：(1) 学生视角：引入两种数据平衡策略促进训练中信息状态的探索；(2) 教师视角：发现可靠监督依赖于聚合token级指导与结果奖励的顺序一致性，开发了结果引导的边际校准机制以恢复正确与错误轨迹间的顺序一致性。

Result: 在5个领域、16个基准上进行了广泛实验，涵盖单教师和多教师蒸馏（LLM和MLLM）、强到弱蒸馏以及跨模态蒸馏，验证了Uni-OPD的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: Uni-OPD框架有效解决了OPD中的两大瓶颈，为可靠的在线策略蒸馏提供了实用见解。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for consolidating the capabilities of specialized expert models into a single student model. Despite its empirical success, the conditions under which OPD yields reliable improvement remain poorly understood. In this work, we identify two fundamental bottlenecks that limit effective OPD: insufficient exploration of informative states and unreliable teacher supervision for student rollouts. Building on this insight, we propose Uni-OPD, a unified OPD framework that generalizes across Large Language Models (LLMs) and Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), centered on a dual-perspective optimization strategy. Specifically, from the student's perspective, we adopt two data balancing strategies to promote exploration of informative student-generated states during training. From the teacher's perspective, we show that reliable supervision hinges on whether aggregated token-level guidance remains order-consistent with the outcome reward. To this end, we develop an outcome-guided margin calibration mechanism to restore order consistency between correct and incorrect trajectories. We conduct extensive experiments on 5 domains and 16 benchmarks covering diverse settings, including single-teacher and multi-teacher distillation across LLMs and MLLMs, strong-to-weak distillation, and cross-modal distillation. Our results verify the effectiveness and versatility of Uni-OPD and provide practical insights into reliable OPD.

</details>


### [178] [Rethinking the Rank Threshold for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03724)
*Juneyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LoRA微调在神经正切核（NTK）框架下的秩需求进行了理论分析。现有理论给出充分条件 $r(r+1)/2 > KN$（对典型小样本RoBERTa设置要求 $r \geq 12$），但该条件在特定场景的紧性及对实际使用的交叉熵损失的有效性尚不明确。本文给出三项结果，将二分类场景的所需秩降至 $r=1$：(1) 用非对称LoRA流形维数替代对称Sard计数，得到更弱容量条件 $r(m+n)-r^2 > C^*\cdot KN$（高斯iid特征下 $C^*\approx 1.35$），典型设置下 $r=1$ 即可满足；(2) 交叉熵损失下，Polyak--Łojasiewicz不等式完全消除秩阈值；(3) Rademacher复杂度界预测，当偏置项饱和时（二分类成立，多分类不成立），秩一方差最优。实验上，在四个GLUE二分类任务、三种编码器架构及RoBERTa-large规模下，秩一与现有 $r=12$ 相当；多分类MNLI上最优秩高于一，与预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA微调NTK理论给出充分条件 $r(r+1)/2 > KN$，对典型RoBERTa设置要求 $r \geq 12$，但该条件在特定场景（如二分类）的紧性未知，且仅对平方误差损失成立，而实际微调使用交叉熵损失。本文旨在理论上揭示二分类场景下秩一即可满足条件，弥合理论与实践的差距。

Method: (1) 用非对称LoRA流形维数替代对称Sard计数，推导更弱容量条件；(2) 引入Polyak--Łojasiewicz不等式分析交叉熵损失下的秩阈值消除；(3) 通过Rademacher复杂度界分析偏置项饱和对秩最优性的影响；(4) 在GLUE二分类任务、三种编码器架构及RoBERTa-large上进行实验验证。

Result: (1) 导出更弱容量条件 $r(m+n)-r^2 > C^*\cdot KN$，高斯iid特征下 $C^*\approx 1.35$，典型设置 $r=1$ 满足；(2) 交叉熵损失下PL不等式消除秩阈值；(3) Rademacher复杂度界预测偏置饱和时秩一方差最优（二分类成立，多分类不成立）；(4) 实验上二分类秩一与 $r=12$ 相当，多分类MNLI最优秩高于一。

Conclusion: 本文从理论角度证明，在NTK框架下二分类LoRA微调的秩需求可降至一，并揭示了交叉熵损失与平方损失在秩阈值上的本质区别、偏置饱和对秩最优性的关键影响。实验验证了理论预测。多分类扩展留待未来工作。

Abstract: A recent landscape analysis of LoRA fine-tuning in the neural tangent kernel regime establishes a sufficient condition $r(r+1)/2 > KN$ on the LoRA rank $r$ for the absence of spurious local minima under squared-error loss, prescribing $r \geq 12$ on canonical few-shot RoBERTa setups. The condition is stated for general output dimension $K$, so its sharpness in any particular regime, and its practical implication for the cross-entropy loss actually used in fine-tuning, are open. We give three results that together reduce the prescribed rank to $r = 1$ for binary classification in this regime. First, replacing the symmetric Sard-form count with the non-symmetric LoRA manifold dimension yields a strictly weaker capacity requirement, $r(m+n) - r^2 > C^* \cdot KN$ with $C^* \approx 1.35$ under Gaussian-iid features, satisfied at $r = 1$ on canonical setups. Second, in the cross-entropy setting the Polyak--Łojasiewicz inequality removes the rank threshold entirely. Third, a Rademacher-complexity bound predicts rank-one variance optimality precisely when the bias term is saturated, which is the case for binary classification but not for $K > 2$. Empirically, across four GLUE-style binary tasks, three encoder architectures, and at scale on RoBERTa-large, rank one is competitive with the existing prescription $r = 12$; on multi-class MNLI the optimal rank shifts above one, also as predicted. The binary-regime guarantees are conditional on standard NTK assumptions; the multi-class extension is left to future work.

</details>


### [179] [GEM-FI: Gated Evidential Mixtures with Fisher Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03750)
*Marco Mustafa Mohammed,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gated Evidential Mixtures (GEM)系列模型，通过门控机制和混合专家架构改进证据深度学习(EDL)，在单次推理中实现更好的校准性、不确定性估计和多模态认知不确定性表达。GEM-CORE利用能量门控抑制低支持度证据，GEM-MIX使用轻量混合头捕获多模态，GEM-FI通过Fisher正则化稳定分配。实验表明，GEM在分类精度、校准误差和OOD检测上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准证据深度学习存在过度自信、校准不良，且无法表达多模态认知不确定性。现有方法要么依赖多次前向传播（集成）来捕捉多模态，要么在单次推理中表达能力不足。需要一种既能实现单次推理又能捕获多模态不确定性的高效方法。

Method: 提出GEM系列模型：(1) GEM-CORE：学习特征级能量并映射为有界门控信号，在支持度低时平滑抑制证据输出；(2) GEM-MIX：添加轻量混合证据头并学习路由权重，通过单次推理捕获认知多模态；(3) GEM-FI：引入Fisher信息正则化项，稳定混合分配，防止头坍缩并产生更平滑的边界不确定性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，GEM-FI vs DAEDL：准确率91.11→93.75(+2.64pp)，Brier分数14.27→6.81(-7.46)，误检AUPR 99.08→99.94(+0.86)。OOD检测中，CIFAR-10→SVHN：AUPR/AUROC 92.59/95.09 vs DAEDL 85.54/89.30；CIFAR-10→CIFAR-100：90.20/89.06 vs 88.19/86.10。

Conclusion: GEM系列方法通过距离感知的门控机制和混合证据头，在单次推理中同时改善了校准性、分类精度和OOD检测能力。Fisher正则化有效缓解了混合模型中的头坍缩问题。实验证明该方法在多个基准上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) enables single-pass uncertainty estimation by predicting Dirichlet evidence, but it can remain overconfident and poorly calibrated, and it often fails to represent multi-modal epistemic uncertainty. We introduce Gated Evidential Mixtures (GEM), a family of models that learns an in-model energy signal and uses it to gate evidential outputs end-to-end in a distance-informed manner. GEM-CORE learns a feature-level energy and maps it to a bounded gate that smoothly suppresses evidence when support is low. To capture epistemic multi-modality without multi-pass ensembling, GEM-MIX adds a lightweight mixture of evidential heads with learned routing weights while preserving single-pass inference. Finally, GEM-FI stabilizes mixture allocations via a Fisher-informed regularizer, reducing head collapse and producing smoother boundary uncertainty. Across image classification and OOD detection benchmarks, GEM improves calibration and ID/OOD separation with single-pass inference. On CIFAR-10, GEM-FI vs. DAEDL improves accuracy from 91.11 to 93.75 (+2.64 pp), reduces Brier x100 from 14.27 to 6.81 (-7.46), and also improves misclassification-detection AUPR from 99.08 to 99.94 (+0.86). For epistemic OOD detection, GEM-FI achieves AUPR/AUROC of 92.59/95.09 on CIFAR-10 to SVHN and 90.20/89.06 on CIFAR-10 to CIFAR-100, compared with 85.54/89.30 and 88.19/86.10 for DAEDL.

</details>


### [180] [Vanishing L2 regularization for the softmax Multi Armed Bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03752)
*Stefana-Lucia Anita,Gabriel Turinici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了L2正则化softmax策略梯度方法，证明了当正则化参数趋近于零时的收敛性，并验证了该设置在实际基准测试中的数值优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究利用凸性分析L2正则化softmax策略梯度时，未能建立正则化参数消失情况下的收敛理论框架，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。
